# 存在与状态

> 本章收跨领域的「存在、存续、处于某种状态」框架词：系动词与存在动词、状态名物、维持/终止、可用与就绪、姿态与占用等。**不**收人际行为义 stay/remain/settle（行为与动作或旅行章）；**不**收 alive/dead/life 生死轴心词（人生阶段章）；**不**收 nation state 政体义 state（政治与政府章）；**不**收 seem/appear 感知判断（感知与感觉章）；**不**收 become/turn 变化过程（变化与转变章）。

**be**
英/美 /biː; bi; bɪ; biz/
v. 是；在；存在；发生
[义] To exist; to have a particular quality, identity, or location; used as a linking verb or auxiliary. 英语最核心动词：表存在（There is a problem 有问题）、身份与属性（She is a doctor 她是医生）、位置（The keys are on the desk 钥匙在桌上）、进行（She is reading 她在读）。作助动词构成进行时与被动语态。口语缩略 I'm, you're, it's, won't 等极频。
[形] am / is / are；was / were；been / being；第三人称单数 is
[例] ① There must be a simpler way to explain this. 一定有更简单的解释方法。 ② The meeting will be at three o'clock in the main hall. 会议下午三点在大厅举行。
[搭] there is/are 有, be able to 能够, be going to 将要, be about to 即将, be worth doing 值得做, be supposed to 应该
[联] exist 存在, become 变成, seem 似乎, remain 保持, stay 停留, occur 发生, live 生活/存活, happen 发生
[记] be 不可用于完成时表「是」（× I have been a teacher for ten years 用 have been 表持续身份时正确；× have been happy 表状态持续亦常见）。there be 表「有/存在」。be vs. become：be 表当前状态，become 表转变

**exist**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzɪst/
v. 存在；生存
[义] To be real; to live, especially in a difficult or minimal way. 某物在现实中「有」（Do ghosts exist? 鬼存在吗？）；亦指在困境中勉强维持（exist on very little money 靠极少钱过活）。比 be 更强调「实有、存续」，常用于讨论、哲学或否定（cease to exist 不再存在）。
[形] existed / existed / existing；第三人称单数 exists
[例] ① No evidence exists that the product is harmful. 没有证据表明该产品有害。 ② The family existed on rice and vegetables for months. 这家人靠米饭和蔬菜撑了几个月。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *existere*（站出来），*ex-*（出）+ *sistere*（站立）→ 站出来 → 存在。
[族] existence 存在, existing 现存的, coexist 共存
[搭] exist in 存在于, exist on 靠……过活, continue to exist 继续存在, cease to exist 不再存在, still exist 仍然存在
[联] be 是/在, live 活着, survive 幸存, occur 发生, remain 留存, vanish 消失, real 真实的, actual 实际的
[记] exist 不可用于进行时表示「正在存在」（× is existing）。exist on + 名词表「靠……维生」。exist vs. live：exist 偏「有没有」或「勉强过活」；live 偏正常生活

**existence**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzɪstəns/
n. 存在；生存；生活
[义] The state of being real or alive; a way of living. 存在这一状态（come into existence 诞生, in existence 现存的）；亦指生活方式，常带艰难色彩（a miserable existence 悲惨的生活）。不可数为主；可数指某种生存方式（lead a quiet existence）。
[例] ① The company came into existence in 1998. 这家公司成立于 1998 年。 ② He led a quiet existence in a small coastal town. 他在一座海滨小城过着安静的生活。
[源] 源自 exist + -ence（名词后缀）；拉丁语 *existentia*。
[族] exist 存在, existing 现存的, existential 存在主义的
[搭] come into existence 诞生, go out of existence 消亡, in existence 现存的, human existence 人类存在, lead an existence 过某种生活
[联] life 生命, survival 生存, being 存在, reality 现实, presence 在场, absence 缺席, creation 创造, death 死亡
[记] 不可数：human existence。可数：a miserable existence。existence vs. life：existence 更抽象或偏「有没有」；life 更日常、可指人生质量

**survive**
英/美 /səˈvaɪv/
v. 幸存；活下来；比……活得久；在困境中继续存在
[义] To continue to live or exist after a dangerous event; to remain alive after someone else has died; to continue despite difficulties. 经历危险后仍活着（survive the crash 空难幸存）；比某人活得久（She survived her husband by ten years）；机构、传统等在困境中续存（Only two shops survived the recession）。比 live 更绑「险境、打击后仍在」。
[形] survived / survived / surviving；第三人称单数 survives
[例] ① Only a few passengers survived the plane crash. 空难中只有少数乘客幸存。 ② Small family farms are struggling to survive in a changing market. 小型家庭农场在变化的市场中艰难维持。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *supervivere*，*super-*（超过）+ *vivere*（活）→ 活过 → 幸存。
[族] survival 生存, survivor 幸存者, surviving 幸存的
[搭] survive an accident/attack 经历事故/袭击后幸存, survive on 靠……过活, survive the winter 熬过冬天, barely survive 勉强活下来, survive as 以……形式续存
[联] live 活着, die 死亡, perish 丧生, endure 经受, escape 逃脱, rescue 救援, remain 留存, thrive 繁荣, collapse 崩溃, crisis 危机
[记] survive 可接人作宾语表「比某人活得久」（outlive 同义更书面）。survive on 与 exist on 近义。surviving 作形容词：surviving members 仍在世的成员

**survival**
英/美 /səˈvaɪvl/
n. 生存；幸存；残存物
[义] The state of continuing to live or exist; something that has continued from an earlier time. 继续活着或存在（fight for survival 为生存而战）；亦指遗留物（a survival from the Middle Ages 中世纪遗存）。不可数为主；可数指幸存实例或遗存物。
[例] ① Clean water is essential for survival in the desert. 在沙漠中，清洁的水是生存所必需的。 ② The ritual is a survival from an earlier agricultural society. 这一仪式是早期农业社会的遗存。
[搭] fight/struggle for survival 为生存而战, survival rate 存活率, survival skills 生存技能, survival of the fittest 适者生存, a survival from ……的遗存
[联] survive 幸存, existence 存在, life 生命, death 死亡, instinct 本能, emergency 紧急情况, remnant 残余, extinction 灭绝
[记] 不可数：struggle for survival。可数：a survival from the past。survival vs. existence：survival 强调险境后仍在；existence 更中性

**subsist**
英/美 /səbˈsɪst/
v. 维持生存；靠……过活
[义] To maintain oneself at a minimal level; to live on a very limited supply. 靠极少资源维生（subsist on bread and water 靠面包和水过活）；正式语体中亦指「存在」（the right subsists 权利仍然存在）。比 live/exist 更书面、更绑「最低限度维持」。
[形] subsisted / subsisted / subsisting
[例] ① The refugees subsisted on emergency rations for weeks. 难民靠紧急配给撑了几周。 ② The claim still subsists in law even if it is rarely enforced. 该诉求在法律上仍然存在，尽管很少执行。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *subsistere*（站立、维持），*sub-*（在下）+ *sistere*（站立）→ 维持不动 → 存续。
[族] subsistence 生计, subsistent 存在的
[搭] subsist on 靠……过活, subsist by 以……维生, still subsist 仍然存在
[联] exist 存在, survive 幸存, live 生活, sustain 维持, maintain 维持, barely 勉强, ration 配给, minimal 最低的
[记] subsist on 极书面；口语多用 live on / survive on。subsistence living 指勉强糊口的生计

**thrive**
英/美 /θraɪv/
v. 茁壮成长；繁荣；兴旺
[义] To grow or develop well; to be successful and healthy. 生物生长旺盛（Children thrive in a stable home 孩子在稳定家庭茁壮成长）；机构、业务兴旺（The business is thriving 生意兴隆）。与 survive 相对：survive 是「活下来」，thrive 是「活得好、发展好」。
[形] thrived / thrived 或 throve / thrived / thriving
[例] ① These plants thrive in direct sunlight and well-drained soil. 这些植物在直射阳光和排水良好的土壤里长得很好。 ② Local craft shops are thriving thanks to tourism. 得益于旅游，本地手工艺店生意兴隆。
[源] 古北欧语 *thrīfask*（ grasp for oneself）→ 抓牢生机 → 兴旺。
[族] thriving 兴旺的, thriver 兴旺者（罕）
[搭] thrive on 靠……而兴旺, thrive in 在……中茁壮成长, thriving business 兴旺的生意, children thrive 孩子茁壮成长
[联] survive 幸存, flourish 繁荣, prosper 兴旺, grow 生长, decline 衰退, wither 枯萎, succeed 成功, healthy 健康的, boom 繁荣
[记] thrive on 表「靠某条件反而更起劲」（She thrives on pressure 她越压越勇）。不规则过去式英式 throve 美式 thrived 均可

**perish**
英/美 /ˈperɪʃ/
v. 丧生；毁灭；腐烂
[义] To die, especially in a sudden or violent way; to be destroyed; to decay (food). 突然或惨死（hundreds perished in the fire 数百人葬身火海）；事物毁灭（The manuscript perished in the flood）；食物腐坏（Bread perishes quickly in heat）。比 die 正式、文学，常见于新闻与历史叙述。
[形] perished / perished / perishing
[例] ① More than fifty people perished when the building collapsed. 楼房倒塌时五十多人丧生。 ② Without refrigeration, the fruit will perish within days. 没有冷藏，水果几天内就会坏掉。
[源] 古法语 *perir*，拉丁语 *perire*（走完、毁灭），*per-*（完全）+ *ire*（走）→ 走完了 → 灭亡。
[族] perishable 易腐的, imperishable 不朽的
[搭] perish in a fire/crash 在火灾/事故中丧生, perish from hunger 饿死, perish the thought 别这么想（口语）, perishable goods 易腐货物
[联] die 死亡, survive 幸存, destroy 毁灭, decay 腐烂, expire 到期/死亡, kill 杀死, tragedy 悲剧, fatal 致命的
[记] perish 新闻与正式语体常见，日常口语多用 die。perish the thought 表强烈否定某念头。perishable vs. imperishable：易腐 vs. 不朽

**cease**
英/美 /siːs/
v. 停止；终止
n. 停止（罕）
[义] To stop doing something or stop existing; to bring to an end. 活动或状态停止（cease fire 停火, cease operations 停止运营）；不再存在（The tradition ceased centuries ago）。比 stop 正式，多见于书面、公告、法律（cease and desist 停止并禁止）。
[形] ceased / ceased / ceasing
[例] ① The factory ceased production last year. 该厂去年停产。 ② Hostilities ceased at midnight under the new agreement. 根据新协议，敌对行动于午夜停止。
[源] 古法语 *cesser*，拉丁语 *cessare*（停住），*cedere*（走）→ 不再继续 → 停止。
[族] ceaseless 不停的, cessation 停止
[搭] cease fire 停火, cease to exist 不再存在, cease operations 停止运营, cease and desist 停止并禁止（法律）, without cease 不停地
[联] stop 停止, end 结束, halt 停止, terminate 终止, continue 继续, persist 持续, exist 存在, disappear 消失
[记] cease to do / cease doing 均可表停止做某事。cease 不可用于进行式表「正在停」（× is ceasing to exist 虽语法可接受但少见）。名词 cessation 更正式

**vanish**
英/美 /ˈvænɪʃ/
v. 消失；不见
[义] To disappear suddenly and completely. 突然、完全地不见（The child vanished in the crowd 孩子在人群中消失了）；亦可指感觉、希望等骤然消散（All doubts vanished 一切疑虑烟消云散）。比 disappear 更带「骤然、神秘、彻底」色彩，文学与口语均常见。
[形] vanished / vanished / vanishing
[例] ① The magician made the coin vanish into thin air. 魔术师让硬币消失在空气中。 ② Public support for the plan vanished overnight. 公众对该计划的支持一夜之间烟消云散。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *evanescere*（消失），*e-*（出）+ *vanus*（空）→ 变空 → 消失。
[族] vanishing 正在消失的, vanity 虚荣（同根 *vanus* 空）
[搭] vanish into thin air 消失得无影无踪, vanish without trace 消失得无迹可寻, vanish from sight 从视野中消失, vanishing point 消失点
[联] disappear 消失, fade 消退, evaporate 蒸发, appear 出现, emerge 浮现, exist 存在, remain 留存, mystery 神秘, trace 痕迹
[记] vanish 强调突然与彻底；disappear 更中性、通用。vanishing 作定语：a vanishing tradition 正在消失的传统

**disappear**
英/美 /ˌdɪsəˈpɪə(r)/
v. 消失；不见
[义] To become impossible to see or find; to stop existing. 从视野或认知中不见（The sun disappeared behind the clouds）；不再存在（Dinosaurs disappeared millions of years ago）。最通用的「消失」动词，语域中性，口语与书面均常用。
[形] disappeared / disappeared / disappearing
[例] ① My keys have disappeared again—have you seen them? 我的钥匙又不见了——你看见了吗？ ② Many traditional crafts are disappearing as younger generations move to cities. 随着年轻人进城，许多传统手艺正在消失。
[源] dis-（不）+ appear（出现）→ 不再出现 → 消失。
[族] disappearance 消失, reappear 再出现
[搭] disappear from 从……消失, disappear without a trace 消失得无影无踪, make sb. disappear 使某人消失（可含负面含义）, disappearing act 躲起来（口语）
[联] vanish 骤然消失, fade 消退, appear 出现, exist 存在, missing 失踪的, gone 不见了, lost 丢失的, emerge 浮现, remain 留存
[记] disappear 为及物性弱的不及物动词；不说 × disappear the keys，用 The keys disappeared。disappear vs. vanish：前者中性，后者更骤然

**prevail**
英/美 /prɪˈveɪl/
v. 盛行；获胜；说服
[义] To be common or widespread; to win or prove stronger; to persuade (prevail upon). 某观点、风俗、状况占上风（Justice will prevail 正义终将胜利, A sense of calm prevailed 局面恢复平静）；说服某人（prevail upon sb. to do）。强调「在竞争或变化后仍然站住、成为主导状态」。
[形] prevailed / prevailed / prevailing
[例] ① Common sense prevailed, and the dangerous plan was abandoned. 理智占了上风，危险方案被放弃。 ② A culture of long working hours still prevails in some industries. 在一些行业，长时间工作的文化仍然盛行。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *praevalere*，*prae-*（前）+ *valere*（强）→ 更强 → 占上风。
[族] prevailing 现行的, prevalence 流行程度
[搭] prevail over 战胜, prevail against 抵御, prevail upon/on sb. 说服某人, justice prevails 正义得胜, prevailing wind 盛行风向
[联] dominate 占主导, win 赢, survive 幸存, exist 存在, disappear 消失, common 常见的, widespread 广泛的, persuade 说服, defeat 击败
[记] prevail 不及物；prevail upon sb. to do 表说服。prevailing 作形容词：prevailing attitude 普遍态度

**condition**
英/美 /kənˈdɪʃn/
n. 状况；条件；疾病
v. 使适应；训练
[义] The state that something is in; a requirement; a medical problem. 事物所处的状态（in good condition 状况良好, working condition 可用状态）；前提条件（on condition that 条件是）；疾病（a heart condition 心脏病）。动词 condition 指使适应（condition the air 调节空气）或训练（conditioned response 条件反射）。
[形] 复数 conditions；动词 conditioned / conditioned / conditioning
[例] ① The house is old but in excellent condition. 房子虽旧，但状况极好。 ② She agreed to help on condition that her name was not mentioned. 她同意帮忙，条件是不提她的名字。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *condicio*（ agreement, state），*con-* + *dicere*（说）→ 约定条款 → 条件/状况。
[族] conditional 有条件的, conditioner 护发素/调节器, air-conditioned 空调的
[搭] in good/poor condition 状况好/差, on condition that 条件是, working condition 可用状态, heart condition 心脏病, weather conditions 天气状况
[联] state 状态, situation 局面, status 状况, circumstance 情况, requirement 要求, health 健康, shape 状况, quality 质量
[记] 可数/不可数：in good condition 不可数；conditions 复数可指「条件、环境」（living conditions 生活条件）。condition vs. situation：condition 偏内在状态；situation 偏整体局面

**situation**
英/美 /ˌsɪtʃuˈeɪʃn/
n. 情况；局面；处境
[义] A set of circumstances at a particular time; a job or position (formal). 某时某地的整体局面（a difficult situation 困境, the economic situation 经济形势）；正式语体中指职位（fill a situation 填补职位，旧式）。比 condition 更宏观，常指需应对的「形势」。
[例] ① The government is monitoring the situation closely after the protests. 抗议发生后，政府正密切监控局势。 ② We need to assess the situation before making a decision. 我们需要先评估形势再作决定。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *situatio*，*situs*（位置）→ 所处的位置 → 局面。
[族] situate 使位于, situational 情境的
[搭] current situation 当前形势, difficult situation 困境, economic/political situation 经济/政治局势, handle the situation 处理局面, sticky situation 棘手局面
[联] condition 状况, circumstance 情况, context 语境, crisis 危机, problem 问题, status 状况, state 状态, position 位置
[记] 可数：a difficult situation。the situation 常指「当前局势」。situation vs. condition：situation 偏「局面、形势」；condition 偏「状态、条件」

**circumstance**
英/美 /ˈsɜːkəmstəns/ 美 /ˈsɜːrkəmstæns/
n. 情况；环境；境况
[义] A fact or condition connected with an event or action; financial situation (plural). 与某事相关的具体情况（under the circumstances 在这种情况下）；复数 circumstances 指境况、环境（in reduced circumstances 境况拮据）或「细节」（never mention the circumstances 别提细节）。
[例] ① Under the circumstances, canceling the trip was the right decision. 在这种情况下，取消行程是正确的决定。 ② They had fallen on hard circumstances after the business failed. 生意失败后，他们陷入了困境。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *circumstantia*，*circum-*（周围）+ *stare*（站立）→ 站在周围的东西 → 环境情况。
[族] circumstantial 间接的
[搭] under the circumstances 在这种情况下, in/under no circumstances 绝不, due to circumstances beyond our control 因不可抗力, financial circumstances 经济状况
[联] situation 局面, condition 状况, context 语境, factor 因素, environment 环境, detail 细节, event 事件, case 事例
[记] 可数，常用复数 circumstances。in no circumstances 与 under no circumstances 表「绝不」。circumstance vs. situation：circumstance 更偏「具体条件、细节因素」

**instance**
英/美 /ˈɪnstəns/
n. 例子；情况；实例
[义] A particular example or case of something; a moment (archaic). 某类事物的一个具体例子（for instance 例如, in this instance 在这种情况下）；古语可指「瞬间」。可数。与 example 近义，instance 更偏「某一具体发生的一次/一个案例」。
[例] ① There have been several instances of data theft this year. 今年已发生数起数据盗窃案例。 ② For instance, you could try contacting the supplier directly. 例如，你可以试着直接联系供应商。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *instantia*（ presence, example），*in-* + *stare*（站立）→ 站在近处 → 实例。
[族] instant 瞬间, instantiate  instantiate（术语）
[搭] for instance 例如, in this instance 在这种情况下, a rare instance 罕见案例, instance of ……的实例, in the first instance 首先
[联] example 例子, case 案例, situation 局面, occurrence 发生, sample 样本, evidence 证据, pattern 模式
[记] for instance 与 for example 可互换，instance 略正式。in the first instance 表「首先、第一步」。instance vs. example：instance 强调「某一具体发生件」

**predicament**
英/美 /prɪˈdɪkəmənt/
n. 困境；尴尬处境
[义] A difficult, unpleasant, or embarrassing situation. 难以脱身的困境或尴尬局面（financial predicament 财务困境, leave sb. in a predicament 让某人陷入窘境）。比 problem 更强调「左右为难、不知如何是好」的处境感。
[例] ① The team found itself in a predicament when two key players were injured. 两名主力受伤，球队陷入困境。 ② I am in a bit of a predicament—I promised to be in two places at once. 我有点为难——我答应同时出现在两个地方。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *praedicamentum*（ category），后引申为「被归类到的（困境）状态」。
[搭] in a predicament 处于困境, financial predicament 财务困境, awkward predicament 尴尬处境, get out of a predicament 摆脱困境
[联] dilemma 两难, plight 困境, trouble 麻烦, situation 局面, crisis 危机, embarrassment 尴尬, bind 束缚, fix 僵局
[记] 可数：a predicament。predicament vs. dilemma：predicament 泛指困境；dilemma 强调「两难选择」

**plight**
英/美 /plaɪt/
n. 困境；苦境
[义] A very difficult or sad situation. 艰难或悲惨的处境（the plight of refugees 难民的困境, a sorry plight 可悲的境况）。偏书面、新闻、人道叙述，常带同情色彩。可数。
[例] ① The documentary highlights the plight of children living in war zones. 纪录片聚焦战区儿童的困境。 ② Farmers spoke about their plight after months without rain. 农民们讲述了数月无雨后的苦境。
[源] 源自古法语 *plit*（ fold, condition），与 fold 折叠同源 → 被折进的状态 → 困境。
[搭] the plight of ……的困境, desperate plight 绝境, sorry plight 可悲境况, highlight the plight 凸显困境
[联] predicament 困境, crisis 危机, hardship 艰难, suffering 痛苦, refugee 难民, poverty 贫困, struggle 挣扎, rescue  rescue
[记] 可数，常 the plight of + 群体。plight vs. predicament：plight 更悲苦、常指群体；predicament 可较轻、较个人

**keep**
英/美 /kiːp/
v. 保持；保留；继续；饲养
[义] To remain in a particular state; to continue doing something; to retain possession. 保持某种状态（keep calm 保持冷静, keep silent 保持沉默）；继续做（keep working 继续工作）；保留不丢（keep the receipt 留收据）。跨领域极高频，本章取「维持状态、使持续」义；具体行为义见行为章。
[形] kept / kept / keeping
[例] ① Keep the door closed until I get back. 我回来前把门关上。 ② She kept working even when everyone else had stopped. 其他人都停了，她仍在继续工作。
[搭] keep calm/silent/quiet 保持冷静/沉默/安静, keep doing sth. 继续做, keep sb. from doing 阻止, keep in touch 保持联系, keep track of 记录
[联] hold 保持, retain 保留, maintain 维持, stay 保持, remain 保持, continue 继续, preserve  preserve, lose 失去
[记] keep + 形容词表状态：keep warm 保暖。keep on doing 强调持续。keep vs. hold：keep 更宽；hold 更绑「抓住不放、维持某水平」

**hold**
英/美 /həʊld/
v. 抓住；保持；容纳；举行
[义] To keep something in a particular position or state; to contain; to organize an event. 使保持某状态（hold steady 保持稳定, hold one's breath 屏息）；容纳（The tank holds 500 litres）；举行（hold a meeting 开会）。本章侧重「保持、维持」义；「手持、抓住」与行为章有交叉但以通用义收录于此。
[形] held / held / holding
[例] ① Prices have held steady despite inflation. 尽管通胀，价格保持稳定。 ② The auditorium holds about eight hundred people. 礼堂约容纳八百人。
[搭] hold steady 保持稳定, hold one's breath 屏息, hold a meeting 开会, hold the line  hold the line, on hold 保持
[联] keep 保持, retain 保留, maintain 维持, contain  contain, grasp  grasp, release  release, drop  drop, stable  stable
[记] hold + 形容词：hold still 别动。hold 可作名词：on hold 等候中。hold vs. keep：hold 更绑「稳住、定住」；keep 更宽

**retain**
英/美 /rɪˈteɪn/
v. 保留；保持；记住
[义] To keep something and not lose it; to maintain a quality; to remember information. 保留不失去（retain control 保持控制, retain employees 留住员工）；保持特性（retain moisture  retain moisture）；记住（formal）。比 keep 正式，多见于书面、商业、法律。
[形] retained / retained / retaining
[例] ① The company managed to retain most of its skilled staff. 公司设法留住了大部分熟练员工。 ② This soil retains water better than sandy soil. 这种土比沙土更保水。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *retinere*，*re-*（回）+ *tenere*（持有）→ 继续持有 → 保留。
[族] retention  retention, retainer  retainer
[搭] retain control 保持控制, retain employees 留住员工, retain information 记住信息, retain moisture  retain moisture, retain a lawyer 聘请律师
[联] keep 保持, hold 保持, maintain 维持, preserve  preserve, lose 失去, release  release, remember  remember, employ  employ
[记] retain 正式；口语保留东西多用 keep。retention 为名词：customer retention 客户留存

**idle**
英/美 /ˈaɪdl/
adj. 闲置的；空闲的；懒散的
v.  idle
[义] Not being used or employed; lazy; to spend time doing nothing. 机器、资源闲置（idle machinery 闲置设备）；人空闲（sit idle 闲坐着）；懒散（an idle student 懒散的学生）。动词 idle 指空转、虚度（The engine idled  engine idled）。
[形] 比较级 idler / more idle；最高级 idlest / most idle；动词 idled / idled / idling
[例] ① Half the factory machines stood idle during the strike. 罢工期间，半数工厂机器闲置。 ② He was accused of letting valuable time go idle. 他被指虚度宝贵时间。
[搭] sit idle 闲坐, idle machinery 闲置设备, idle away time 虚度时光, idle gossip 闲言碎语, stand idle 闲置
[联] inactive  inactive, dormant  dormant, busy  busy, lazy  lazy, vacant  vacant, unemployed  unemployed, active  active, engaged  engaged
[记] idle 可含贬义「懒散」；inactive 更中性表「不活跃」。idle vs. lazy：idle 可指「未被使用」；lazy 专指人的懒惰

**dormant**
英/美 /ˈdɔːmənt/
adj. 休眠的；潜伏的；暂停活动的
[义] Temporarily inactive but able to become active again. 暂时不活动、但可再激活（a dormant volcano 休眠火山, dormant account  dormant account）；潜伏未发作（a dormant infection  latent infection）。偏正式、书面，强调「假死、待机」而非永久停止。
[例] ① The virus can remain dormant in the body for years. 病毒可在体内潜伏多年。 ② Funds in the account had lain dormant for over a decade. 账户里的资金已闲置十多年。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *dormire*（ sleep）→ 睡着 → 休眠。
[族] dormitory  dormitory, dormancy  dormancy
[搭] dormant volcano 休眠火山, dormant account  dormant account, lie dormant 处于休眠, remain dormant 保持休眠
[联] inactive  inactive, idle  idle, latent  latent, asleep  asleep, active  active, awake  awake, hibernate  hibernate, suspend  suspend
[记] lie dormant / remain dormant 为固定搭配。dormant vs. inactive：dormant 强调「可再激活的休眠」

**inactive**
英/美 /ɪnˈæktɪv/
adj. 不活跃的；闲置的； inactive
[义] Not doing anything or not in use; not participating. 不活动、不使用（inactive users  inactive users）；未参与（politically inactive  politically inactive）。中性、正式，无 dormant 的「待机再启」意味，亦无 idle 的「懒散」贬义。
[例] ① Accounts that remain inactive for two years may be closed. 账户若两年无活动可能被销户。 ② He has been politically inactive since leaving office. 离任后他不再参与政治活动。
[源] in-（不）+ active（活跃的）→ 不活跃。
[族] inactivity  inactivity, activate  activate
[搭] inactive account  inactive account, remain inactive 保持 inactive, politically inactive 不参与政治, inactive lifestyle  inactive lifestyle
[联] active  active, idle  idle, dormant  dormant, busy  busy, engaged  engaged, lazy  lazy, participate  participate
[记] inactive 中性；idle 可贬；dormant 强调休眠。反义 active 在性格章亦有收录，此处取「活动/不活动」状态义

**busy**
英/美 /ˈbɪzi/
adj. 忙碌的；繁忙的；占线的
[义] Having a lot to do; full of activity; (of a phone line) engaged. 人忙（I'm busy right now 我现在忙）；地方、时段繁忙（a busy street  busy street）；电话占线（The line is busy）。与 free/available 构成可用状态对立。
[形] 比较级 busier；最高级 busiest
[例] ① She was too busy to return my call until the next day. 她太忙，直到第二天才回我电话。 ② The restaurant gets very busy on Friday nights. 这家餐厅周五晚上非常忙。
[搭] busy with 忙于, busy doing 忙着做, busy schedule  busy schedule, busy signal 占线音, get busy 忙起来
[联] free  free, available  available, idle  idle, engaged  engaged, occupied  occupied, hectic  hectic, quiet  quiet
[记] busy 不可说 × busy to do，用 busy doing / busy with。busy vs. engaged：busy 泛指忙；engaged 可指「电话占线、有约」

**free**
英/美 /friː/
adj. 空闲的；免费的；自由的
v. 释放
[义] Not busy; not occupied; costing nothing; not restricted. 有空（Are you free tonight? 今晚有空吗？）；免费（free admission  free admission）；自由（free choice  free choice）。本章取「空闲、未被占用」义；「免费、自由」义亦常用。
[形] 比较级 freer；最高级 freest；动词 freed / freed / freeing
[例] ① Call me when you're free—I need to discuss the contract. 你有空给我打电话——我得商量合同。 ② Is this seat free, or is someone sitting here? 这个座位有人吗，还是空的？
[搭] free time 空闲时间, feel free to  feel free to, free of charge 免费, set sb. free 释放, free from  free from
[联] busy  busy, available  available, occupied  occupied, idle  idle, spare  spare, vacant  vacant, engaged  engaged
[记] free vs. available：free 偏「有空」；available 偏「可得到、可联系到」。feel free to do 表「尽管……」

**available**
英/美 /əˈveɪləbl/
adj. 可获得的；有空的；可联系到
[义] Able to be used or obtained; free to do something. 可得到、可使用（Tickets are still available 票仍有；resources available 可用资源）；人有空或能联系到（The manager is not available right now 经理现在没空）。正式与商务极频。
[例] ① Detailed information is available on the company website. 详细信息见公司网站。 ② Several candidates are available for interview next week. 下周有几位候选人可安排面试。
[源] 源自 avail（有用）+ -able → 可动用的 → 可用的。
[族] availability  availability, unavailable  unavailable
[搭] readily available  readily available, available for 可供, make oneself available  make oneself available, not available  unavailable
[联] free  free, busy  busy, accessible  accessible, obtain  obtain, obtain  obtain, spare  spare, occupied  occupied, engage  engage
[记] available 不可用于 × available to do，用 available for interview / available to help。availability 名词极常用

**engaged**
英/美 /ɪnˈɡeɪdʒd/
adj. 忙碌的；已占用的；已订婚的
[义] Busy; already in use (phone line, toilet); having agreed to marry. 人忙或有约（I'm engaged this afternoon 下午有事）；资源被占用（The line is engaged 电话占线）；订婚（get engaged 订婚）。本章取「忙碌、占用」状态义。
[例] ① She can't talk now—she's engaged in a meeting. 她现在不能谈——她在开会。 ② I tried calling but the number was engaged. 我试着打过去，但电话占线。
[源] 源自 engage（使忙碌、订婚）+ -ed。
[族] engage  engage, engagement  engagement, disengaged  disengaged
[搭] engaged in 忙于, engaged to sb. 与某人订婚, engaged tone 占线音, get engaged 订婚, fully engaged  fully engaged
[联] busy  busy, occupied  occupied, free  free, available  available, involved  involved, single  single, married  married
[记] 英式 engaged 表电话占线；美式多用 busy。be engaged in 表「忙于某事」。engaged vs. busy：engaged 更正式，且有「占用、订婚」义

**ongoing**
英/美 /ˈɒnɡəʊɪŋ/ 美 /ˈɑːnɡoʊɪŋ/
adj. 进行中的；持续的
[义] Continuing to exist or develop; still in progress. 仍在进行、尚未结束（an ongoing investigation  ongoing investigation, ongoing support  ongoing support）。不可作谓语形容词位置灵活；书面与新闻常见。
[例] ① Police said the investigation is ongoing and no arrests have been made. 警方称调查仍在进行，尚未逮捕任何人。 ② The project requires ongoing maintenance after launch. 项目上线后需要持续维护。
[搭] ongoing investigation  ongoing investigation, ongoing process  ongoing process, ongoing debate  ongoing debate, ongoing support  ongoing support, ongoing issue  ongoing issue
[联] continuing  continuing, current  current, pending  pending, finished  finished, complete  complete, temporary  temporary, permanent  permanent
[记] ongoing 只作形容词，不说 × the investigation is ongoinging。ongoing vs. current：ongoing 强调「还在进行、没完」

**pending**
英/美 /ˈpendɪŋ/
adj. 待定的；待决的； prep. 在等待……期间
[义] Waiting to be decided or settled; until (formal). 悬而未决（pending approval  pending approval, cases pending  pending cases）；介词 pending 表「在……发生前」（pending further notice  pending further notice）。正式、法律、商务语体。
[例] ① The deal is pending regulatory approval. 交易有待监管批准。 ② Several charges are still pending against the former director. 对前董事的几项指控仍未裁决。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *pendere*（ hang）→ 悬着 → 待定。
[族] depend  depend, suspend  suspend, pendant  pendant
[搭] pending approval  pending approval, pending investigation  pending investigation, pending further notice  pending further notice, pending trial  pending trial
[联] awaiting  awaiting, unresolved  unresolved, ongoing  ongoing, settled  settled, approved  approved, complete  complete, wait  wait
[记] pending 不可作动词。pending vs. waiting：pending 更正式，多用于公文。介词 pending = while waiting for

**ready**
英/美 /ˈredi/
adj. 准备好的；愿意的；现成的
[义] Prepared for something; willing; immediately available. 准备就绪（ready to leave  ready to leave, get ready 准备好）；愿意（ready to help  ready to help）；现成的（ready meal  ready meal）。口语极频，表「状态已到位、可以行动」。
[形] 比较级 readier；最高级 readiest
[例] ① Dinner is ready—please wash your hands. 饭好了——请洗手。 ② Are you ready to start the presentation? 你准备好开始演示了吗？
[搭] get ready 准备好, ready to do  ready to do, ready for  ready for, ready-made  ready-made, at the ready  at the ready
[联] prepared  prepared, willing  willing, set  set, available  available, unfinished  unfinished, reluctant  reluctant, prompt  prompt
[记] ready to do 极常用。ready vs. prepared：ready 更口语；prepared 更正式、常指「事先准备」

**prepared**
英/美 /prɪˈpeəd/ 美 /prɪˈperd/
adj. 有准备的；愿意的
[义] Made ready in advance; willing to do something. 事先已做好准备（be prepared for emergencies  prepared for emergencies）；愿意（I'm prepared to accept  prepared to accept）。比 ready 更强调「提前筹划、有预案」。
[例] ① Always be prepared for unexpected delays when traveling abroad. 出国旅行总要防备意外延误。 ② She was not prepared to compromise on safety standards. 她不愿在安全标准上妥协。
[源] 源自 prepare（准备）+ -ed。
[族] prepare  prepare, preparation  preparation, preparedness  preparedness
[搭] be prepared for  prepared for, well prepared  well prepared, prepared to do  prepared to do, mentally prepared  mentally prepared
[联] ready  ready, willing  willing, equipped  equipped, unprepared  unprepared, plan  plan, anticipate  anticipate, set  set
[记] prepared to do 表「愿意（尤指经考虑后）」。preparedness 名词：emergency preparedness 应急准备

**vacant**
英/美 /ˈveɪkənt/
adj. 空的；空缺的；茫然的
[义] Empty and available for use; not filled (job); showing no sign of thought. 空间无人占用（vacant seat 空位, vacant lot  vacant lot）；职位空缺（a vacant position  vacant position）；眼神空洞（a vacant stare  vacant stare）。正式程度高于 empty（empty 偏「里面没有东西」）。
[例] ① Is this seat vacant, or is someone using it? 这个座位空着吗，还是有人？ ② The company has several vacant positions in the sales department. 公司销售部有几个空缺职位。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *vacare*（ be empty）→ 空着 →  vacant。
[族] vacancy  vacancy, vacate  vacate, vacation  vacation（同源异义）
[搭] vacant seat/room 空座/空房, vacant position  vacant position, vacant lot  vacant lot, vacant stare  vacant stare, remain vacant  remain vacant
[联] empty  empty, occupied  occupied, available  available, free  free, blank  blank, hire  hire, fill  fill
[记] vacant 多指「应被占用而空着」；empty 多指「里面无物」。vacancy 名词：job vacancy 职位空缺

**occupied**
英/美 /ˈɒkjupaɪd/ 美 /ˈɑːkjupaɪd/
adj. 被占用的；忙碌的
[义] Being used by someone; busy. 空间、资源已被占用（Occupied）；人忙碌（keep sb. occupied 使某人有事做）。与 vacant/free 相对。动词 occupy 见关系或行为章；此处取形容词状态义。
[例] ① All the changing rooms were occupied, so we had to wait. 更衣室都有人，我们只好等。 ② Puzzle games keep the children occupied on long trips. 长途旅行中益智游戏让孩子们有事可做。
[源] 源自 occupy（占用）+ -ed。
[族] occupy  occupy, occupation  occupation, occupant  occupant
[搭] fully occupied  fully occupied, keep sb. occupied  occupied, occupied territory  occupied territory, toilet occupied  occupied
[联] vacant  vacant, busy  busy, engaged  engaged, free  free, available  available, empty  empty, use  use
[记] 英式厕所标识 Engaged；美式常用 Occupied。occupied vs. busy：occupied 可指「空间被占」；busy 专指人/时段忙

**hang**
英/美 /hæŋ/
v. 悬挂；垂下；停滞
[义] To be attached at the top so the lower part is free; to remain static in the air or in a state. 悬于上方（curtains hang from the rail  curtains hang）；低垂（His head hung in shame  head hung）；状态停滞（The project hung in the balance  hung in the balance）。不规则 hung / hung；表「绞死」时过去式 hanged。
[形] hung / hung / hanging；表绞刑时 hanged / hanged
[例] ① A single light bulb hung from the ceiling. 一只灯泡吊在天花板上。 ② The decision hung in the balance until the final vote. 直到最后一票，决定仍悬而未决。
[搭] hang from  hang from, hang in the balance  hang in the balance, hang on  hang on, hang out  hang out, hang up  hang up
[联] suspend  suspend, dangle  dangle, lean  lean, attach  attach, drop  drop, fall  fall, stuck  stuck, balance  balance
[记] hang vs. hanged：悬挂、垂下用 hung；绞刑用 hanged（He was hanged）。hang in the balance 表「未定、悬而未决」

**lean**
英/美 /liːn/
v. 倚靠；倾斜
adj.  lean
[义] To rest against something for support; to slope; thin (adj.). 倚靠（lean against the wall  lean against the wall）；倾斜（The tower leans slightly  leans slightly）；形容词 lean 表「瘦、精简」。本章取「倚靠、倾斜」姿态状态义。
[形] leaned / leant / leaned / leant / leaning；形容词 leaner, leanest
[例] ① She leaned against the doorframe, listening. 她倚在门框上听着。 ② The old fence leaned dangerously toward the road. 旧篱笆危险地向路面倾斜。
[搭] lean against/on  lean against/on, lean back  lean back, lean forward  lean forward, lean toward  lean toward, lean years  lean years（形容词）
[联] rest  rest, bend  bend, slope  slope, straight  straight, upright  upright, support  support, thin  thin
[记] 过去式 leaned / leant 英美均可。lean on sb. 可比喻「依赖某人」。形容词 lean 义「瘦、精干」见属性章

**pose**
英/美 /pəʊz/
v. 摆姿势；造成；提出
n. 姿势
[义] To hold a particular body position; to create a problem; to ask a question. 维持某种身体姿态（pose for a photo  pose for a photo）；造成（pose a risk  pose a risk）；提出（pose a question  pose a question）。名词 pose 指「姿势、装腔作势」。
[形] posed / posed / posing
[例] ① The model posed beside the vintage car for the shoot. 模特在复古车旁摆拍。 ② Outdated software poses a security risk to the whole network. 过时软件对整个网络构成安全风险。
[源] 源自古法语 *poser*（放置），拉丁语 *pausare*（停住）→ 停在一个姿势 → 摆姿势。
[族] position 位置, posture  posture, repose  repose
[搭] pose for  pose for, pose a threat/risk/question  pose a threat/risk/question, strike a pose  strike a pose, hold a pose  hold a pose
[联] posture  posture, position 位置, sit  sit, stand  stand, model  model, risk  risk, pretend  pretend
[记] pose 作名词可贬：His friendliness is just a pose 他的友好只是装样子。pose vs. posture：posture 更中性指「体态」。

**stuck**
英/美 /stʌk/
adj. 卡住的；动不了的；困住的
[义] Unable to move or progress; fixed in a difficult situation (informal). 物理上卡住（The key is stuck  key is stuck）；抽象上陷住（I'm stuck on this question  stuck on this question, feel stuck in a job  feel stuck）。stick 的过去分词作形容词用，口语极频。
[例] ① The elevator got stuck between floors for twenty minutes. 电梯卡在楼层之间达二十分钟。 ② Many graduates feel stuck in low-paid jobs with little chance of promotion. 许多毕业生觉得困在低薪工作里，几无晋升机会。
[搭] get stuck 卡住, stuck in traffic  stuck in traffic, stuck on  stuck on, feel stuck  feel stuck, stuck with  stuck with
[联] trapped  trapped, fixed  fixed, immobile  immobile, jammed  jammed, free  free, move  move, unstuck  unstuck
[记] stuck 为 stick 的过去分词：get stuck / be stuck。stuck with 表「被迫留着、甩不掉」。stuck vs. trapped：stuck 更口语；trapped 更强调「被困住、无法 escape」

**intrinsic**
英/美 /ɪnˈtrɪnzɪk/ 美 /ɪnˈtrɪnsɪk/
adj. 内在的；固有的
[义] Belonging naturally to something; essential to its nature. 事物本身固有的、不可或缺的（intrinsic value  intrinsic value, intrinsic motivation  intrinsic motivation）。与 extrinsic（外在的）相对，哲学、教育、心理学常见。
[例] ① The intrinsic value of the painting lies in its craftsmanship, not its price. 这幅画的价值在于工艺本身，而非售价。 ② She is driven by intrinsic motivation rather than by rewards. 她靠内在动力驱动，而非外在奖励。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *intrinsecus*（ inwardly），*intra-*（内）+ *secus*（ along）→ 内在的。
[族] intrinsically  intrinsically
[搭] intrinsic value  intrinsic value, intrinsic motivation  intrinsic motivation, intrinsic quality  intrinsic quality, intrinsic to  intrinsic to
[联] inherent  inherent, internal  internal, innate  innate, extrinsic  extrinsic, essential  essential, natural  natural, built-in  built-in
[记] intrinsic to 表「……所固有的」。intrinsic vs. inherent：近义，intrinsic 更偏「本质属性」；inherent 更偏「与生俱来、难以分离」

**inherent**
英/美 /ɪnˈherənt/ 美 /ɪnˈhɪrənt/
adj. 固有的；内在的
[义] Existing as a natural and permanent part of something. 作为自然、永久组成部分而存在（inherent risk  inherent risk, inherent in the design  inherent in the design）。常接 in：risk inherent in the process 流程固有的风险。
[例] ① There are inherent risks in any investment. 任何投资都有固有风险。 ② The conflict was inherent in the way the two departments were organized. 冲突在两部门的组织方式中本就不可避免。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *inhaerere*（ stick in），*in-* + *haerere*（粘）→ 粘在里面 → 固有的。
[族] inherit  inherit, inheritance  inheritance, inherently  inherently
[搭] inherent in  inherent in, inherent risk/danger/flaw  inherent risk/danger/flaw, inherently  inherently
[联] intrinsic  intrinsic, innate  innate, built-in  built-in, extrinsic  extrinsic, natural  natural, essential  essential, permanent  permanent
[记] inherent in 为固定搭配。inherent vs. intrinsic：inherent 强调「内在于、难以剥离」；intrinsic 强调「本质价值/属性」

**extrinsic**
英/美 /eksˈtrɪnzɪk/ 美 /eksˈtrɪnsɪk/
adj. 外在的；外来的
[义] Not part of the essential nature of something; coming from outside. 非本质、来自外部（extrinsic factors  extrinsic factors, extrinsic motivation  extrinsic motivation）。与 intrinsic / inherent 构成对立，学术与心理学常用。
[例] ① Student performance depends on both intrinsic ability and extrinsic support. 学生表现既取决于内在能力，也取决于外部支持。 ② The bonus was an extrinsic reward for meeting sales targets. 奖金是达成销售目标的外在奖励。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *extrinsecus*（ outwardly），*extra-*（外）→ 外在的。
[族] extrinsically  extrinsically
[搭] extrinsic factors  extrinsic factors, extrinsic motivation  extrinsic motivation, extrinsic value  extrinsic value
[联] intrinsic  intrinsic, inherent  inherent, external  external, outside  outside, internal  internal, reward  reward, factor  factor
[记] extrinsic vs. external：extrinsic 更抽象、常指「非本质属性」；external 更宽，指「外部的」

**static**
英/美 /ˈstætɪk/
adj. 静态的；不变的
n. 静电； static
[义] Not moving or changing; fixed. 不移动、不变化（remain static  remain static, static population  static population）；名词指静电干扰（ radio static  radio static）。与 dynamic 相对。
[例] ① Wages have remained static for several years despite rising prices. 物价上涨，工资却多年不变。 ② I couldn't hear the station clearly because of the static. 静电干扰让我听不清电台。
[源] 源自希腊语 *statikos*（ causing to stand），*stat-*（站立）→ 静立 → 静态。
[族] station  station, stable  stable, statistic  statistic, status 状况
[搭] remain static  remain static, static electricity  static electricity, static image  static image, static population  static population
[联] stationary  stationary, stable  stable, fixed  fixed, dynamic  dynamic, unchanged  unchanged, motionless  motionless, status 状况
[记] static vs. stationary：static 可指「数据、状态不变」；stationary 专指「物理上不动」。static 作名词指静电

**presence**
英/美 /ˈprezns/
n. 存在；出席；在场
[义] The state of being in a particular place; the fact of existing or being felt. 在某处「人/物在场」（in the presence of 在……面前, presence at the meeting 出席会议）；亦可指某种气质或影响力的存在（a strong presence 气场强, make one's presence felt 让人感觉到你在）。不可数为主；可数指短暂到场（make a brief presence）。
[例] ① Your presence at the ceremony would mean a lot to the family. 你能出席典礼对这家人意义重大。 ② She has a calm presence that puts people at ease. 她有一种让人放松的气场。
[搭] in the presence of 在……面前, presence of mind 镇定, make one's presence felt 让人感知到在场, physical presence 本人在场, online presence 线上存在感
[联] absence 缺席, exist 存在, appear 出现, aura 气场, attend 出席, missing 缺席, impression 印象, influence 影响力
[记] 不可数：in the presence of。presence of mind 指「临危不乱」。presence vs. attendance：presence 强调「在场这一状态」；attendance 强调「到场记录/出席行为」

**absence**
英/美 /ˈæbsəns/
n. 缺席；不在；缺乏
[义] The state of not being in a place; the lack of something. 人不在场（absence from work 缺勤, in sb.'s absence 某人不在时）；某物缺失（in the absence of evidence 缺乏证据时）。不可数为主；可数指一次缺席（repeated absences）。
[例] ① In the absence of further information, we cannot draw a conclusion. 在没有更多信息的情况下，我们无法下结论。 ② Her absence from the team was felt immediately. 她一不在，队伍立刻感到缺口。
[搭] in the absence of 缺乏/在……不在时, absence from 缺席, leave of absence 停职/休假, conspicuous absence  conspicuous absence, repeated absences 多次缺席
[联] presence 在场, absent 缺席的, lack 缺乏, missing 缺失的, leave 离开, void  void, gap 缺口, without 没有
[记] 不可数：in the absence of。leave of absence 指获准的停职或学术休假。absence vs. lack：absence 偏「不在场/没有」；lack 更宽，指不足

**latent**
英/美 /ˈleɪtnt/
adj. 潜在的；潜伏的
[义] Existing but not yet visible, active, or developed. 已存在但尚未显现或发作（latent talent 潜在天赋, latent infection  latent infection, latent demand  latent demand）。比 dormant 更偏「尚未表现」，不一定「休眠待机」；医学、心理、市场分析常见。
[例] ① The test can detect latent infections before symptoms appear. 该检测可在症状出现前发现潜伏感染。 ② The product tapped latent demand among younger consumers. 该产品触发了年轻消费者的潜在需求。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *latere*（隐藏）→ 藏着 → 潜在的。
[族] latency  latency, latently  latently
[搭] latent talent 潜在天赋, latent infection  latent infection, latent demand  latent demand, remain latent 保持 latent, latent period 潜伏期
[联] dormant 休眠的, inactive  inactive, hidden  hidden, potential  potential, manifest  manifest, emerge  emerge, active  active, visible  visible
[记] latent 常修饰 ability, infection, demand, period。latent vs. dormant：latent 强调「未显现」；dormant 强调「暂停、可再激活」

**manifest**
英/美 /ˈmænɪfest/
v. 显现；表明
adj. 明显的
n.  manifest
[义] To show something clearly; obvious. 使显现、表露（symptoms manifest themselves 症状显现, manifest as anger  manifest as anger）；形容词表「一目了然」（with manifest relief  manifest relief）。名词 manifest 指货单（航运）。与 latent 相对。
[形] manifested / manifested / manifesting
[例] ① Stress can manifest itself as headaches or sleep problems. 压力可表现为头痛或睡眠问题。 ② There was manifest disappointment when the deal fell through. 交易告吹时，失望写在脸上。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *manifestus*（可被手触到的），*manus*（手）+ *festus*（ struck）→ 一眼能抓住 → 明显。
[族] manifestation  manifestation
[搭] manifest itself as 表现为, manifest interest  manifest interest, with manifest relief  manifest relief, cargo manifest  manifest, self-manifest  self-manifest
[联] latent  latent, appear 出现, show 显示, reveal  reveal, obvious  obvious, express  express, symptom  symptom, display  display
[记] manifest 正式；口语表「明显」多用 obvious / clear。manifest itself 常接 as + 名词。manifest vs. demonstrate：manifest 偏「显露出来」；demonstrate 偏「证明、示范」

**outlast**
英/美 /ˌaʊtˈlɑːst/ 美 /ˌaʊtˈlæst/
v. 比……持久；活过
[义] To last longer than someone or something else; to survive beyond. 比另一事物更久（outlast a trend  outlast a trend）；比某人活得久（outlast one's rivals  outlast one's rivals）。强调「在比较中续存更久」，与 survive 近义但更绑「时长对比」。
[形] outlasted / outlasted / outlasting
[例] ① Few fads outlast more than a couple of seasons in fashion. 时尚界很少有风潮能撑过两季以上。 ② The stone walls have outlasted generations of owners. 石墙比一代代主人都更长久。
[搭] outlast a trend/crisis  outlast a trend/crisis, outlast one's rivals  outlast one's rivals, outlast expectations  outlast expectations
[联] survive 幸存, last 持续, endure  endure, exceed  exceed, perish 丧生, fade  fade, remain 保持, persist  persist
[记] outlast 及物：A outlasted B。与 outlive 近义，outlive 更常指「比某人活得久」。outlast 可用于物与抽象趋势

**stasis**
英/美 /ˈsteɪsɪs/ 美 /ˈstæsɪs/
n. 停滞；静态平衡
[义] A state in which there is no change or development; equilibrium. 无变化、无发展的状态（political stasis  political stasis, stasis between growth and decay  stasis between growth and decay）。正式、书面、学术；医学亦指血液淤滞（deep vein stasis）。不可数。
[例] ① Years of stasis left the organization unable to respond to new competitors. 多年停滞使机构无力应对新对手。 ② The story ends not in chaos but in an uneasy stasis. 故事并非以混乱收场，而是形成一种不安的静态平衡。
[源] 源自希腊语 *stasis*（站立、停止），与 static / station 同根。
[族] static 静态的, station  station, stable  stable
[搭] political stasis  political stasis, in stasis 处于停滞, stasis of blood 血液淤滞, uneasy stasis 不安的静态平衡
[联] static  static, stagnation  stagnation, equilibrium  equilibrium, pause  pause, dynamic  dynamic, change  change, deadlock  deadlock, balance  balance
[记] 不可数。stasis vs. static：stasis 为名词，指「停滞状态」；static 多作形容词。in stasis 常见于科幻/文学

**underway**
英/美 /ˌʌndəˈweɪ/ 美 /ˌʌndərˈweɪ/
adj. 进行中的
[义] Having started and now in progress. 已经启动、正在进行（work is underway  work is underway, get underway  get underway）。常作表语（The project is underway）。与 ongoing 近义，underway 更绑「已开动、在途中」。
[例] ① Rescue operations were already underway when dawn broke. 天亮时救援行动已在进行。 ② Preparations are underway for the annual conference. 年度会议筹备工作正在进行。
[搭] get underway  get underway, already underway  already underway, work underway  work underway, well underway  well underway
[联] ongoing  ongoing, current  current, in progress  in progress, start  start, complete  complete, pending  pending, active  active, launch  launch
[记] 常作表语：be underway。get underway 表「开始进行」（The meeting got underway at nine）。underway vs. ongoing：underway 略偏「已启动在途」

**suspended**
英/美 /səˈspendɪd/
adj. 暂停的；悬浮的
[义] Temporarily stopped or hanging in place. 暂时中止（suspended operations  suspended operations, suspended sentence  suspended sentence）；物理上悬吊（suspended bridge  suspended bridge）。suspend 的过去分词作形容词；强调「挂起、未继续」状态。
[例] ① Talks remain suspended until both sides agree to a ceasefire. 在双方同意停火前，谈判仍处于暂停状态。 ② She stared at the suspended particles in the sunbeam. 她盯着阳光里悬浮的微粒。
[源] 源自 suspend（悬吊、暂停）+ -ed。
[族] suspend  suspend, suspension  suspension, suspender  suspender
[搭] suspended operations  suspended operations, remain suspended  remain suspended, suspended sentence  suspended sentence, suspended animation  suspended animation, suspended bridge  suspended bridge
[联] pending  pending, pause  pause, halt  halt, active  active, resume  resume, hang  hang, temporary  temporary, frozen  frozen
[记] suspended animation 指「假死/休眠状态」（科幻、医学）。suspended sentence 法律「缓刑」。resume 表「恢复进行」

**poised**
英/美 /pɔɪzd/
adj. 准备好的；沉着的；悬停的
[义] Ready and balanced; calm and confident; not moving but about to move.  poised for  ready for action  poised for action；沉着自信（poised and professional  poised and professional）；悬停未落（a bird poised in mid-air  a bird poised in mid-air）。pose 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The company is poised for expansion into Asian markets. 公司准备向亚洲市场扩张。 ② She remained poised throughout the hostile questioning. 面对敌意提问，她始终沉着。
[搭] poised for  poised for, poised to do  poised to do, poised and confident  poised and confident, remain poised  remain poised
[联] ready  ready, prepared  prepared, balanced  balanced, calm  calm, set  set, hover  hover, confident  confident, tense  tense
[记] poised for / poised to do 表「蓄势待发」。poised vs. ready：poised 更带「平衡、沉着」画面。名词 poise 指「仪态、镇定」

**dynamic**
英/美 /daɪˈnæmɪk/
adj. 动态的；不断变化的
n.  dynamic
[义] Characterized by constant change or activity; energetic. 处于变化、活动之中（dynamic market  dynamic market, dynamic equilibrium  dynamic equilibrium）；亦可指「有活力的」（a dynamic leader  a dynamic leader）。与 static 构成对立。名词 dynamics 指力学/动态关系。
[例] ① The industry is highly dynamic, with new players entering every year. 该行业高度动态，每年都有新玩家进入。 ② They studied the group dynamics during the workshop. 他们研究了工作坊中的群体互动模式。
[源] 源自希腊语 *dynamis*（力），与 dynamite 同根。
[族] dynamics  dynamics, dynamism  dynamism, dynamite  dynamite
[搭] dynamic market  dynamic market, dynamic equilibrium  dynamic equilibrium, group dynamics  group dynamics, dynamic range  dynamic range
[联] static  static, stable  stable, changing  changing, active  active, energetic  energetic, fluid  fluid, stagnant  stagnant, momentum  momentum
[记] 可数名词 a dynamic 指「动力/活力因素」。dynamic vs. static：dynamic 强调「在变、在动」；static 强调「不变」。group dynamics 固定搭配

**unstable**
英/美 /ʌnˈsteɪbl/
adj. 不稳定的；易变的
[义] Likely to change suddenly; not firmly fixed. 易突然变化（unstable economy  unstable economy, emotionally unstable  emotionally unstable）；物理上不稳固（unstable structure  unstable structure）。与 stable 相对；本章取「状态不稳」义。
[形] 比较级 more unstable；最高级 most unstable
[例] ① The patient remains in critical but unstable condition. 病人情况危急且不稳定。 ② Politically unstable regions often see capital flight. 政局不稳的地区常出现资本外流。
[源] un-（不）+ stable（稳定的）→ 不稳定。
[族] instability  instability, stabilize  stabilize
[搭] unstable condition  unstable condition, politically unstable  politically unstable, unstable economy  unstable economy, emotionally unstable  emotionally unstable, remain unstable  remain unstable
[联] stable  stable, static  static, volatile  volatile, shaky  shaky, secure  secure, fixed  fixed, dynamic  dynamic, steady  steady
[记] unstable 可修饰 condition, economy, relationship, surface。instability 名词：political instability 政治动荡

**steady**
英/美 /ˈstedi/
adj. 稳定的；平稳的
v.  stabilize
[义] Firmly fixed, balanced, or constant; not shaking or changing suddenly. 稳定不变（steady progress  steady progress, steady relationship  steady relationship）；平稳不晃（hold steady  hold steady）。动词 steady 指使稳定（steady the ladder  steady the ladder）。与 unstable / static 不同：steady 偏「平稳持续、不剧烈波动」。
[形] 比较级 steadier；最高级 steadiest；动词 steadied / steadied / steadying
[例] ① After a shaky start, sales showed steady growth. 开局波动后，销售稳步增长。 ② She steadied herself against the railing before continuing down the stairs. 她扶稳栏杆，继续下楼。
[搭] steady progress  steady progress, hold steady  hold steady, steady relationship  steady relationship, steady income  steady income, steady state  steady state
[联] stable  stable, constant  constant, firm  firm, shaky  shaky, unstable  unstable, even  even, regular  regular, static  static
[记] steady 口语可指「固定的男女朋友」（a steady boyfriend）。hold steady 表「稳住不动/保持水平」。steady vs. stable：steady 更强调「平稳、不晃」

**withstand**
英/美 /wɪðˈstænd/
v. 承受；抵御；经受住
[义] To remain undamaged or unaffected by something; to resist successfully. 在压力、攻击、考验下「撑住、未被击垮」（withstand pressure  withstand pressure, withstand scrutiny  withstand scrutiny）。比 endure 更绑「对抗外部力量而保持原状」；正式、书面。
[形] withstood / withstood / withstanding
[例] ① The dam was built to withstand earthquakes up to magnitude 7. 大坝设计可抗七级地震。 ② Few products can withstand such intense price competition. 很少有产品能扛住如此激烈的价格战。
[源] with-（ against）+ stand（站立）→ 站着顶住 → 抵御。
[搭] withstand pressure  withstand pressure, withstand scrutiny  withstand scrutiny, withstand the test of time  withstand the test of time, withstand an attack  withstand an attack
[联] endure  endure, resist  resist, survive  survive, bear  bear, tolerate  tolerate, collapse  collapse, yield  yield, weather  weather
[记] withstand 及物，不接 withstand to。withstand the test of time 表「经得住时间考验」。withstand vs. endure：withstand 强调「顶住未垮」

**devoid**
英/美 /dɪˈvɔɪd/
adj. 完全没有的
[义] Completely lacking in something. 完全不具备（devoid of meaning  devoid of meaning, devoid of emotion  devoid of emotion）。只作表语，后接 of；正式、书面。比 without 语气更强、更绝对。
[例] ① The speech was devoid of any concrete proposals. 演讲没有任何具体方案。 ② The landscape looked bleak and devoid of colour. 那片景色萧瑟，毫无色彩。
[搭] devoid of 完全没有, entirely devoid of  entirely devoid of, devoid of meaning  devoid of meaning, devoid of emotion  devoid of emotion
[联] lacking  lacking, empty  empty, bare  bare, bereft  bereft, without  without, full  full, rich  rich, void  void
[记] 只作表语：be devoid of，不说 × a devoid speech，用 a speech devoid of content。devoid vs. lacking：devoid 语气绝对、正式

**bereft**
英/美 /bɪˈreft/
adj. 失去的；缺少的
[义] Completely lacking something needed or valued, especially after a loss. 因失去而极度缺失（bereft of hope  bereft of hope, bereft after his death  bereft after his death）。正式、文学；常带 grief 或丧失背景。亦可为 bereave 的过去分词。
[例] ① The town was bereft of its main employer when the factory closed. 工厂关闭后，小镇失去了主要雇主。 ② She looked bereft when she heard the news. 听到消息时，她一脸失落。
[源] 古英语 *bereafian*（剥夺），与 rob 同源。
[搭] bereft of 失去/缺乏, left bereft  left bereft, bereft of hope  bereft of hope, bereft of power  bereft of power
[联] devoid  devoid, lacking  lacking, deprived  deprived, grief  grief, loss  loss, empty  empty, robbed  robbed, orphaned  orphaned
[记] bereft of + 抽象名词极常见。bereft vs. devoid：bereft 更绑「丧失后的空落」；devoid 更中性正式

**complete**
英/美 /kəmˈpliːt/
adj. 完整的；完全的
v. 使完整
[义] Having all necessary parts; total. 全部齐备、无缺失（a complete set  a complete set, complete silence  complete silence）；彻底（complete failure  complete failure）。动词 complete 指使完整或完成。本章取形容词「完整状态」义；完成动作义与变化章有交叉。
[形] 动词 completed / completed / completing
[例] ① The archive holds a complete record of the negotiations. 档案库有谈判的完整记录。 ② The room fell into complete silence when the judge entered. 法官进来时，全场鸦雀无声。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *completus*，*com-*（完全）+ *plere*（填满）→ 填满 → 完整。
[族] completion  completion, completely  completely, incomplete  incomplete
[搭] complete set  complete set, complete silence  complete silence, complete control  complete control, complete stranger  complete stranger
[联] incomplete  incomplete, whole  whole, partial  partial, full  full, empty  empty, total  total, intact  intact, finished  finished
[记] complete stranger 指「完全不认识的人」。complete vs. whole：complete 强调「该有的都有」；whole 强调「作为一个整体未分裂」

**incomplete**
英/美 /ˌɪnkəmˈpliːt/
adj. 不完整的；未完成的
[义] Not having all necessary parts; not finished. 缺部分、未完工（incomplete data  incomplete data, incomplete work  incomplete work）。与 complete 相对；亦可指「不全面」（an incomplete picture  an incomplete picture）。
[例] ① The report was rejected because the data were incomplete. 报告因数据不完整被拒。 ② We still have an incomplete understanding of how the brain works. 我们对大脑工作机制仍了解不全。
[源] in-（不）+ complete（完整的）→ 不完整。
[族] incompletely  incompletely, incompleteness  incompleteness
[搭] incomplete data  incomplete data, incomplete work  incomplete work, incomplete picture  incomplete picture, remain incomplete  remain incomplete
[联] complete  complete, partial  partial, unfinished  unfinished, fragmentary  fragmentary, whole  whole, missing  missing, draft  draft
[记] incomplete 可修饰 data, form, picture, understanding。incomplete vs. partial：incomplete 强调「缺块未完工」；partial 可指「部分的、偏袒的」

**blank**
英/美 /blæŋk/
adj. 空白的；茫然的
n.  blank
[义] Empty of content; showing no expression or understanding. 无内容（blank page  blank page, blank screen  blank screen）；表情茫然（a blank stare  a blank stare）。名词 blank 指空白处或空白表格。与 vacant 不同：blank 偏「表面无内容/无表情」。
[例] ① She signed a blank cheque without checking the amount. 她没看金额就签了空白支票。 ② His mind went blank during the interview. 面试时他脑子一片空白。
[搭] blank page  blank page, blank stare  blank stare, go blank  go blank, fill in the blanks  fill in the blanks, blank screen  blank screen
[联] empty  empty, vacant  vacant, void  void, expressionless  expressionless, clear  clear, full  full, form  form
[记] go blank 指「脑子一片空白」。fill in the blanks 字面或比喻「补全信息」。blank vs. empty：blank 多指「未填写/无表情」；empty 指「里面没有东西」

**full**
英/美 /fʊl/
adj. 满的；完整的；充分的
[义] Containing as much as possible; complete; at maximum capacity. 容器、空间装满（The room is full  The room is full）；充分（full details  full details, full capacity  full capacity）。与 empty / vacant 相对。本章取「满/处于满载状态」义。
[形] 比较级 fuller；最高级 fullest
[例] ① The hotel was full, so we had to find another place to stay. 酒店已满，我们只好另找住处。 ② Please provide full details of your employment history. 请提供完整工作经历详情。
[搭] full capacity  full capacity, full details  full details, full of  full of, at full speed  at full speed, in full  in full
[联] empty  empty, vacant  vacant, complete  complete, packed  packed, partial  partial, occupied  occupied, loaded  loaded, whole  whole
[记] full of 表「充满」。in full 表「全额/全文」。full vs. complete：full 可指物理「满」；complete 强调「该有的都有」

**fluid**
英/美 /ˈfluːɪd/
adj. 流动的；易变的
n. 流体
[义] Able to flow; likely to change. 可流动、非固定（fluid state 流体状态, fluid situation 易变局势）；名词指液体。比喻义强调边界、角色、状态不僵死，与 static / fixed 相对。
[例] ① The situation on the ground remains fluid and could change overnight. 实地局势仍易变，一夜之间就可能改变。 ② Water is the most common fluid in everyday life. 水是日常生活中最常见的流体。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *fluidus*（流动），*fluere*（流）→ 流动。
[族] fluent 流利的, flush 涌出, influence 影响（同根 *flu-*）
[搭] fluid situation 易变局势, fluid state 流体状态, fluid boundaries 流动边界, remain fluid 保持未定, fluid movement 流畅动作
[联] static 静态的, dynamic 动态的, flexible 灵活的, fixed 固定的, stable 稳定的, changeable 易变的, liquid 液体, flow 流动
[记] fluid 作形容词比 liquid 更宽，可指局势、组织、身份。fluid vs. liquid：liquid 专指液体；fluid 可喻「不固定」

**equilibrium**
英/美 /ˌiːkwɪˈlɪbriəm/ 美 /ˌekwəˈlɪbriəm/
n. 平衡；均衡状态
[义] A state of balance between opposing forces or influences. 对立力量之间的平衡（reach equilibrium 达到均衡, dynamic equilibrium 动态平衡）；亦可指身体平衡感。可数/不可数。比 balance 更正式、科学。
[例] ① The market eventually returned to equilibrium after the shock. 冲击过后，市场最终回归均衡。 ② She struggled to regain her equilibrium after the fall. 摔倒后她努力恢复身体平衡。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *aequilibrium*，*aequus*（平等）+ *libra*（秤）→ 秤平 → 均衡。
[族] equilibrate 使平衡
[搭] reach equilibrium 达到均衡, dynamic equilibrium 动态平衡, in equilibrium 处于平衡, market equilibrium 市场均衡, lose one's equilibrium 失去平衡
[联] balance 平衡, stability 稳定, stasis 停滞, steady 平稳的, unstable 不稳定的, dynamic 动态的, tension 张力, harmony 和谐
[记] dynamic equilibrium 动态平衡（化学、生态常见）。equilibrium vs. balance：equilibrium 更学术，强调力量对冲后的稳态

**halt**
英/美 /hɔːlt/
v. 停止；中止
n. 停止
[义] To stop moving or happening; to bring to a stop. 运动或进程停下（halt production 停产, come to a halt 停下来）。比 stop 正式，常见于命令、新闻、交通。名词表停止、停顿。
[形] halted / halted / halting
[例] ① Production halted when the main supplier went bankrupt. 主供应商破产后，生产停了下来。 ② Traffic came to a halt as protesters blocked the road. 抗议者堵路，交通陷入停滞。
[源] 古英语 *healtian*（跛行、停步）→ 停住。
[族] halting 迟疑的, halter 缰绳
[搭] come to a halt 停下来, halt production 停产, halt traffic 阻断交通, halt operations 停止运营, put a halt to 制止
[联] stop 停止, cease 终止, pause 暂停, suspend 暂停, resume 恢复, continue 继续, freeze 冻结, stall 停滞
[记] come to a halt 极常用。halt vs. stop：halt 更正式，常指系统性中止。halting 形容词表「断断续续、迟疑的」

**bare**
英/美 /beə(r)/
adj. 赤裸的；光秃的；仅仅的
v. 暴露
[义] Without covering or contents; just sufficient. 无覆盖（bare feet 赤脚, bare walls 光秃墙壁）；仅够最低限度（the bare minimum 最低限度, bare facts 赤裸裸的事实）。动词 bare 指暴露（bare one's soul 袒露心声）。
[形] 比较级 barer；最高级 barest；动词 bared / bared / baring
[例] ① The apartment was bare except for a mattress on the floor. 公寓里除地上一张床垫外空空如也。 ② We only had the bare essentials for the camping trip. 露营我们只带了最基本的必需品。
[搭] bare minimum 最低限度, bare walls 光秃墙壁, bare feet 赤脚, bare facts 赤裸裸的事实, bare essentials 基本必需品
[联] empty 空的, naked 赤裸的, stripped 剥光的, plain 朴素的, minimal 最低的, full 满的, covered 有覆盖的, sparse 稀疏的
[记] the bare minimum / bare essentials 极常用。bare vs. empty：bare 强调无覆盖、无装饰；empty 强调内部无物

**lacking**
英/美 /ˈlækɪŋ/
adj. 缺乏的；不足的
[义] Not having enough of something; missing. 缺少某要素（lacking in confidence 缺乏信心, lacking evidence 缺乏证据）。常作表语或后置定语。比 without 更带评价，可指品质不足。
[例] ① The proposal is creative but lacking in practical detail. 方案有创意，但缺乏实操细节。 ② She felt her experience was lacking compared with other candidates. 她觉得与其他候选人比，自己的经验不足。
[搭] lacking in 缺乏, lacking confidence 缺乏信心, lacking evidence 缺乏证据, skills lacking 技能不足, be found lacking 被发现不足
[联] devoid 完全没有的, bereft 失去的, missing 缺失的, insufficient 不足的, abundant 充裕的, complete 完整的, weak 弱的, scarce 稀缺的
[记] lacking in + 名词/抽象品质。be found lacking 表「经检验不足」。lacking vs. devoid：lacking 较温和；devoid 绝对、正式

**hover**
英/美 /ˈhɒvə(r)/ 美 /ˈhʌvər/
v. 悬停；徘徊；临近
[义] To stay in the air in one place; to remain near without settling; to be close to a level. 空中悬停；徘徊不去；数值临近某水平（hover around 5% 徘徊在百分之五左右）。介于 hang 与 land 之间：悬而未定。
[形] hovered / hovered / hovering
[例] ① A drone hovered above the crowd filming the parade. 一架无人机在人群上方悬停拍摄游行。 ② Unemployment has hovered around four percent for months. 失业率数月徘徊在百分之四左右。
[搭] hover over 在……上方盘旋, hover around 徘徊于, hover in the background 在旁徘徊, hover near 在附近徘徊
[联] hang 悬挂, float 漂浮, linger 徘徊, pause 暂停, poised 蓄势的, settle 安定, fly 飞, orbit 环绕
[记] hover 不及物。hover around 可指人徘徊，也可指数据「徘徊于」。hover vs. hang：hover 强调空中定住不动

**stagnation**
英/美 /stæɡˈneɪʃn/
n. 停滞；萧条
[义] A state of little or no activity, growth, or progress. 活动、增长或进步几乎停顿（economic stagnation 经济停滞, years of stagnation 多年停滞）。不可数为主。比 stasis 更绑经济、职业「停滞不前」的负面感。
[例] ① Years of stagnation left wages far behind inflation. 多年停滞使工资远远落后于通胀。 ② He feared career stagnation if he stayed in the same role. 他担心若一直做同一岗位，职业生涯会停滞。
[源] 源自 stagnate（停滞）+ -ation；拉丁语 *stagnare*（积水）→ 不流动 → 停滞。
[族] stagnate 停滞, stagnant 停滞的
[搭] economic stagnation 经济停滞, years of stagnation 多年停滞, period of stagnation 停滞期, avoid stagnation 避免停滞
[联] stasis 静态平衡, decline 衰退, growth 增长, dynamic 动态的, static 静态的, progress 进步, slump 萧条, inertia 惰性
[记] 不可数：economic stagnation。stagnation vs. stasis：stagnation 常带「僵化、无进展」负面义；stasis 可中性或科学

**shaky**
英/美 /ˈʃeɪki/
adj. 摇晃的；不稳固的；不确定的
[义] Not firm or steady; uncertain or unreliable. 物理不稳（shaky hands 颤抖的双手）；抽象不可靠（shaky evidence 站不住脚的证据, shaky start 开局不稳）。与 steady / stable 相对。
[形] 比较级 shakier；最高级 shakiest
[例] ① Her voice sounded shaky during the opening minutes of the speech. 演讲开头几分钟，她的声音有些发颤。 ② The company is on shaky financial ground after two bad quarters. 连续两个糟糕季度后，公司财务基础不稳。
[搭] shaky hands 颤抖的双手, shaky start 开局不稳, shaky evidence 站不住脚的证据, on shaky ground 立足不稳, feel shaky 感到发虚
[联] steady 平稳的, stable 稳定的, secure 稳固的, unstable 不稳定的, trembling 颤抖的, weak 弱的, uncertain 不确定的, firm 牢固的
[记] on shaky ground 表「立足不稳、理由不牢」。shaky vs. unstable：shaky 更口语，可指轻微颤抖

**outlive**
英/美 /ˌaʊtˈlɪv/
v. 比……活得久；比……持久
[义] To live longer than someone; to continue to exist after something else has ended. 比某人活得久；比某事物更持久（outlive its usefulness 过了有用期）。与 outlast 近义，outlive 更常指人。
[形] outlived / outlived / outliving
[例] ① She outlived her husband by nearly twenty years. 她比丈夫多活了将近二十年。 ② Some traditions have outlived the empires that created them. 有些传统比创造它们的帝国更长久。
[搭] outlive one's usefulness 过了有用期, outlive sb. 比某人活得久, outlive expectations 超出预期寿命
[联] survive 幸存, outlast 比……持久, die 死亡, last 持续, perish 丧生, endure 经受, obsolete 过时的, legacy 遗产
[记] outlive 及物。outlive one's usefulness 表「过了有用期、该淘汰」。outlive vs. outlast：outlive 更绑生命；outlast 可用于趋势、机构

**subsistence**
英/美 /səbˈsɪstəns/
n. 生计；最低生存
[义] The state of having just enough to stay alive; minimal means of living. 勉强维生的状态或方式（subsistence farming 自给农业, below subsistence level 低于糊口线）。不可数为主。比 survival 更绑「糊口、最低限度」。
[例] ① Many families depend on subsistence farming for their food. 许多家庭靠自给农业糊口。 ② Wages fell below subsistence level during the crisis. 危机期间工资跌至不足以维持基本生活。
[源] 源自 subsist（维持生存）+ -ence。
[族] subsist 维持生存, subsistent 存在的
[搭] subsistence farming 自给农业, subsistence level 糊口线, means of subsistence 生计手段, bare subsistence 勉强糊口
[联] survival 生存, livelihood 生计, poverty 贫困, existence 存在, survive 幸存, minimum 最低限度, thrive 繁荣, abundance 充裕
[记] subsistence level 指「糊口线」。subsistence vs. survival：subsistence 强调最低维生；survival 可指险境后活下来

**coexist**
英/美 /ˌkoʊɪɡˈzɪst/ 英亦 /ˌkəʊɪɡˈzɪst/
v. 共存；并存
[义] To exist together in the same place or at the same time, often despite differences. 在同一时空并存（coexist peacefully 和平共存, old and new coexist 新旧并存）。强调同时存在、未必融合。
[形] coexisted / coexisted / coexisting
[例] ① Different faiths have coexisted in this city for centuries. 不同信仰在这座城市并存了数百年。 ② Traditional shops coexist with online retailers in many towns. 许多城镇里，传统店铺与网店并存。
[源] co-（共同）+ exist（存在）→ 共同存在。
[族] coexistence 共存, coexisting 共存的
[搭] coexist peacefully 和平共存, coexist with 与……并存, learn to coexist 学会共存, coexist side by side 并肩共存
[联] exist 存在, harmony 和谐, conflict 冲突, coexistence 共存, tolerate 容忍, merge 融合, separate 分离, parallel 并行的, side by side 并肩的, clash 冲突
[记] coexist with 表「与……并存」。不说 × coexist together（together 冗余）。coexist vs. merge：coexist 是并存，未必融合

**packed**
英/美 /pækt/
adj. 挤满的；塞满的
[义] Completely full of people or things; compressed into a container. 人满或物满（a packed train 挤满的列车, packed lunch 自带午餐）；紧密填装（packed soil 压实土壤）。与 full / crowded 近义，packed 更强调「挤得满满当当」。
[例] ① The stadium was packed for the championship final. 冠军决赛时体育场座无虚席。 ② She handed me a packed lunch for the long journey. 她递给我一份便当，好应付长途旅行。
[搭] packed with 满是, packed train/room 挤满的列车/房间, packed lunch 自带午餐, packed house 满座
[联] full 满的, crowded 拥挤的, loaded 满载的, empty 空的, vacant 空的, dense 密集的, sparse 稀疏的, jammed 塞满的
[记] packed with 表「满是……」。packed lunch 英式「自带午餐」。packed vs. full：packed 带「挤、塞」画面感

**loaded**
英/美 /ˈloʊdɪd/ 英 /ˈləʊdɪd/
adj. 满载的；上膛的；阔绰的（俚）
[义] Carrying a load; filled with something; (slang) wealthy. 装载货物或充满某种东西（fully loaded 满载, loaded with debt 负债累累）；俚语指「很有钱」。load 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The truck was fully loaded and ready to leave the warehouse. 卡车已满载，准备驶离仓库。 ② The question was loaded and impossible to answer fairly. 这个问题带有预设陷阱，无法公正作答。
[搭] fully loaded 满载, loaded with 充满, loaded question 带陷阱的问题, heavily loaded 重负的
[联] full 满的, packed 挤满的, empty 空的, unloaded 未装载的, burdened 负担沉重的, armed 武装的, wealthy 富有的
[记] loaded question 指「暗含偏见、逼对方入套的问题」。fully loaded 可指「满载」或软件「全功能版」。loaded vs. packed：loaded 偏「承载重量/负担」

**unloaded**
英/美 /ʌnˈloʊdɪd/ 英 /ʌnˈləʊdɪd/
adj. 未装载的；卸空的
[义] Not carrying a load; emptied of cargo or contents. 车辆、容器等未装货或已卸空（an unloaded truck 空车, unloaded gun 未上膛的枪）。与 loaded 相对；unload 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The truck returned to the depot unloaded after the morning delivery. 上午送完货后，卡车空载返回仓库。 ② Never point an unloaded gun at anyone—even empty weapons need careful handling. 切勿用未上膛的枪对准任何人——即便空枪也须谨慎对待。
[搭] unloaded truck/vessel 空载卡车/船只, unloaded gun 未上膛的枪, return unloaded 空载返回, fully unloaded 完全卸空的
[联] loaded 满载的, empty 空的, vacant 空的, packed 挤满的, bare 赤裸的/空的, full 满的, cargo 货物, freight 货运
[记] unloaded gun 在射击语境指「未装弹/未上膛」。unloaded vs. empty：unloaded 专指「卸货后/未装载」；empty 更宽，可指任何空容器

**inert**
英/美 /ɪnˈɜːrt/ 英 /ɪˈnɜːt/
adj. 惰性的；不活泼的；无反应的
[义] Unable to move or act; chemically unreactive; lacking energy or initiative. 物理上不动、无活性（inert gas 惰性气体）；人缺乏反应、 sluggish（He seemed inert after the long flight 长途飞行后他显得没精神）。比 inactive 更书面、可指化学惰性。
[例] ① Argon and other inert gases do not react easily with other substances. 氩等惰性气体不易与其他物质反应。 ② After the accident, he lay inert on the pavement until help arrived. 事故发生后，他在路面上一动不动，直到救援赶到。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *inertem*（无技艺、无活力），*in-*（不）+ *ars*（技艺）→ 缺乏活动力 → 惰性的。
[族] inertia 惯性, inertness 惰性
[搭] inert gas 惰性气体, inert material 惰性材料, chemically inert 化学惰性的, lie inert 一动不动地躺着
[联] inactive 不活跃的, dormant 休眠的, static 静态的, sluggish 迟缓的, reactive 有反应的, mobile 可动的, energy 能量, passive 被动的
[记] inert gas 化学课高频。inert vs. inactive：inert 可指化学「不反应」或人「完全没动静」；inactive 更宽，指「暂不活动」

**jammed**
英/美 /dʒæmd/
adj. 卡住的；塞满的；堵塞的
[义] Stuck and unable to move; filled so tightly that nothing more can pass. 机械卡住（The drawer is jammed 抽屉卡住了）；交通或通道堵塞（jammed traffic 交通堵塞）；挤满（The hall was jammed with fans 大厅挤满粉丝）。jam 的过去分词作形容词，口语极频。
[例] ① The photocopier is jammed again—can someone clear the paper tray? 复印机又卡纸了——谁能清理一下纸盘？ ② Phone lines were jammed for hours after the announcement. 公告发布后，电话线路堵塞了数小时。
[搭] jammed traffic 交通堵塞, jammed with 挤满, jammed drawer/door 卡住的抽屉/门, paper jammed 卡纸
[联] stuck 卡住的, trapped 困住的, packed 挤满的, blocked 堵塞的, crowded 拥挤的, free 自由的, clear 畅通的, congested 拥堵的
[记] get jammed / be jammed 均常见。jammed with 表「挤满……」。jammed vs. stuck：jammed 更绑「塞满、通道堵」；stuck 更口语通用

**trapped**
英/美 /træpt/
adj. 被困住的；陷入……的
[义] Unable to escape from a place or situation. 物理上被困（trapped in the elevator 困在电梯里）；抽象上陷入（feel trapped in a dead-end job 觉得困在无出路的工作里）。trap 的过去分词作形容词，比 stuck 更强调「无法脱身」。
[例] ① Rescuers worked through the night to reach people trapped under the rubble. 救援人员彻夜搜救困在废墟下的人。 ② She felt trapped in a marriage that had lost all warmth. 她觉得自己困在一段早已失去温度的婚姻里。
[搭] trapped in/under 困在……里/……下, feel trapped 感到被困住, trapped by debt 被债务困住, trapped air 滞留的空气
[联] stuck 卡住的, confined 受困的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, free 自由的, escape 逃脱, rescue 救援, cornered 被逼到绝路的, ensnared 落入圈套的
[记] trapped 常接 in / under / by。feel trapped 抽象用法极频。trapped vs. stuck：trapped 强调「出不去、被围住」；stuck 可指轻微卡住

**operational**
英/美 /ˌɑːpəˈreɪʃənl/ 英 /ˌɒpəˈreɪʃənl/
adj. 可运行的；在运作的；作战用的
[义] Ready and able to function; in working order; relating to military or business operations. 系统、设备、机构处于可运行状态（fully operational 全面运转, become operational 投入运行）；亦指作战/业务层面（operational costs 运营成本）。比 working 正式，常见于技术、军事、管理语境。
[例] ① The new hospital wing will become fully operational next month. 新住院楼将于下月全面投入使用。 ② All backup systems are operational after the upgrade. 升级后所有备份系统均可正常运行。
[族] operate 操作, operation 运作, operator 操作员, operationalize 使可运作
[搭] fully operational 全面运转, become operational 投入运行, operational status 运行状态, operational costs 运营成本, non-operational 未运行的
[联] functional 正常运作的, working 在工作的, active 活跃的, defunct 已停用的, inactive 未激活的, online 在线的, offline 离线的, ready 就绪的
[记] operational 多修饰 system, facility, base, costs。non-operational 表「未运行、停用」。operational vs. functional：operational 偏「已投入运行、可执行任务」；functional 偏「功能正常」

**functional**
英/美 /ˈfʌŋkʃənl/
adj. 正常运作的；实用的；功能的
[义] Working correctly; designed for practical use rather than decoration. 设备、系统功能正常（The app is fully functional 应用功能完备）；设计重实用（functional clothing 功能性服装）。与 decorative 相对；比 operational 更绑「功能是否完好」。
[例] ① The website is functional again after engineers fixed the server error. 工程师修复服务器错误后，网站恢复可用。 ② They chose simple, functional furniture for the small apartment. 他们为小公寓选了简洁实用的家具。
[族] function 功能, functionalize 使功能化, dysfunction 功能障碍, malfunction 故障
[搭] fully functional 功能完备的, functional design 实用设计, remain functional 保持可用, non-functional 无法正常运作的
[联] operational 在运作的, working 在工作的, usable 可用的, broken 坏的, defective 有缺陷的, practical 实用的, decorative 装饰性的, intact 完好的
[记] fully functional 技术语境极频。functional vs. operational：functional 问「能不能用」；operational 问「是否在运行/执行任务」

**slack**
英/美 /slæk/
adj. 松弛的；不紧的；萧条的
n. 松弛部分；淡季（略）
[义] Not tight or tense; loose; (of business) slow and inactive. 绳子、肌肉等松弛（slack rope 松绳, slack season 淡季）；抽象上松懈（slack safety standards 松懈的安全标准）。名词指「松弛的一段」或「淡季」。与 taut / tight 相对。
[例] ① Check the rope—it looks slack and may not hold the load. 检查一下绳子——看起来松了，可能挂不住货。 ② Business is slack in January after the holiday rush. 假日高峰过后，一月生意清淡。
[搭] slack off 松懈, slack season 淡季, slack rope/chain 松绳/松链, cut sb. some slack 对某人宽容些（口语）, hold slack 保持松弛
[联] loose 松的, tight 紧的, taut 拉紧的, limp 无力的, tense 紧张的, rigid 僵硬的, sluggish 萧条的, active 活跃的
[记] cut sb. some slack 口语「别对某人太苛刻」。slack off 表「松懈、偷懒」。slack vs. loose：slack 常指「本该拉紧却松了」；loose 更宽

**still**
英/美 /stɪl/
adj. 静止的；不动的
adv. 仍然；还（另义）
[义] Not moving; calm and quiet. 身体或物体不动（stand still 站着别动, hold still 别动）；水面、空气等平静（still water 静水, the still of the night 夜的寂静）。副词 still 表「仍然」是另一高频义，本词条侧重形容词「静止」。
[例] ① The doctor asked the child to hold still while she listened to his chest. 医生让孩子别动，好听诊胸部。 ② Fish are easier to spot in still water than in a rushing stream. 静水里比急流中更容易看见鱼。
[搭] stand/hold/sit still 站着/别/坐着不动, still water/air 静水/静空气, keep still 保持不动, perfectly still 完全静止
[联] motionless 不动的, static 静态的, calm 平静的, restless 坐不住的, moving 移动的, quiet 安静的, frozen 冻结的, active 活跃的
[记] hold still 拍照、医疗检查极频。still vs. motionless：still 较日常；motionless 更书面、强调「毫无移动」。still 作副词「仍然」见时间/副词用法，与本 adj. 同形

**restless**
英/美 /ˈrestləs/
adj. 坐不住的；睡不安稳的；不安宁的
[义] Unable to relax or stay still; showing lack of rest or sleep. 身体坐不住（a restless child 坐不住的孩子）；心里不宁（restless nights 辗转难眠, restless energy 闲不下来的劲）。与 still / calm 相对，可指人也可指局势（restless crowd 躁动的人群）。
[形] 比较级 more restless；最高级 most restless
[例] ① The baby was restless all night and woke every hour. 宝宝整夜睡不安稳，每小时醒一次。 ② After three days indoors, she grew restless and needed to get out. 在屋里待了三天后，她开始坐不住，想出门走走。
[搭] restless night 辗转难眠的一夜, restless sleep 睡不安稳, feel restless 感到坐不住, restless crowd 躁动的人群, restless energy 闲不住的劲
[联] still 静止的, calm 平静的, agitated 焦躁的, anxious 焦虑的, sleepless 失眠的, fidgety 坐不住的, settled 安定的, uneasy 不安的
[记] restless nights 常指「辗转反侧」。restless vs. agitated：restless 偏「静不下来、想动」；agitated 偏「激动、焦躁、情绪上扬」

**exposed**
英/美 /ɪkˈspoʊzd/ 英 /ɪkˈspəʊzd/
adj. 暴露的；无遮蔽的；易受伤害的
[义] Not protected from harm, weather, or view; left without cover. 无遮蔽（exposed skin 裸露的皮肤, exposed wiring 裸露的线路）；易受攻击或伤害（exposed position 暴露的位置, feel exposed 感到毫无掩护）。expose 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Hikers should cover exposed skin to avoid sunburn at high altitude. 徒步者应注意遮蔽裸露皮肤，以免高海拔晒伤。 ② The scandal left the minister politically exposed. 丑闻使这位部长在政治上十分被动、处境堪忧。
[族] expose 暴露, exposure 暴露, unexposed 未暴露的
[搭] exposed to 暴露于, exposed skin/wiring 裸露的皮肤/线路, feel exposed 感到暴露无遗, exposed position 暴露的位置
[联] uncovered 未遮盖的, vulnerable 脆弱的, protected 受保护的, sheltered 有遮蔽的, naked 裸露的, hidden 隐藏的, sealed 密封的, isolated 孤立的
[记] exposed to 表「暴露于（风险、元素）」：exposed to radiation 受辐射。exposed vs. naked：exposed 可指「未受保护」；naked 更指「没穿衣服」

**isolated**
英/美 /ˈaɪsəleɪtɪd/
adj. 孤立的；偏远的；单独的
[义] Far from others; separate; occurring alone. 地理上偏远、与世隔绝（an isolated village 偏远的村庄）；人或系统被隔开（feel isolated 感到孤立, isolated case 个别案例）。isolate 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The storm cut off power to several isolated communities in the mountains. 暴风雨切断了山区几个偏远社区的电力。 ② She felt isolated working from home without daily contact with colleagues. 居家办公、缺少日常同事交流，她感到孤立。
[族] isolate 隔离, isolation 隔离, isolating 使孤立的
[搭] isolated case/incident 个别案例/事件, feel isolated 感到孤立, remote and isolated 偏远隔绝的, socially isolated 社交孤立的
[联] alone 单独的, lonely 孤独的, remote 偏远的, connected 相连的, exposed 暴露的, separate 分离的, clustered 成群的, integrated 一体化的
[记] isolated case 指「个案、非普遍现象」。isolated vs. lonely：isolated 强调「被隔开的状态」；lonely 强调「孤独感」

**sealed**
英/美 /siːld/
adj. 密封的；封存的；敲定的
[义] Closed tightly so that air, liquid, or information cannot enter or escape; officially closed. 容器密封（sealed container 密封容器）；文件封存（sealed envelope 封口的信封）；交易「敲定」（a sealed deal 已定局的交易）。seal 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Store the spices in a sealed jar to keep them fresh. 把香料放在密封罐里以保持新鲜。 ② The jury's deliberations remain sealed until the verdict is read. 在宣读裁决前，陪审团的评议内容保密。
[族] seal 密封, unsealed 未密封的, airtight 气密的
[搭] sealed container/envelope 密封容器/封口的信封, sealed bid 密封投标, sealed off 封锁, hermetically sealed 完全密封的
[联] closed 关闭的, open 开着的, exposed 暴露的, airtight 气密的, locked 锁住的, confidential 保密的, intact 完好的, broken 破损的
[记] sealed bid 指「密封投标、开标前保密」。sealed off 表「封锁区域」。sealed vs. closed：sealed 强调「封死、不漏」；closed 仅指「关着」

**intermittent**
英/美 /ˌɪntərˈmɪtənt/
adj. 间歇的；断断续续的
[义] Starting and stopping at irregular intervals; not continuous. 时有时无、非持续（intermittent rain 阵雨, intermittent pain 间歇性疼痛, intermittent fasting 间歇性禁食）。比 occasional 更强调「停—起—停」的节奏。
[例] ① Intermittent showers made it hard to plan outdoor activities. 阵雨时断时续，户外安排很难定。 ② He suffered from intermittent back pain for years before seeing a specialist. 他多年间歇性背痛，直到看了专科医生。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *intermittere*（中途停下），*inter-*（之间）+ *mittere*（送）→ 断断续续。
[族] intermittently 间歇地, intermittence 间歇
[搭] intermittent rain/pain 阵雨/间歇性疼痛, intermittent fasting 间歇性禁食, intermittent supply 断续供应, intermittent fault 间歇性故障
[联] continuous 连续的, constant 不断的, sporadic 零星的, occasional 偶尔的, periodic 周期性的, steady 稳定的, ongoing 进行中的, irregular 不规则的
[记] intermittent fasting 近年健康话题高频。intermittent vs. sporadic：intermittent 有「停—起—停」节奏；sporadic 偏「零星、无规律出现」

**extant**
英/美 /ekˈstænt/ 亦 /ˈekstənt/
adj. 现存的；尚存的
[义] Still in existence; not lost or destroyed. 文献、物种、建筑等至今仍存在（the oldest extant manuscript 现存最早的手稿, extant species 现存物种）。正式、学术语体；与 extinct（灭绝）相对。
[例] ① This is the only extant copy of the poem from that period. 这是该时期这首诗唯一现存的副本。 ② Few extant records describe daily life in the settlement. 描述该定居点日常生活的现存记录很少。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *exstare*（站出来、存在），*ex-*（出）+ *stare*（站立）→ 仍然立着 → 现存的。
[联] existing 现存的, surviving 幸存的, extinct 灭绝的, lost 遗失的, ancient 古老的, manuscript 手稿, record 记录, present-day 当今的
[记] extant 多修饰 copy, manuscript, species, records。extant vs. existing：extant 更书面、常绑「历经时间仍留存」；existing 更日常

**defunct**
英/美 /dɪˈfʌŋkt/
adj. 已不存在的；已停业的；失效的
[义] No longer existing or functioning; dead or obsolete. 机构、品牌、法律等已停止运作（a defunct company 已倒闭的公司, defunct law 已失效的法律）。比 dead 更正式，常用于商业、法律、历史语境。
[例] ① The website redirects to the homepage of a defunct startup. 该网站跳转至一家已倒闭初创公司的主页。 ② Several defunct regulations still appear in the old handbook. 旧手册里仍列着几条已失效的规定。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *defunctus*（已完成、已死），*de-* + *fungi*（履行）→ 履行完毕 → 不再存在。
[联] extinct 灭绝的, obsolete 过时的, dead 死的, operational 在运作的, active 活跃的, dissolved 已解散的, bankrupt 破产的, expired 已过期的
[记] defunct company/brand/law 为常见搭配。defunct vs. obsolete：defunct 指「已停止存在/运作」；obsolete 指「过时但仍可能存在」

**viable**
英/美 /ˈvaɪəbl/
adj. 可行的；能存活的；能发育的
[义] Capable of working successfully; able to survive or develop. 计划、方案可行（a viable option 可行选项）；生物能存活或发育（viable fetus 可成活的胎儿）；企业能持续经营（economically viable 经济上可行）。比 possible 更强调「真能成、能活」。
[例] ① Solar power is now a viable alternative in many sunny regions. 在许多日照充足地区，太阳能已是可行替代方案。 ② The clinic only transfers viable embryos during the procedure. 诊所只在手术中移植可发育的胚胎。
[源] 源自法语 *viable*，*vie*（生命）→ 能活的 → 可行的。
[族] viability 可行性, viably 可行地, nonviable 不可行的
[搭] economically viable 经济上可行的, viable option/alternative 可行选项/替代方案, viable candidate 有胜算的人选, remain viable 保持可行
[联] feasible 可行的, possible 可能的, workable 可运作的, practical 实际的, unviable 不可行的, survival 生存, option 选项, sustainable 可持续的
[记] economically viable 商业计划书高频。viable vs. feasible：viable 更绑「能存活、能持续」；feasible 偏「做得到」

**precarious**
英/美 /prɪˈkeriəs/ 英 /prɪˈkeəriəs/
adj. 不稳固的；危险的；不确定的
[义] Not securely held or balanced; dependent on uncertain conditions. 物理上摇摇欲坠（a precarious ladder 不稳的梯子）；生计、地位不稳（precarious employment 不稳定就业, precarious balance 岌岌可危的平衡）。比 shaky 更书面、常带「随时可能垮」的危机感。
[例] ① The hut stood on a precarious ledge above the cliff. 小屋建在悬崖上方一块不稳固的岩台上。 ② Millions rely on precarious gig work with no benefits. 数百万人靠没有福利的零工维生，处境不稳。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *precarius*（靠请求得来的），*prex*（请求）→ 非自有、靠他人 → 不稳固的。
[族] precariously 不稳固地, precariousness 不稳固
[搭] precarious balance 岌岌可危的平衡, precarious employment 不稳定就业, precarious position 不稳的处境, live in precarious circumstances 生活无保障
[联] shaky 摇晃的, unstable 不稳定的, secure 稳固的, steady 平稳的, risky 有风险的, fragile 脆弱的, tentative 试探性的, solid 牢固的
[记] precarious employment 指「非正式、无保障的工作」。precarious vs. shaky：precarious 更书面、常绑生计与结构；shaky 更口语

**wobbly**
英/美 /ˈwɑːbli/ 英 /ˈwɒbli/
adj. 摇晃的；不稳的
[义] Moving unsteadily from side to side; not firm or secure. 物体物理上晃（a wobbly table 晃动的桌子, wobbly legs 发软的双腿）；抽象上不稳（a wobbly voice 颤抖的声音, the economy looks wobbly 经济前景不稳）。比 shaky 更带「左右晃、晃荡」的画面感，口语常见。
[形] 比较级 wobblier；最高级 wobbliest
[例] ① Put a folded napkin under the leg—the table is wobbly. 桌腿下垫张餐巾纸——桌子在晃。 ② She gave a wobbly smile and tried to hide how nervous she felt. 她勉强笑了笑，想掩饰有多紧张。
[搭] wobbly table/chair 晃动的桌子/椅子, feel wobbly 感到发软/不稳, wobbly voice 发颤的声音, go wobbly 开始摇晃
[联] shaky 摇晃的, unsteady 不稳的, steady 平稳的, stable 稳定的, limp 无力的, firm 牢固的, trembling 颤抖的, secure 稳固的
[记] go wobbly 英式口语可指「腿发软、快要撑不住」。wobbly vs. shaky：wobbly 偏「左右晃」；shaky 可指证据、开局等抽象「站不住脚」

**sporadic**
英/美 /spəˈrædɪk/
adj. 零星的；偶发的；无规律的
[义] Happening only occasionally or at irregular intervals; scattered. 出现无固定节奏、时有时无（sporadic violence 零星暴力, sporadic attendance 出勤时断时续）。比 intermittent 更强调「不规律、分散」，未必有清晰的停—起—停周期。
[例] ① Sporadic gunfire could be heard through the night. 整夜不时传来零星枪声。 ② She made sporadic attempts to learn the language but never stuck with it. 她偶尔想学这门语言，但从未坚持。
[源] 源自希腊语 *sporadikos*（分散的），*sporad-*（散布）→ 零星散布 → 偶发的。
[族] sporadically 零星地
[搭] sporadic violence/outbreaks 零星暴力/爆发, sporadic attendance 出勤时断时续, sporadic attempts 偶尔尝试, remain sporadic 仍然零星
[联] intermittent 间歇的, occasional 偶尔的, frequent 频繁的, regular 有规律的, scattered 分散的, continuous 连续的, rare 罕见的, constant 不断的
[记] sporadic vs. intermittent：sporadic 偏「零星、无规律」；intermittent 偏「停—起—停」有节奏。sporadic outbreaks 新闻常见

**inoperable**
英/美 /ɪnˈɑːpərəbl/ 英 /ɪnˈɒpərəbl/
adj. 无法运作的；不能手术的
[义] Not able to function or be used; (medicine) not treatable by surgery. 设备、系统无法运行（inoperable equipment 无法使用的设备）；医学上指「不能动手术」（an inoperable tumour 无法手术切除的肿瘤）。与 operational / functional 相对。
[例] ① The backup generator was inoperable after years without maintenance. 多年未维护后，备用发电机已无法运行。 ② Doctors said the tumour was inoperable because of its location. 医生说肿瘤位置特殊，无法手术切除。
[搭] inoperable equipment 无法使用的设备, inoperable tumour 无法手术的肿瘤, render inoperable 使无法运作, temporarily inoperable 暂时无法使用
[联] operational 可运行的, functional 正常运作的, broken 坏的, defective 有缺陷的, usable 可用的, working 在工作的, surgical 手术的, treatable 可治疗的
[记] inoperable 医学语境指「不能手术」；技术语境指「不能运行」。inoperable vs. broken：inoperable 更正式、可指「设计上无法启用」

**submerged**
英/美 /səbˈmɜːrdʒd/ 英 /səbˈmɜːdʒd/
adj. 淹没的；水下的
[义] Under the surface of water; hidden beneath something. 被水淹没（submerged village 被淹的村庄, submerged rocks 水下礁石）；比喻被掩盖（submerged feelings 压抑的情感）。submerge 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Divers explored the submerged wreck off the coast. 潜水员探查了近海的水下沉船。 ② Much of the coastline was submerged after the storm surge. 风暴潮过后，大片海岸线被淹没。
[族] submerge 淹没, submersion 淹没
[搭] submerged rocks/village 水下礁石/被淹村庄, partially submerged 部分淹没, remain submerged 仍在水下, submerged feelings 压抑的情感
[联] underwater 水下的, immersed 浸没的, flooded 被淹的, afloat 漂浮的, exposed 暴露的, dry 干燥的, sunken 沉没的, engulfed 吞没的
[记] submerged 强调「在水面以下」。submerged vs. immersed：submerged 专指「被水淹/在水下」；immersed 可指液体浸没或「沉浸于」抽象事物

**immersed**
英/美 /ɪˈmɜːrst/ 英 /ɪˈmɜːst/
adj. 浸没的；沉浸于……的
[义] Put deeply into a liquid; completely involved in something. 物理上浸在液体里（immersed in water 浸在水中）；抽象上全神贯注（immersed in work 埋头工作, immersed in a book 沉浸书中）。immerse 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Keep the sensor immersed in the solution for ten minutes. 将传感器浸在溶液中十分钟。 ② She was so immersed in the novel that she missed her stop. 她读小说太入神，坐过了站。
[族] immerse 浸没, immersion 沉浸
[搭] immersed in 沉浸于/浸在, fully immersed 完全浸没, deeply immersed 深度沉浸, remain immersed 保持浸没状态
[联] submerged 淹没的, absorbed 全神贯注的, engaged 投入的, distracted 分心的, soaked 浸湿的, dry 干燥的, focused 专注的, detached 抽离的
[记] be immersed in 抽象义极频：immersed in thought 陷入沉思。immersed vs. absorbed：immersed 可指物理浸没；absorbed 更偏「注意力被吸走」

**engulfed**
英/美 /ɪnˈɡʌlft/ 英 /ɪnˈɡʌlft/
adj. 被吞没的；被包围的
[义] Completely surrounded or covered by something, especially something overwhelming. 被火焰、洪水、黑暗等完全包围（engulfed in flames 陷入火海）；抽象上被情绪或事务淹没（engulfed in grief 悲痛欲绝, engulfed in work 被工作吞没）。engulf 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Within minutes the warehouse was engulfed in flames. 几分钟内仓库便陷入火海。 ② He felt engulfed by doubt after the failed launch. 产品发布后失败，他疑云密布。
[族] engulf 吞没, engulfment 吞没
[搭] engulfed in flames/smoke/darkness 陷入火海/浓烟/黑暗, engulfed by grief/doubt 被悲痛/疑虑吞没, completely engulfed 完全被吞没
[联] surrounded 被包围的, submerged 淹没的, consumed 被吞噬的, overwhelmed 被压垮的, trapped 困住的, free 自由的, exposed 暴露的, isolated 孤立的
[记] engulfed in/by 为固定搭配。engulfed vs. submerged：engulfed 强调「从四面八方吞没」；submerged 专指「在水下」

**tenuous**
英/美 /ˈtenjuəs/ 美 /ˈtenjuəs/
adj. 纤细的；脆弱的；站不住脚的
[义] Very weak or slight; thin; unlikely to last or be valid. 联系、证据、存在「薄、弱」（a tenuous link 牵强的联系, tenuous existence 朝不保夕的存在）；物理上极细（tenuous thread 细线）。比 weak 更书面，常带「随时可能断/垮」感。
[例] ① The case rests on a tenuous connection between the two events. 此案建立在两事件之间牵强的关联上。 ② After the layoffs, many staff lived in tenuous employment. 裁员后，许多员工工作朝不保夕。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *tenuis*（细、薄）→ 纤细 → 脆弱的。
[族] tenuously 脆弱地, tenuousness 脆弱
[搭] tenuous link/connection 牵强的联系, tenuous existence 朝不保夕的存在, tenuous hold 岌岌可危的掌控, remain tenuous 仍然脆弱
[联] weak 弱的, fragile 脆弱的, precarious 不稳固的, solid 牢固的, strong 强的, thin 薄的, dubious 可疑的, firm 坚定的
[记] tenuous link/connection 学术与新闻常见。tenuous vs. weak：tenuous 更书面，常绑「关系、证据、地位」的脆弱

**dysfunctional**
英/美 /dɪsˈfʌŋkʃənl/
adj. 功能失调的；不正常的
[义] Not operating normally; characterized by unhealthy or impaired functioning. 系统、机构运作失常（dysfunctional system 失灵的系统）；家庭、团队关系 unhealthy（dysfunctional family 功能失调的家庭）。比 broken 更绑「整体机制出问题」。
[例] ① Outdated software created a dysfunctional workflow across departments. 过时软件导致各部门流程失灵。 ② She grew up in a dysfunctional household but built a stable life as an adult. 她在问题家庭长大，成年后却建立了稳定生活。
[族] dysfunction 功能障碍, dysfunctionally 功能失调地
[搭] dysfunctional family/system 功能失调的家庭/系统, dysfunctional relationship 不健康的关系, become dysfunctional 变得失灵, deeply dysfunctional 严重失调
[联] broken 坏的, functional 正常运作的, healthy 健康的, normal 正常的, impaired 受损的, operational 在运作的, toxic 有毒的, stable 稳定的
[记] dysfunctional family 心理与社会学高频。dysfunctional vs. broken：dysfunctional 指「还能运转但方式有害/低效」；broken 指「坏了不能用」

**malfunctioning**
英/美 /ˌmælˈfʌŋkʃənɪŋ/
adj. 发生故障的；失灵中的
[义] Failing to work correctly. 设备、器官等正在出故障（a malfunctioning printer 故障打印机, malfunctioning heart valve 失灵的心脏瓣膜）。malfunction 的现在分词作形容词，比 broken 更指「功能层面出错、未必完全报废」。
[例] ① Passengers were stuck when the malfunctioning doors refused to open. 门出故障打不开，乘客被困。 ② The report traced the crash to a malfunctioning sensor. 报告将坠机归因于传感器失灵。
[族] malfunction 故障, malfunctioning 故障的
[搭] malfunctioning equipment 故障设备, malfunctioning sensor/valve 失灵的传感器/瓣膜, temporarily malfunctioning 暂时失灵
[联] broken 坏的, faulty 有故障的, defective 有缺陷的, functional 正常运作的, working 在工作的, operational 可运行的, repair 修理, failure 故障
[记] malfunctioning 强调「正在出功能故障」。malfunctioning vs. broken：malfunctioning 可能修好后恢复；broken 更指「坏了」

**offline**
英/美 /ˌɔːfˈlaɪn/ 英 /ˌɒfˈlaɪn/
adj. 离线的；未联网的
adv. 离线地
[义] Not connected to the internet or a network; not available on a system. 设备、服务未联网（go offline 断线, offline mode 离线模式）；亦指「线下、非线上」（offline meeting 线下会议）。与 online 相对，数字时代极频。
[例] ① The app lets you download maps for offline use while travelling. 这款应用可下载地图，旅行时离线使用。 ② The payment system went offline for scheduled maintenance. 支付系统因计划维护暂时下线。
[搭] go offline 断线/下线, offline mode 离线模式, work offline 离线工作, offline meeting 线下会议, temporarily offline 暂时离线
[联] online 在线的, connected 已连接的, disconnected 断开的, unavailable 不可用的, available 可用的, digital 数字的, network 网络, server 服务器
[记] go offline 可指设备断网，也可指人「下线、不在线」。offline vs. disconnected：offline 偏「系统/服务未上线」；disconnected 偏「连接被切断」

**online**
英/美 /ˌɑːnˈlaɪn/ 英 /ˌɒnˈlaɪn/
adj. 在线的；联网的
adv. 在线地
