[义] Connected to the internet or a network; available on a digital platform. 设备、服务已联网（stay online 保持在线, online banking 网上银行）；活动在线上进行（online course 网课）。与 offline 相对。
[例] ① The store moved most of its sales online during the pandemic. 疫情期间，该店大部分销售转到线上。 ② Make sure your phone stays online during the video call. 视频通话期间请保持手机联网。
[搭] go/stay online 上线/保持在线, online shopping/banking 网购/网银, online course 网课, bring online 使上线
[联] offline 离线的, connected 已连接的, digital 数字的, virtual 虚拟的, available 可用的, website 网站, platform 平台, remote 远程的
[记] bring sth. online 指「使系统/服务上线」。online vs. virtual：online 强调「联网」；virtual 强调「非物理、数字化存在」

**usable**
英/美 /ˈjuːzəbl/
adj. 可用的；能用的
[义] In good enough condition to be used; capable of being used. 尚可使用、未报废（still usable 仍可用, barely usable 勉强能用）；界面、设计「好用」（user-usable interface 易用界面）。与 functional / operational 近义，usable 更绑「人能否拿来用」。
[例] ① The laptop is old but still usable for email and documents. 笔记本虽旧，写邮件、做文档仍够用。 ② Engineers declared the bridge safe and usable after repairs. 工程师宣布桥梁修好后安全可用。
[族] use 使用, usability 可用性, unusable 无法使用的
[搭] still usable 仍可用, barely usable 勉强能用, safe and usable 安全可用, highly usable 非常好用
[联] functional 正常运作的, operational 可运行的, broken 坏的, unusable 无法使用的, practical 实用的, available 可用的, defective 有缺陷的, handy 顺手的
[记] usability 指「可用性、易用性」，UX 领域高频。usable vs. functional：usable 问「能不能用」；functional 问「功能是否正常」

**taut**
英/美 /tɔːt/
adj. 拉紧的；绷紧的
[义] Stretched or pulled tight; tense and controlled. 绳子、肌肉、皮肤等绷紧（a taut rope 拉紧的绳, taut muscles 紧绷的肌肉）；抽象上紧凑、无赘余（a taut thriller 节奏紧凑的惊悚片）。与 slack / loose 相对。
[例] ① Hold the line taut while I tie the knot. 我打结时你把绳子拉紧。 ② Her face looked taut with worry before the results came out. 结果出来前，她一脸紧绷。
[搭] hold taut 拉紧, taut rope/wire 拉紧的绳/线, taut muscles 紧绷的肌肉, taut narrative 紧凑的叙事
[联] tight 紧的, slack 松弛的, loose 松的, tense 紧张的, limp 无力的, firm 牢固的, stretched 拉伸的, relaxed 放松的
[记] hold taut 固定说法。taut vs. tight：taut 常指「被拉紧到适度张力」；tight 更宽，可指「太紧、难以松动」

**rickety**
英/美 /ˈrɪkəti/
adj. 摇晃的；不结实的
[义] Not strong or well made; likely to break or collapse. 结构不牢、摇摇欲坠（a rickety chair 晃悠的椅子, rickety stairs 吱嘎作响的楼梯）。比 wobbly 更绑「老旧、快散架」，带「随时可能塌」的不安全感。
[例] ① They climbed the rickety wooden stairs to the attic. 他们踩着吱嘎作响的木楼梯上了阁楼。 ② The charity depends on a rickety funding model that could collapse any year. 这家慈善机构靠一套随时可能崩的资金模式维生。
[搭] rickety chair/stairs/ladder 摇晃的椅子/楼梯/梯子, rickety structure 不稳固的结构, rickety bridge 摇摇欲坠的桥
[联] wobbly 摇晃的, shaky 不稳的, sturdy 结实的, solid 牢固的, fragile 脆弱的, precarious 不稳固的, stable 稳定的, broken 坏的
[记] rickety 常形容老旧家具、建筑。rickety vs. wobbly：rickety 强调「结构不牢、快散架」；wobbly 偏「左右晃」

**obsolete**
英/美 /ˌɑːbsəˈliːt/ 英 /ˈɒbsəliːt/
adj. 过时的；淘汰的
[义] No longer produced or used; out of date. 技术、方法、设备已被取代（obsolete technology 过时技术, become obsolete 变得过时）。与 defunct 近义：obsolete 偏「被新版本取代仍可能存在」；defunct 偏「已停止运作/存在」。
[例] ① Many factories still run on obsolete equipment because upgrades cost too much. 许多工厂因升级太贵，仍在用过时设备。 ② The law has been obsolete for decades but was never formally repealed. 该法过时多年，却从未正式废止。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *obsolescere*（磨损、变旧），*ob-* + *solere*（习惯）→ 不再适用 → 过时的。
[族] obsolescence 过时, obsolescent 正在过时的
[搭] become obsolete 变得过时, obsolete technology/equipment 过时技术/设备, render obsolete 使过时, largely obsolete 大体已淘汰
[联] outdated 过时的, defunct 已停用的, modern 现代的, current 当前的, extinct 灭绝的, viable 可行的, legacy 遗留的, upgrade 升级
[记] planned obsolescence 指「计划性报废、故意做不耐用」。obsolete vs. defunct：obsolete 东西可能还在用；defunct 指机构/法律等已不存在

**nonexistent**
英/美 /ˌnɑːnɪɡˈzɪstənt/ 英 /ˌnɒnɪɡˈzɪstənt/
adj. 不存在的；没有的
[义] Not existing; not present or real. 某物根本没有（nonexistent budget 根本不存在的预算, safety standards were nonexistent 安全标准形同虚设）。比 absent 更强调「从未有过、等于零」。
[例] ① Public transport is virtually nonexistent in some rural areas. 有些农村地区几乎完全没有公共交通。 ② Customer support was nonexistent after we paid for the subscription. 付完订阅费后，客户支持形同虚设。
[搭] virtually nonexistent 几乎不存在, nonexistent budget/standards 不存在的预算/标准, remain nonexistent 仍然不存在, largely nonexistent 大体上没有
[联] existent 现存的, absent 缺席的, missing 缺失的, imaginary 想象的, real 真实的, present 在场的, available 可用的, lacking 缺乏的
[记] virtually nonexistent 极频。nonexistent vs. absent：nonexistent 指「根本没有这东西」；absent 指「该在却不在」

**virtual**
英/美 /ˈvɜːrtʃuəl/ 英 /ˈvɜːtʃuəl/
adj. 虚拟的；实质上的
[义] Existing in effect but not in physical form; almost or nearly. 数字/模拟意义上的存在（virtual meeting 虚拟会议, virtual reality 虚拟现实）；实质上等效（a virtual monopoly 事实上的垄断）。与 real / physical 相对。
[例] ① The team held a virtual meeting because members were spread across three countries. 成员分布三国，团队开了虚拟会议。 ② After the merger, the company became a virtual monopoly in the region. 合并后，该公司在该地区形成事实垄断。
[源] 源自中世纪拉丁语 *virtualis*，*virtus*（效力、美德）→ 有效力而无实体 → 虚拟的。
[族] virtually 几乎, virtuality 虚拟性, virtualization 虚拟化
[搭] virtual meeting/reality 虚拟会议/虚拟现实, virtual office 虚拟办公室, virtual monopoly 事实垄断, in virtual format 以虚拟形式
[联] real 真实的, physical 物理的, online 在线的, digital 数字的, actual 实际的, imaginary 想象的, remote 远程的, simulated 模拟的
[记] virtually 副词义「几乎、差不多」：It's virtually impossible 几乎不可能。virtual vs. online：virtual 可指 VR 或「实质上的」；online 专指联网

**closed**
英/美 /kloʊzd/ 英 /kləʊzd/
adj. 关闭的；不开放的；结束的
[义] Not open; shut; no longer accepting new members or cases. 门、店、路关闭（The museum is closed on Mondays 博物馆周一闭馆）；系统、讨论不再开放（a closed group 封闭群组, closed case 已结案件）。close 的过去分词作形容词；open 见行为章，本章收「关闭状态」。
[例] ① The border remained closed to tourists for another month. 边境对游客再关闭一个月。 ② She prefers a closed workspace where she can focus without interruptions. 她更喜欢封闭工位，以免被打扰。
[搭] closed for repairs 因维修关闭, closed group/system 封闭群组/系统, closed case 已结案件, closed session 闭门会议
[联] open 开着的, shut 关着的, sealed 密封的, locked 锁住的, available 可用的, accessible 可进入的, restricted 受限制的, active 活跃的
[记] closed vs. shut：closed 可指「不营业、不开放」；shut 更口语、偏「物理关上」。closed book 比喻「摸不透的人/事」

**locked**
英/美 /lɑːkt/ 英 /lɒkt/
adj. 锁上的；锁定的
[义] Fastened with a lock; fixed in position and unable to move. 物理上锁（The door is locked 门锁着）；数据、设置被锁定（locked file 锁定文件, locked position 固定位置）。lock 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① I found the gate locked when I arrived early in the morning. 一大早到时，我发现大门锁着。 ② The phone was locked to a single network provider. 这部手机被锁定在单一运营商网络。
[族] lock 锁, unlock 解锁, unlocked 未锁的
[搭] locked door/gate 锁上的门/大门, locked file/account 锁定文件/账户, locked in place 固定到位, keep locked 保持上锁
[联] unlocked 未锁的, sealed 密封的, closed 关闭的, open 开着的, secure 安全的, trapped 困住的, fixed 固定的, accessible 可进入的
[记] locked in 可指「被困在里面」或「锁定在某种状态」。locked vs. sealed：locked 靠锁；sealed 强调「封死、不漏」

**disconnected**
英/美 /ˌdɪskəˈnektɪd/
adj. 断开的；脱节的
[义] Not connected; lacking logical connection or coherence. 物理或网络断开（disconnected phone 断线的电话）；抽象上脱节（disconnected ideas 互不关联的想法, feel disconnected 感到与世隔绝）。disconnect 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The call dropped and the line went disconnected. 通话中断，线路断开。 ② His speech felt disconnected, jumping from topic to topic without a clear thread. 他的演讲东一榔头西一棒子，缺乏主线。
[族] disconnect 断开, disconnection 断开, connected 已连接的
[搭] feel disconnected 感到脱节/孤立, disconnected from 与……断开, disconnected ideas 脱节的想法, go disconnected 断线
[联] connected 已连接的, offline 离线的, isolated 孤立的, incoherent 不连贯的, linked 相连的, integrated 一体化的, separate 分离的, online 在线的
[记] disconnected from reality 指「与现实脱节」。disconnected vs. isolated：disconnected 可指「逻辑/网络断线」；isolated 偏「被隔开、孤立」

**unbalanced**
英/美 /ʌnˈbælənst/
adj. 不平衡的；失衡的
[义] Not evenly distributed; mentally or emotionally unstable (informal). 物理或结构失衡（an unbalanced load 载荷不均）；饮食、预算等比例失调（unbalanced diet 不均衡饮食）；口语指「精神不太正常」（informal）。与 balanced / equilibrium 相对。
[例] ① An unbalanced load can make the washing machine shake violently. 衣物分布不均，洗衣机会剧烈晃动。 ② The report warned that the economy remained unbalanced, with too much reliance on exports. 报告警告经济仍失衡，过度依赖出口。
[族] balance 平衡, unbalance 使失衡, balanced 平衡的
[搭] unbalanced load/diet 不均的载荷/不均衡饮食, mentally unbalanced 精神失常（口语）, remain unbalanced 仍然失衡, economically unbalanced 经济失衡
[联] balanced 平衡的, unstable 不稳定的, equilibrium 均衡, lopsided 一边倒的, even 均匀的, steady 平稳的, skewed 偏斜的, stable 稳定的
[记] unbalanced diet 健康话题高频。unbalanced vs. unstable：unbalanced 指「比例/分布失调」；unstable 更宽，可指「整体不稳」

**shut**
英/美 /ʃʌt/
adj. 关着的；关闭的
v. 关上；关闭
[义] Not open; closed, especially by moving a door, lid, or window. 门、窗、盖等关着（The door is shut 门关着, keep your eyes shut 闭着眼睛）。作形容词极口语；closed 更正式、可指「不营业」。 shut 的过去分词与形容词同形。
[形] shut / shut / shutting
[例] ① I tried the handle, but the door was shut and locked. 我试了试把手，门关着还锁了。 ② The shop keeps its shutters shut until ten in the morning. 这家店上午十点前卷帘门紧闭。
[搭] shut tight 关紧, wide shut 紧闭, keep shut 保持关闭, shut for the night 夜间打烊
[联] closed 关闭的, open 开着的, locked 锁住的, sealed 密封的, ajar 半开的, accessible 可进入的, available 可用的, tight 紧的
[记] keep your mouth shut 口语「闭嘴、别声张」。shut vs. closed：shut 更口语、偏物理关合；closed 可指「不对外开放」

**unlocked**
英/美 /ʌnˈlɑːkt/ 英 /ʌnˈlɒkt/
adj. 未锁的；已解锁的
[义] Not fastened with a lock; accessible without a key or code. 门、设备未上锁（leave the door unlocked 留门不上锁）；手机、账户已解锁（unlocked phone 已解锁手机）。与 locked 相对。
[例] ① Someone left the back door unlocked overnight. 有人整夜没锁后门。 ② You can install apps from outside the store on an unlocked device. 已解锁设备可安装应用商店外的应用。
[族] unlock 解锁, lock 锁, locked 锁上的
[搭] leave unlocked 不上锁, unlocked door/phone 未锁的门/已解锁手机, remain unlocked 保持未锁状态
[联] locked 锁上的, open 开着的, accessible 可进入的, secure 安全的, vulnerable 脆弱的, sealed 密封的, closed 关闭的, protected 受保护的
[记] leave unlocked 安全提醒常见。unlocked vs. open：unlocked 指「锁未扣」；open 指「开着、可通行」

**unsealed**
英/美 /ʌnˈsiːld/
adj. 未密封的；已启封的
[义] Not closed tightly; with the seal broken or never applied. 容器、信件未封或未封严（unsealed envelope 未封口的信, unsealed jar 未密封的罐）。与 sealed 相对。
[例] ① Health inspectors found unsealed food containers in the kitchen. 卫生检查员在厨房发现未密封的食品容器。 ② The ballot arrived in an unsealed envelope, so it was rejected. 选票装在未封口的信封里，因而作废。
[族] unseal 启封, seal 密封, sealed 密封的
[搭] unsealed container/envelope 未密封容器/未封口信封, remain unsealed 仍未密封, improperly unsealed 未妥善密封的
[联] sealed 密封的, open 开着的, exposed 暴露的, airtight 气密的, leaky 漏的, intact 完好的, broken 破损的, fresh 新鲜的
[记] unsealed 强调「封口/密封未做好」。unsealed vs. open：unsealed 专指「密封未闭合」；open 更宽

**uncovered**
英/美 /ʌnˈkʌvərd/
adj. 未遮盖的；暴露的
[义] Not covered or protected; revealed. 无遮盖（uncovered head 未戴帽的头, uncovered wiring 裸露线路）；揭露出来的（uncovered evidence 被发现的证据）。uncover 的过去分词作形容词；与 exposed 近义，uncovered 更偏「盖子/覆盖物拿掉」。
[例] ① Workers must wear helmets in areas with uncovered wiring. 有裸露线路的区域，工人必须戴安全帽。 ② Investigators uncovered a pattern of fraud that had gone unnoticed for years. 调查人员发现了多年来未受注意的一连串欺诈模式。
[族] uncover 揭开, cover 覆盖, covered 有遮盖的
[搭] uncovered wiring/head 裸露线路/未戴帽, leave uncovered 不遮盖, remain uncovered 仍未遮盖
[联] exposed 暴露的, covered 有遮盖的, naked 裸露的, hidden 隐藏的, sealed 密封的, protected 受保护的, bare 赤裸的, masked 遮掩的
[记] leave uncovered 可指「不盖盖子」或「不投保」。uncovered vs. exposed：uncovered 偏「覆盖物移除」；exposed 更宽，含「易受伤害」

**masked**
英/美 /mæskt/ 英 /mɑːskt/
adj. 戴面具的；遮掩的；掩蔽的
[义] Wearing a mask; hidden or disguised; (technical) prevented from having an effect. 戴面罩（masked robber 蒙面劫匪）；情感、意图被掩饰（masked anger 压抑的怒意）；技术语境「被掩蔽」（masked signal 被掩蔽的信号）。mask 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Masked protesters gathered outside the parliament building. 戴面罩的抗议者聚集在议会大楼外。 ② She answered with masked irritation that did not fool anyone. 她答话时压着怒意，但也没瞒过谁。
[族] mask 面具, unmasked 未遮掩的, masking 掩蔽
[搭] masked robber/protester 蒙面劫匪/抗议者, masked anger/fear 掩饰的怒意/恐惧, masked signal 掩蔽信号
[联] hidden 隐藏的, disguised 伪装的, unmasked 未遮掩的, exposed 暴露的, concealed 藏匿的, revealed 显露的, covered 有遮盖的, anonymous 匿名的
[记] masked 疫情后亦指「戴口罩的」。masked vs. hidden：masked 强调「表面一层遮盖/伪装」；hidden 更宽

**enveloped**
英/美 /ɪnˈveləpt/
adj. 被包围的；被笼罩的
[义] Completely wrapped or surrounded by something, often softly or diffusely. 被雾、黑暗、声音等笼罩（enveloped in fog 笼罩在雾中）；被材料包裹（enveloped in blankets 裹在毯子里）。比 engulfed 柔和，少「吞没、压倒」的危机感；envelop 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The valley was enveloped in thick fog by dawn. 黎明时分，山谷浓雾笼罩。 ② She sat on the sofa, enveloped in a wool blanket with a book in her hand. 她裹在羊毛毯里坐在沙发上，手里捧着书。
[族] envelop 包围, envelope 信封
[搭] enveloped in fog/darkness/silence 笼罩在雾/黑暗/寂静中, enveloped by 被……包围, completely enveloped 完全被笼罩
[联] surrounded 被包围的, engulfed 被吞没的, wrapped 包裹的, covered 有遮盖的, exposed 暴露的, immersed 浸没的, hidden 隐藏的, shrouded 笼罩的
[记] enveloped in 常接 fog, darkness, silence, warmth。enveloped vs. engulfed：enveloped 偏「包裹、笼罩」；engulfed 偏「吞没、压倒」

**anchored**
英/美 /ˈæŋkərd/
adj. 锚定的；固定的；以……为基础的
[义] Held firmly in place; based on something stable. 船抛锚固定（anchored offshore 在近海锚泊）；物体固定不动（anchored shelf 固定架）；抽象上扎根于（anchored in tradition 植根传统）。anchor 的过去分词作形容词；与 adrift（方位章）相对。
[例] ① The fleet remained anchored in the bay during the storm. 风暴期间，舰队在湾内锚泊。 ② Her confidence is anchored in years of careful preparation. 她的自信来自多年认真准备。
[族] anchor 锚, anchorage 锚地, unanchored 未锚定的
[搭] anchored offshore/in the bay 在近海/湾内锚泊, anchored in tradition 植根传统, firmly anchored 牢牢固定, remain anchored 保持锚定
[联] fixed 固定的, moored 系泊的, adrift 漂流的, afloat 漂浮的, stable 稳定的, secure 稳固的, rooted 扎根的, loose 松的
[记] anchored in 抽象义「以……为基础、扎根于」。anchored vs. moored：anchored 靠锚；moored 靠缆绳系泊

**workable**
英/美 /ˈwɜːrkəbl/ 英 /ˈwɜːkəbl/
adj. 可行的；可运作的；可塑的
[义] Capable of being done or used effectively; practical enough to function. 方案、妥协「做得成」（a workable compromise 可行的妥协）；材料可加工（workable clay 可塑陶土）。与 viable / usable 近义，workable 更绑「能付诸实施、能操作起来」。
[例] ① After hours of talks, both sides reached a workable agreement. 数小时谈判后，双方达成可行协议。 ② The soil is soft and workable after the rain. 雨后土壤松软，便于翻整。
[族] work 工作, workability 可加工性
[搭] workable solution/compromise/plan 可行方案/妥协/计划, workable clay/dough 可塑陶土/面团, remain workable 仍然可行
[联] viable 可行的, feasible 做得到的, usable 可用的, practical 实用的, impractical 不现实的, broken 坏的, operational 可运行的, flexible 灵活的
[记] workable compromise/solution 政治与商务高频。workable vs. viable：workable 偏「能操作、能实施」；viable 偏「能持续、能存活」

**faulty**
英/美 /ˈfɔːlti/ 英 /ˈfɔːlti/
adj. 有缺陷的；出错的
[义] Containing a fault or defect; not working correctly. 设备、逻辑、产品有问题（faulty wiring 线路故障, faulty reasoning 推理有误）。比 broken 轻，常指「某部分出错、未必整体报废」；比 malfunctioning 更常作定语形容词。
[例] ① The fire was traced to faulty wiring in the attic. 火灾起因是阁楼线路故障。 ② The argument rests on faulty assumptions about how markets behave. 该论点建立在关于市场行为的错误假设上。
[族] fault 过错, faultless 无瑕疵的, faultily 有缺陷地
[搭] faulty wiring/equipment 故障线路/设备, faulty logic/reasoning 错误逻辑/推理, faulty product 有缺陷产品
[联] defective 有缺陷的, broken 坏的, malfunctioning 故障中的, functional 正常运作的, flawed 有瑕疵的, sound 完好的, correct 正确的, error 错误
[记] faulty goods 英式「有瑕疵商品」。faulty vs. defective：近义；faulty 更口语、新闻常见

**unusable**
英/美 /ʌnˈjuːzəbl/
adj. 无法使用的；不能用的
[义] Not fit to be used; so damaged or poorly designed that use is impossible. 因损坏或设计问题完全不能用（render unusable 使无法使用, unusable interface 难用的界面）。与 usable 相对，语气比 broken 更绑「对人不可用」。
[例] ① The crash left the laptop bent and completely unusable. 撞击把笔记本摔弯，完全没法用了。 ② Outdated file formats can become unusable on modern systems. 过时文件格式在新系统上可能无法打开。
[族] use 使用, usable 可用的, usability 可用性
[搭] render unusable 使无法使用, completely unusable 完全不能用, unusable interface 难用的界面, practically unusable 几乎没法用
[联] usable 可用的, broken 坏的, functional 正常运作的, defective 有缺陷的, obsolete 过时的, accessible 可使用的, handy 顺手的, useless 无用的
[记] practically unusable 指「几乎没法用」。unusable vs. useless：unusable 指「不能用」；useless 指「没用、无价值」

**airtight**
英/美 /ˈertaɪt/
adj. 密封的；无懈可击的
[义] Completely sealed so that no air can enter or escape; (figurative) allowing no exceptions or criticism. 容器气密（airtight container 密封罐）；比喻「无漏洞」（an airtight alibi 无懈可击的不在场证明）。与 leaky 相对。
[例] ① Store the coffee in an airtight jar to keep it fresh. 把咖啡放在密封罐里保鲜。 ② The prosecution presented what looked like an airtight case. 检方呈上了看似无懈可击的案情。
[搭] airtight container/seal 密封罐/密封, airtight alibi/case 无懈可击的不在场证明/案情, hermetically airtight 完全气密的
[联] sealed 密封的, leaky 漏的, tight 紧的, unsealed 未密封的, secure 安全的, porous 多孔的, waterproof 防水的, flawed 有漏洞的
[记] airtight alibi/case 比喻「挑不出漏洞」。airtight vs. sealed：airtight 专指「不漏气」；sealed 可指封口、保密

**leaky**
英/美 /ˈliːki/
adj. 漏的；易泄密的
[义] Allowing liquid or gas to escape; prone to letting secrets out. 物理上漏（a leaky roof 漏雨的屋顶, leaky pipe 漏水的管子）；比喻易泄密（a leaky organization 容易走漏消息的机构）。与 airtight / sealed 相对。
[形] 比较级 leakier；最高级 leakiest
[例] ① We placed buckets under the leaky ceiling during the storm. 暴风雨中，我们在漏水的天花板下放了桶。 ② The startup was too leaky—product details appeared online before launch. 这家初创公司守不住密，产品细节在发布前就上了网。
[族] leak 泄漏, leakage 泄漏
[搭] leaky roof/pipe/faucet 漏雨的屋顶/管子/龙头, leaky bucket 漏桶, leaky organization 易泄密的机构
[联] sealed 密封的, airtight 气密的, broken 坏的, dripping 滴水的, dry 干燥的, porous 多孔的, secure 安全的, faulty 有缺陷的
[记] leaky bucket 可喻「有漏洞的系统、存不住东西」。leaky vs. faulty：leaky 专指「漏」；faulty 更宽，指各种缺陷

**discontinuous**
英/美 /ˌdɪskənˈtɪnjuəs/
adj. 不连续的；间断的
[义] Having gaps or interruptions; not forming an unbroken whole. 时间、空间或数据上不连贯（discontinuous service 断续服务, discontinuous function 不连续函数）。比 intermittent 更书面、偏「整体被切断成段」。
[例] ① Bus service is discontinuous on Sundays, with long gaps between routes. 周日公交断续运行，班次间隔很长。 ② The fossil record is discontinuous, leaving many gaps in our knowledge. 化石记录不连续，我们的认知因而有不少空白。
[源] 源自 dis-（不）+ continuous（连续的）→ 不连续的。
[族] discontinue 中止, discontinuity 不连续
[搭] discontinuous service 断续服务, discontinuous data 不连续数据, discontinuous function 不连续函数, remain discontinuous 仍然不连续
[联] continuous 连续的, intermittent 间歇的, sporadic 零星的, broken 中断的, uninterrupted 不间断的, gap 缺口, ongoing 进行中的, regular 有规律的
[记] discontinuous 数学、科学语体常见。discontinuous vs. intermittent：discontinuous 强调「被切成段、有缺口」；intermittent 强调「时停时续」

**occasional**
英/美 /əˈkeɪʒənl/
adj. 偶尔的；临时的
[义] Happening sometimes but not often or regularly; used for special situations. 发生不频（occasional rain 偶有降雨, occasional visitor 偶尔来的客人）；临时用途（an occasional table 备用小桌）。比 sporadic 更日常、语气更轻。
[例] ① We enjoy an occasional dinner out, but mostly cook at home. 我们偶尔外出吃饭，但多半在家做。 ② There is occasional traffic on this road, though it is usually quiet. 这条路通常很静，偶尔有车经过。
[族] occasion 场合, occasionally 偶尔
[搭] occasional rain/visitor 偶有降雨/偶尔来客, occasional use 偶尔使用, occasional table/chair 备用小桌/椅
[联] sporadic 零星的, frequent 频繁的, regular 有规律的, rare 罕见的, constant 不断的, temporary 临时的, habitual 习惯性的, intermittent 间歇的
[记] occasional table 英式家具义「备用小几」。occasional vs. sporadic：occasional 更轻、更日常；sporadic 偏「零星、分散、无规律」

**connected**
英/美 /kəˈnektɪd/
adj. 连接的；有关联的；联网的
[义] Joined together; linked logically or digitally; related to influential people (informal). 物理或网络已连接（stay connected 保持联网, connected devices 联网设备）；逻辑上相关（closely connected ideas 紧密相关的想法）；口语「有背景、有人脉」。connect 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Make sure all cables are properly connected before switching on the machine. 开机前请确认所有线缆接好。 ② The two scandals are closely connected, according to the report. 报告称两起丑闻密切相关。
[族] connect 连接, connection 连接, disconnected 断开的
[搭] stay connected 保持联网/联系, connected devices 联网设备, closely connected 密切相关, well connected 有人脉（口语）
[联] disconnected 断开的, linked 相连的, online 在线的, related 相关的, integrated 一体化的, isolated 孤立的, separate 分离的, attached 附着的
[记] well connected 口语指「人脉广、有背景」。connected vs. related：connected 可指物理/网络连接；related 偏「有关联」

**shielded**
英/美 /ˈʃiːldɪd/
adj. 受遮蔽的；屏蔽的
[义] Protected from harm, radiation, or interference by a physical barrier. 受屏障保护（shielded cable 屏蔽线缆, shielded room 屏蔽室）；比喻受保护（shielded from criticism 免受批评）。shield 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Use shielded cables to reduce interference near heavy machinery. 在重型机械附近应使用屏蔽线缆以减少干扰。 ② Growing up wealthy, she was shielded from many everyday hardships. 她在富裕环境中长大，许多日常艰辛都接触不到。
[族] shield 盾牌, shielding 屏蔽
[搭] shielded cable/room 屏蔽线缆/屏蔽室, shielded from 免受, heavily shielded 严密防护的
[联] protected 受保护的, exposed 暴露的, sheltered 有遮蔽的, unprotected 无防护的, covered 有遮盖的, vulnerable 脆弱的, secure 安全的, isolated 孤立的
[记] shielded from 表「免受……影响/伤害」。shielded vs. protected：shielded 强调「有屏障挡着」；protected 更宽

**protected**
英/美 /prəˈtektɪd/
adj. 受保护的；受法律保护的
[义] Kept safe from harm or damage; legally safeguarded. 人、物、环境受保护（protected species 受保护物种, protected area 保护区）；数据、账户受防护（password-protected file 密码保护文件）。protect 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The wetland is a protected area where building is not allowed. 这片湿地是保护区，禁止建设。 ② All employee records are stored in a password-protected system. 所有员工档案存放在密码保护系统中。
[族] protect 保护, protection 保护, unprotected 无防护的
[搭] protected species/area 受保护物种/区域, password-protected 密码保护的, protected status 受保护地位, remain protected 仍受保护
[联] unprotected 无防护的, shielded 受遮蔽的, exposed 暴露的, secure 安全的, vulnerable 脆弱的, safe 安全的, endangered 濒危的, legal 法律的
[记] protected status 环保与劳动法语境常见。protected vs. secure：protected 强调「有人/制度在保护」；secure 偏「稳固、安全无虞」

**surrounded**
英/美 /səˈraʊndɪd/
adj. 被包围的；环绕的
[义] On every side by something or someone. 物理上四面被围（surrounded by trees 树木环绕, surrounded by enemies 被敌人包围）；抽象上遍布（surrounded by friends 身边都是朋友）。surround 的过去分词作形容词，比 enveloped 更中性、可好可坏。
[例] ① The cottage is surrounded by apple orchards in every direction. 农舍四周都是苹果园。 ② She felt surrounded by doubt after the project failed. 项目失败后，她疑云密布。
[族] surround 包围, surroundings 环境
[搭] surrounded by 被……环绕, completely surrounded 完全被包围, surrounded on all sides 四面被围
[联] enveloped 被笼罩的, engulfed 被吞没的, isolated 孤立的, exposed 暴露的, encircled 被围住的, trapped 困住的, free 自由的, nestled 坐落于…的
[记] surrounded by 后可接人或物：surrounded by friends / by mountains。surrounded vs. trapped：surrounded 未必负面；trapped 强调「出不去」

**balanced**
英/美 /ˈbælənst/
adj. 平衡的；均衡的；清醒的
[义] Evenly distributed; stable and sensible. 物理或比例均衡（a balanced load 均衡载荷, balanced diet 均衡饮食）；判断清醒（a balanced view 平衡的观点）。与 unbalanced / equilibrium 相对；balance 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A balanced breakfast includes protein, fruit, and whole grains. 均衡早餐应含蛋白质、水果和全谷物。 ② The article offered a balanced account of both sides of the debate. 文章对辩论双方作了平衡报道。
[族] balance 平衡, unbalanced 不平衡的, imbalance 失衡
[搭] balanced diet/view 均衡饮食/平衡观点, balanced approach 均衡做法, remain balanced 保持平衡, well balanced 均衡良好的
[联] unbalanced 不平衡的, stable 稳定的, even 均匀的, fair 公平的, skewed 偏斜的, equilibrium 均衡, steady 平稳的, lopsided 一边倒的
[记] balanced diet/view 健康与媒体高频。balanced vs. stable：balanced 指「比例得当」；stable 指「整体稳固、不易变」

**perched**
英/美 /pɜːrtʃt/ 英 /pɜːtʃt/
adj. 栖息的；位于高处的；摇摇欲坠的
[义] Resting on something narrow or high; situated in a precarious but prominent position. 鸟栖枝头（birds perched on wires 电线上的鸟）；建筑、城镇位于高处（a village perched on a cliff 悬崖上的村庄）；物体险险搭在边缘（glasses perched on her nose 眼镜架在鼻尖）。perch 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A hawk remained perched on the fence post, watching the field. 一只鹰栖在栅栏柱上，盯着田野。 ② The hotel is perched on a hillside with views over the lake. 酒店建在山坡上，可俯瞰湖景。
[族] perch 栖息, perching 栖息的
[搭] perched on 栖息在/位于, perched high above 高悬于, precariously perched 摇摇欲坠地搭在
[联] poised 蓄势待发的, balanced 平衡的, precarious 不稳固的, elevated 高处的, nested 筑巢的, grounded 着地的, cliff 悬崖, edge 边缘
[记] perched on the edge 可指物理边缘或「处于临界点」。perched vs. poised：perched 强调「停在高处/窄处」；poised 强调「蓄势、稳住」

**moored**
英/美 /mʊrd/ 英 /mʊəd/ 美亦 /mɔːrd/
adj. 系泊的；停泊的
[义] Secured to a fixed object by ropes or cables, especially a boat. 船只用缆绳系在码头、浮标等固定物上（moored yacht 系泊的游艇, remain moored 继续停泊）。与 anchored（抛锚）近义，moored 更绑「缆绳系泊」；与 adrift（漂流）相对。
[例] ① The fishing boats stayed moored in the harbour during the gale. 大风期间，渔船都系在港内。 ② A small dinghy was moored to the pier with a frayed rope. 一艘小艇用磨损的绳子系在码头上。
[族] moor 系泊, mooring 系泊处, unmoored 未系泊的
[搭] moored to the pier/buoy 系在码头/浮标上, remain moored 继续停泊, securely moored 系泊牢固的
[联] anchored 锚泊的, adrift 漂流的, afloat 漂浮的, docked 停靠的, tied 系住的, loose 松的, harbour 港口, fixed 固定的
[记] moored to 表「系在……上」。moored vs. anchored：moored 靠缆绳；anchored 靠锚。stay moored 航海常见

**tethered**
英/美 /ˈteðərd/
adj. 拴住的；受约束的
[义] Tied to a fixed point with a rope or chain; restricted in movement or freedom. 动物、物体被拴住（a tethered goat 拴着的山羊）；比喻受限制（tethered to a desk job 困在案头工作）。tether 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The dog was tethered to a post outside the shop. 狗被拴在店外的柱子上。 ② She felt tethered to her phone by constant work messages. 工作消息不断，她觉得自己被手机拴住了。
[族] tether 拴绳, untethered 未拴住的
[搭] tethered to 拴在/受……约束, tethered goat/dog 拴着的山羊/狗, remain tethered 仍然被拴住
[联] tied 系住的, bound 被束缚的, trapped 困住的, free 自由的, anchored 锚定的, chained 锁链拴住的, loose 松的, restricted 受限制的
[记] tethered to 抽象义「被……拴住、脱不开」。untethered 近年亦指「未系安全绳」或「不受约束」

**unmasked**
英/美 /ʌnˈmæskt/ 英 /ʌnˈmɑːskt/
adj. 未遮掩的；暴露身份的
[义] With the mask removed; identity or true nature revealed. 面具摘掉（unmasked face 露出的脸）；身份或真面目被揭露（the spy was unmasked 间谍身份曝光）。与 masked 相对。
[例] ① The thief left an unmasked face clearly visible on security footage. 监控清楚拍到了劫匪未遮的脸。 ② Investigative reporting unmasked conflicts of interest on the board. 调查报道揭露了董事会成员的利益冲突。
[族] unmask 揭露, mask 遮掩, masked 遮掩的
[搭] unmasked identity 暴露的身份, leave unmasked 不遮掩, finally unmasked 最终被揭露
[联] masked 遮掩的, exposed 暴露的, revealed 显露的, hidden 隐藏的, anonymous 匿名的, identified 已确认身份的, concealed 藏匿的, disguised 伪装的
[记] unmask 作动词指「揭露真面目」。unmasked vs. exposed：unmasked 专指「去掉伪装/面具」；exposed 更宽

**unprotected**
英/美 /ˌʌnprəˈtektɪd/
adj. 无防护的；未采取保护措施的
[义] Not kept safe from harm; without legal or physical protection. 无物理防护（unprotected wiring 无保护线路）；未采取安全措施（unprotected sex 无保护性行为）；法律上无保障（unprotected workers 无保障劳工）。与 protected / shielded 相对。
[例] ① Children should not play near unprotected electrical outlets. 儿童不应在无防护插座附近玩耍。 ② Many gig workers remain unprotected by standard labour laws. 许多零工仍不受标准劳动法保护。
[族] protect 保护, protected 受保护的, unprotectedly 无防护地
[搭] unprotected sex 无保护性行为, unprotected wiring/workers 无保护线路/无保障劳工, leave unprotected 不设防护
[联] protected 受保护的, exposed 暴露的, vulnerable 脆弱的, shielded 受遮蔽的, secure 安全的, safe 安全的, covered 有遮盖的, insured 有保险的
[记] unprotected 医学与劳动法语境均常见。unprotected vs. exposed：unprotected 强调「没有保护措施」；exposed 强调「露在外面、易受伤害」

**existent**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzɪstənt/ 英 /ɪɡˈzɪstənt/
adj. 现存的；存在的
[义] Still in existence; real at the present time. 目前仍然存在（the only existent copy 唯一现存本, barely existent 几乎不存在）。比 existing 更书面、常绑「经时间筛选后仍在」；与 nonexistent 相对。
[例] ① This is the only existent manuscript from that workshop. 这是该作坊唯一现存的手稿。 ② Public trust in the institution was barely existent after the scandal. 丑闻之后，公众对该机构的信任几乎荡然无存。
[族] exist 存在, existence 存在, nonexistent 不存在的
[搭] only existent copy 唯一现存本, barely existent 几乎不存在, still existent 仍然存在的
[联] existing 现存的, nonexistent 不存在的, extant 尚存的, absent 缺席的, real 真实的, imaginary 想象的, present 在场的, missing 缺失的
[记] existent 多修饰 copy, record, trust, threat 等。existent vs. existing：existent 更书面、常带「历经时间仍在」；existing 更日常

**plugged**
英/美 /plʌɡd/
adj. 插好的；堵住的
[义] Connected by inserting a plug into a socket; blocked to stop a leak. 电器已插电（stay plugged in 保持插电）；孔洞被塞住（a plugged drain 堵住的下水道）。plug 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The laptop must stay plugged in during the software update. 软件更新期间笔记本必须插着电源。 ② A plugged filter caused the machine to overheat. 滤芯堵塞导致机器过热。
[族] plug 插头, unplug 拔插头, unplugged 未插电的
[搭] stay plugged in 保持插电, plugged drain/filter 堵住的下水道/滤芯, plugged into 插入/接入
[联] unplugged 未插电的, connected 连接的, powered 通电的, blocked 堵塞的, loose 松的, online 在线的, charged 充电的, empty 空的
[记] plugged into 可指「接入系统/网络」：plugged into social media 沉迷社交媒体。plugged vs. connected：plugged 专指「插头插入」或「堵住」

**unplugged**
英/美 /ˌʌnˈplʌɡd/
adj. 未插电的；拔下插头的
[义] Not connected to an electrical supply; (music) performed with acoustic instruments. 设备未接电源（work unplugged 不插电工作）；音乐「不插电」演唱会（an unplugged session 原声专场）。与 plugged 相对。
[例] ① You can run the test unplugged if the battery is fully charged. 电池满电时可不插电运行测试。 ② The band released an unplugged version of their biggest hit. 乐队发布了热门曲目的不插电版。
[族] unplug 拔插头, plug 插头, plugged 插好的
[搭] work unplugged 不插电工作, unplugged session/version 不插电演出/版本, go unplugged 改为不插电
[联] plugged 插好的, powered 通电的, unpowered 未通电的, offline 离线的, acoustic 原声的, charged 充电的, battery 电池, connected 连接的
[记] MTV Unplugged 使该词在音乐语境极知名。unplugged vs. offline：unplugged 专指「未接电源」；offline 指「未联网」

**powered**
英/美 /ˈpaʊərd/
adj. 有动力的；通电的
[义] Supplied with energy to operate; driven by a specified source. 有电力或动力驱动（battery-powered device 电池供电设备, solar-powered 太阳能驱动的）。power 的过去分词作形容词，常作复合词后半部分。
[例] ① The gate is powered by a small electric motor. 大门由小电动机驱动。 ② Solar-powered lights line the garden path at night. 夜间花园小径两侧是太阳能灯。
[族] power 动力, unpowered 无动力的, empower 赋能
[搭] battery/solar-powered 电池/太阳能驱动的, powered by 由……驱动, fully powered 全功率运行的
[联] unpowered 无动力的, plugged 插好的, operational 可运行的, manual 手动的, electric 电动的, charged 充电的, active 活跃的, idle 闲置的
[记] -powered 作后缀构词极活跃：computer-powered, AI-powered。powered vs. plugged：powered 指「有动力来源」；plugged 专指「插头插着」

**unpowered**
英/美 /ʌnˈpaʊərd/
adj. 无动力的；断电的
[义] Not equipped with an engine or electrical supply; without power. 无发动机或电力（unpowered glider 无动力滑翔机, unpowered flight 无动力飞行）；系统断电（go unpowered 断电）。与 powered 相对。
[例] ① The glider is an unpowered aircraft that relies on rising air. 滑翔机是无动力飞机，靠上升气流飞行。 ② The whole block went unpowered after the substation failed. 变电站故障后，整个街区断电。
[族] power 动力, powered 有动力的
[搭] unpowered glider/flight 无动力滑翔机/飞行, go unpowered 断电, remain unpowered 仍未通电
[联] powered 有动力的, manual 手动的, offline 离线的, dead 无电的, operational 可运行的, electric 电动的, idle 闲置的, active 活跃的
[记] unpowered 航空语境指「无引擎」。go unpowered 口语可指「断电、没动力」

**drooping**
英/美 /ˈdruːpɪŋ/
adj. 低垂的；萎靡的
[义] Hanging or bending downward; lacking energy or firmness. 物理上下垂（drooping branches 低垂的树枝, drooping eyelids 耷拉的眼皮）；精神萎靡（drooping spirits 低落情绪）。drooping 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① The flowers looked drooping after a week without water. 一周没浇水，花儿都蔫了。 ② He spoke in a drooping voice after hearing the bad news. 听到坏消息后，他说话有气无力。
[族] droop 低垂, droopy 低垂的（口语）
[搭] drooping branches/eyelids 低垂的树枝/眼皮, drooping spirits 萎靡的情绪, look drooping 显得蔫了
[联] limp 无力的, sagging 下坠的, wilted 蔫了的, perky 精神抖擞的, upright 竖立的, tired 疲惫的, lively 有活力的, flaccid 松软无力的
[记] drooping vs. limp：drooping 强调「往下耷」；limp 强调「软塌、没劲」。droopy 口语形容词同义

**wrapped**
英/美 /ræpt/
adj. 包裹好的；缠绕的
[义] Covered or enclosed in paper, cloth, or other material; deeply involved (informal). 被包起来（a wrapped gift 包好的礼物, wrapped in bandages 缠着绷带）；口语深陷某事（wrapped up in work 埋头工作）。wrap 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The fragile vase arrived safely, wrapped in layers of bubble wrap. 易碎花瓶裹着多层气泡膜，完好送达。 ② He was too wrapped up in the game to hear the doorbell. 他游戏打得太投入，没听见门铃。
[族] wrap 包裹, unwrapped 未包裹的, wrapping 包装材料
[搭] wrapped in 包裹在/深陷于, wrapped gift 包好的礼物, tightly wrapped 包得很紧, wrapped up in 埋头于
[联] unwrapped 未包裹的, covered 有遮盖的, enveloped 被笼罩的, exposed 暴露的, bundled 捆好的, sealed 密封的, bare 赤裸的, absorbed 全神贯注的
[记] wrapped up in 表「埋头于、深陷于」。wrapped vs. enveloped：wrapped 偏「用材料包住」；enveloped 偏「笼罩、围住」

**unwrapped**
英/美 /ʌnˈræpt/
adj. 未包裹的；已拆封的
[义] Not covered with wrapping; with packaging removed. 礼物、货物未包或已拆包（unwrapped gifts 没包装的礼物, leave food unwrapped 食物不包起来）。与 wrapped / sealed 相对。
[例] ① Unwrapped meat should be stored carefully to avoid contamination. 未包装的肉类应妥善存放，以免污染。 ② The children tore into the unwrapped toys on the table. 孩子们抓起桌上没包装的玩具玩起来。
[族] unwrap 拆开, wrap 包裹, wrapped 包裹好的
[搭] unwrapped gift/food 未包装的礼物/食物, leave unwrapped 不包裹, arrive unwrapped 未包装送达
[联] wrapped 包裹好的, sealed 密封的, exposed 暴露的, packaged 有包装的, bare 赤裸的, covered 有遮盖的, fresh 新鲜的, contaminated 受污染的
[记] leave unwrapped 食品卫生语境常见。unwrapped vs. uncovered：unwrapped 专指「包装材料」；uncovered 更宽

**covered**
英/美 /ˈkʌvərd/
adj. 有遮盖的；承保的
[义] Having something on top as protection or concealment; included in insurance. 表面有覆盖物（snow-covered roads 积雪道路, covered head 戴盖的头）；保险已承保（fully covered 全额承保）。cover 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The trail was muddy and covered with fallen leaves. 小径泥泞，铺满落叶。 ② Check whether pre-existing conditions are covered by your policy. 请核对保单是否承保既往病史。
[族] cover 覆盖, uncovered 未遮盖的, coverage 承保范围
[搭] covered in/with 布满/盖着, snow-covered 积雪的, fully covered 全额承保, covered head 戴盖的头
[联] uncovered 未遮盖的, exposed 暴露的, wrapped 包裹好的, bare 赤裸的, protected 受保护的, insured 承保的, hidden 隐藏的, blanketed 覆盖着的
[记] covered in/with 极频：covered in mud 满身泥。covered by insurance 指「在保险范围内」

**attached**
英/美 /əˈtætʃt/
adj. 附着的；连接的；依恋的
[义] Fastened or joined to something; emotionally devoted. 物理上附着（attached garage 连体车库, attached file 附件）；情感上依恋（become attached to 对……产生依恋）。attach 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Please see the attached spreadsheet for the latest figures. 最新数据见附件表格。 ② The puppy became deeply attached to the family within days. 小狗几天内就对这家人十分依恋。
[族] attach 附着, attachment 附件, detached 分离的
[搭] attached file/garage 附件/连体车库, attached to 附着于/依恋, remain attached 保持连接
[联] detached 分离的, connected 连接的, linked 相连的, separate 分离的, joined 接合的, loose 松的, fixed 固定的, devoted 忠诚的
[记] attached file 邮件极频。attached to 情感义「对……依恋」。attached vs. connected：attached 可指情感依附；connected 偏物理/逻辑连接

**ajar**
英/美 /əˈdʒɑːr/
adj. 半开的；微开的
adv. 半开着
[义] Slightly open. 门、窗等虚掩、留缝（leave the door ajar 门虚掩, stand ajar 半开着）。只作表语或补语，不作定语前置。与 shut / closed 相对，表示「未关严」。
[例] ① She left the window ajar to let in some fresh air. 她把窗户留了一条缝透气。 ② I noticed the office door was ajar and voices were coming from inside. 办公室门虚掩着，里面传来说话声。
[搭] leave ajar 虚掩, stand/sit ajar 半开着, slightly ajar 微开
[联] open 开着的, shut 关着的, closed 关闭的, locked 锁住的, wide 大开的, narrow 窄的, gap 缝隙, sealed 密封的
[记] leave the door ajar 固定说法。ajar 只作补语，不说 × an ajar door（应说 a door standing ajar）。ajar vs. open：ajar 专指「留缝、未关严」

**overt**
英/美 /oʊˈvɜːrt/ 英 /əʊˈvɜːt/ 美 /oʊˈvɜːrt/
adj. 公开的；明显的
[义] Done or shown openly; not secret or hidden. 行为、敌意等公然表露（overt hostility 公然敌意, overt discrimination 公开歧视）。与 covert 相对，正式语体常见。
[例] ① The policy change met with overt criticism from staff. 政策调整遭到员工公开批评。 ② There was no overt sign of damage on the building's exterior. 楼外看不出明显损坏迹象。
[族] overtly 公开地
[搭] overt hostility/discrimination 公然敌意/歧视, overt sign 明显迹象, overt support 公开支持
[联] covert 隐蔽的, open 公开的, hidden 隐藏的, obvious 明显的, secret 秘密的, explicit 明确的, implicit 含蓄的, visible 可见的
[记] overt vs. covert 一对反义，政治、心理、军事语境常见。overt vs. obvious：overt 强调「不隐瞒、公然」；obvious 强调「容易看出」

**covert**
英/美 /ˈkoʊvɜːrt/ 英 /ˈkʌvət/ 美 /ˈkoʊvɜːrt/
adj. 隐蔽的；秘密的
n. 隐藏处（略）
[义] Not openly acknowledged or displayed; secret. 行动、支持等暗中进行（covert operation 秘密行动, covert support 暗中支持）。与 overt 相对；名词 covert 指树丛等「藏身处」，略罕。
[例] ① The agency conducted covert surveillance for several months. 该机构进行了数月的秘密监视。 ② There was covert resistance to the new rules within the department. 部门内部有人暗中抵制新规定。
[族] covertly 暗中地
[搭] covert operation/support 秘密行动/暗中支持, covert surveillance 秘密监视, covert channel 隐蔽渠道
[联] overt 公开的, hidden 隐藏的, secret 秘密的, undercover 卧底的, open 公开的, disguised 伪装的, stealthy 隐秘的, visible 可见的
[记] covert operation 新闻与军事常见。covert 读音英式 /ˈkʌvət/、美式形容词亦可读 /ˈkoʊvɜːrt/，与 overt 不同

**impaired**
英/美 /ɪmˈperd/
adj. 受损的；功能减弱的
[义] Weakened or damaged in function; (polite) having a disability. 听力、视力、判断等受损（visually impaired 视力受损, impaired judgment 判断力下降）；委婉指残障（hearing-impaired 听力障碍的）。impair 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Lack of sleep can leave you with impaired concentration the next day. 睡眠不足会导致第二天注意力下降。 ② The building has ramps for mobility-impaired visitors. 大楼设有坡道，方便行动不便者出入。
[族] impair 损害, impairment 障碍
[搭] visually/hearing-impaired 视力/听力受损的, impaired judgment/memory 判断力/记忆力下降, mobility-impaired 行动不便的
[联] damaged 损坏的, disabled 残障的, functional 正常运作的, intact 完好的, weakened 削弱的, normal 正常的, healthy 健康的, defective 有缺陷的
[记] -impaired 作复合形容词常见：speech-impaired。impaired 比 disabled 更绑「某功能受损」而非整体身份

**unfinished**
英/美 /ʌnˈfɪnɪʃt/
adj. 未完成的；未完工的
[义] Not brought to an end or completed state. 工作、建筑、作品未完成（unfinished business 未了之事, unfinished novel 未完成的小说）。与 complete / incomplete 相对；incomplete 更绑「缺部分」，unfinished 更绑「尚未做完」。
[例] ① The attic remained unfinished for years after the renovation stopped. 装修停工后，阁楼多年未完工。 ② He left behind an unfinished manuscript and a pile of notes. 他留下一部未完成的手稿和一堆笔记。
[族] finish 完成, finished 完成的
[搭] unfinished business/work 未了之事/未完成工作, unfinished novel/building 未完成的小说/未完工建筑, leave unfinished 留下未完成部分
[联] incomplete 不完整的, complete 完整的, done 完成的, ongoing 进行中的, draft 草稿, partial 部分的, whole 完整的, abandoned 被弃置的
[记] unfinished business 可指具体事，也可指「心结、未竟之事」。unfinished vs. incomplete：unfinished 强调「还没做完」；incomplete 强调「缺块、不齐全」

**revealed**
英/美 /rɪˈviːld/
adj. 显露的；被揭露的
[义] Made known or visible; no longer hidden. 事实、身份、情感显露（newly revealed details 新披露的细节, revealed preference 显示性偏好）；身体裸露（revealed skin 露出的皮肤）。reveal 的过去分词作形容词；与 hidden（感知章）相对，本章收「已显露」状态。
[例] ① Newly revealed documents changed how historians viewed the treaty. 新披露的文件改变了史学家对条约的看法。 ② The dress left her shoulders revealed in the evening light. 晚光里，这件礼服露着她的双肩。
[族] reveal 揭露, revelation 揭露, revealing 暴露的
[搭] newly revealed 新披露的, revealed identity/preference 显露的身份/显示性偏好, remain revealed 仍然显露
[联] hidden 隐藏的, disclosed 公开的, exposed 暴露的, concealed 藏匿的, masked 遮掩的, uncovered 未遮盖的, secret 秘密的, visible 可见的
[记] revealed preference 经济学术语「显示性偏好」。revealed vs. disclosed：revealed 偏「被看见/被揭开」；disclosed 偏「被正式公布」

**disclosed**
英/美 /dɪsˈkloʊzd/ 英 /dɪsˈkləʊzd/
adj. 已披露的；公开的
[义] Made known to the public or to relevant parties. 信息、利益、身份已公开（fully disclosed risks 充分披露的风险, disclosed conflict of interest 已披露的利益冲突）。disclose 的过去分词作形容词，金融、法律语境极频。
[例] ① Investors must receive fully disclosed information about fees. 投资者须获得关于费用的充分披露信息。 ② The panel included a disclosed industry consultant. 专家组里有一名已披露身份的业界顾问。
[族] disclose 披露, disclosure 披露, undisclosed 未披露的
[搭] fully disclosed 充分披露的, disclosed conflict of interest 已披露的利益冲突, publicly disclosed 已公开披露的
[联] undisclosed 未披露的, revealed 显露的, secret 秘密的, transparent 透明的, hidden 隐藏的, confidential 保密的, open 公开的, reported 已报告的
[记] fully disclosed 金融合规高频。disclosed vs. revealed：disclosed 更绑「正式公布、合规披露」

**undisclosed**
英/美 /ˌʌndɪsˈkloʊzd/
adj. 未披露的；秘密的
[义] Not made known publicly; kept secret. 金额、身份、条款未公开（undisclosed sum 未公开金额, undisclosed location 秘密地点）。与 disclosed 相对，新闻、并购、法律语境常见。
[例] ① The company was sold for an undisclosed sum last month. 该公司上月以未公开金额售出。 ② Witnesses were moved to an undisclosed location for their safety. 证人被转移到秘密地点以确保安全。
[族] disclose 披露, disclosed 已披露的
[搭] undisclosed sum/amount 未公开金额, undisclosed location 秘密地点, undisclosed buyer 未公开买家, remain undisclosed 仍未披露
[联] disclosed 已披露的, secret 秘密的, confidential 保密的, anonymous 匿名的, hidden 隐藏的, revealed 显露的, unknown 未知的, private 私下的
[记] undisclosed sum/location 新闻标题极频。undisclosed vs. secret：undisclosed 偏「按规定应披露却未披露」；secret 更宽

**undone**
英/美 /ʌnˈdʌn/
adj. 未完成的；松开的
[义] Not fastened or tied; not completed. 扣子、鞋带等松开（come undone 松开了, with hair undone 披散着头发）；工作未完成（leave a task undone 任务没做完）。口语极频。
[例] ① One button on his shirt had come undone during the long walk. 长途步行后，他衬衫有一颗扣子松了。 ② She preferred to leave the hem undone until the final fitting. 她打算等到终试前再缝好下摆。
[搭] come undone 松开/失败, leave undone 未完成, hair undone 披散的头发, undone task 未竟之事
[联] unfinished 未完成的, loose 松的, fastened 系牢的, tied 系住的, done 完成的, broken 断开的, complete 完整的, tangled 缠结的
[记] come undone 可指「扣子松开」，也可指「计划泡汤」。undone vs. unfinished：undone 可指「系带松开」；unfinished 专指「未做完」

**unset**
英/美 /ʌnˈset/
adj. 未固定的；不安的
[义] Not yet fixed in place or decided; slightly worried or disturbed. 胶卷、类型未设定（unset cement 未凝固的水泥）；人心里不踏实（feel unset 感到不安）。与 set / fixed 相对；不安义比 upset 更轻。
[例] ① Walk carefully—the concrete is still unset in that section. 小心走——那段混凝土还没凝固。 ② She felt unset after hearing the rumours about layoffs. 听到裁员传言后，她心里不踏实。
[族] set 设定, unsettle 使不安, unsettled 不安的
[搭] unset cement 未凝固的水泥, feel unset 感到不安, remain unset 仍未固定
[联] fixed 固定的, set 设定的, stable 稳定的, unsettled 不安的, firm 牢固的, decided 已决定的, loose 松的, anxious 焦虑的
[记] unset 技术语境指「未固化」；日常更常见 unsettled（不安的）。unset vs. unstable：unset 可指「未凝固/未设定」；unstable 指「不稳」

**frequent**
英/美 /ˈfriːkwənt/
adj. 频繁的；常去的
[义] Happening often or at short intervals; regularly visited. 事件频发（frequent delays 频繁延误）；场所「常去的」（a frequent guest 常客）。本章收形容词「频发状态」。
[例] ① Frequent power cuts made it hard to run the clinic. 频繁停电，诊所很难正常运转。 ② He is a frequent visitor to the museum on Sunday afternoons. 他周日下午常来这家博物馆。
[族] frequently 频繁地, frequency 频率, infrequent 罕见的
[搭] frequent visitor/guest 常客, frequent delays/breakdowns 频繁延误/故障, frequent changes 频繁变动
[联] infrequent 罕见的, regular 有规律的, occasional 偶尔的, constant 不断的, rare 罕见的, habitual 习惯性的, sporadic 零星的, intermittent 间歇的
[记] frequent 作动词 /friˈkwent/ 义「常去（某地）」，与形容词同拼不同音。frequent vs. regular：frequent 强调「次数多」；regular 强调「有固定节奏」

**infrequent**
英/美 /ɪnˈfriːkwənt/
adj. 罕见的；不频繁的
[义] Not occurring often; rare. 发生次数少（infrequent service 班次稀少, infrequent contact 联系甚少）。与 frequent 相对。
[例] ① Bus service to the village is infrequent after eight in the evening. 晚上八点后，去村里的公交班次很少。 ② Infrequent meetings made it hard for the remote team to bond. 开会稀少，远程团队很难建立默契。
[族] infrequently 不常地, frequency 频率, frequent 频繁的
[搭] infrequent service/contact 班次稀少/联系甚少, relatively infrequent 相对少见, remain infrequent 仍然罕见
[联] frequent 频繁的, rare 罕见的, occasional 偶尔的, regular 有规律的, sporadic 零星的, common 常见的, habitual 习惯性的, constant 不断的
[记] infrequent vs. rare：infrequent 强调「不常发生」；rare 可指「珍贵、难得一见」

**encircled**
英/美 /ɪnˈsɜːrkld/ 英 /ɪnˈsɜːkld/
adj. 被包围的；环绕的
[义] Surrounded on all sides, often by enemies or a barrier. 被围住，常带军事或压迫感（encircled troops 被围部队, encircled by walls 围墙环绕）。与 surrounded 近义，encircled 更书面、常绑「成圈围住」。
[例] ① The battalion found itself encircled and short of supplies. 该营发现自己被包围且补给短缺。 ② The old town is encircled by a medieval stone wall. 古城被一道中世纪石墙环绕。
[族] encircle 包围, circle 圆圈
[搭] encircled by 被……环绕, encircled troops 被围部队, completely encircled 完全被包围
[联] surrounded 被包围的, trapped 困住的, enveloped 被笼罩的, besieged 被围困的, free 自由的, isolated 孤立的, ringed 成环的, enclosed 围住的
[记] encircled 新闻与军事语境常见。encircled vs. surrounded：encircled 更强调「成圈、成环」；surrounded 更中性

**nestled**
英/美 /ˈnesld/
adj. 坐落于……之中的；依偎的
[义] Situated comfortably within or against something; lying close and snug. 村庄、建筑依偎于自然景观中（a village nestled in the valley 山谷里的小村）；人或物依偎（nestled against 依偎着）。nestle 的过去分词作形容词，带温暖、隐蔽感。
[例] ① A small café is nestled between two bookshops on the quiet street. 这条安静街上，两家书店之间藏着一家小咖啡馆。 ② The child was nestled against her mother, already half asleep. 孩子依偎在母亲怀里，已半睡半醒。
[族] nestle 依偎, nest 巢
[搭] nestled in/between/among 坐落于/夹于/藏于, nestled against 依偎着, snugly nestled 舒适地依偎
[联] perched 位于高处的, surrounded 被环绕的, hidden 隐藏的, cozy 舒适的, exposed 暴露的, valley 山谷, hillside 山坡, tucked 藏着的
[记] nestled in the valley 旅游文案极频。nestled vs. perched：nestled 偏「舒适地藏于其中」；perched 偏「架在高处」

**elevated**
英/美 /ˈelɪveɪtɪd/
adj. 升高的；高架的；高尚的
[义] Raised above the ground or normal level; (formal) morally or intellectually superior. 物理上抬高（elevated platform 高架平台, elevated railway 高架铁路）；抽象上「高雅」（elevated tone 高雅语气）。elevate 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The track runs on an elevated line above the busy avenue. 轨道在繁忙大道上方的高架线上延伸。 ② The essay maintains an elevated tone without sounding pretentious. 这篇随笔语气高雅而不做作。
[族] elevate 抬高, elevation 海拔, elevator 电梯
[搭] elevated platform/railway 高架平台/铁路, elevated tone/risk 高雅语气/升高风险, elevated position 居高位置
[联] raised 抬高的, high 高的, grounded 着地的, lofty 崇高的, sunken 低洼的, platform 平台, level 水平的, perched 位于高处的
[记] elevated railway 英式常指高架轻轨。elevated risk/levels 指「升高的风险/水平」

**shackled**
英/美 /ˈʃækld/
adj. 被镣铐锁住的；受束缚的
[义] Restrained with chains or shackles; severely limited in freedom. 物理上戴镣铐（shackled prisoner 戴镣的囚犯）；比喻受制度、债务束缚（shackled by debt 被债务困住）。shackle 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The prisoners were led into court shackled and under guard. 囚犯戴镣铐、在警卫押送下进入法庭。 ② Small firms feel shackled by red tape and licensing rules. 小企业觉得被繁文缛节和许可规则捆住手脚。
[族] shackle 镣铐, unshackled 未受束缚的
[搭] shackled prisoner 戴镣囚犯, shackled by debt/rules 被债务/规则束缚, remain shackled 仍受束缚
[联] chained 锁链拴住的, trapped 困住的, bound 被束缚的, free 自由的, restrained 受约束的, tethered 拴住的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, liberated 获释的
[记] shackled by 比喻「被……束缚」。shackled vs. chained：shackled 专指「镣铐」；chained 更宽，可指各种链条

**fastened**
英/美 /ˈfæsnd/
adj. 系牢的；扣紧的
[义] Securely attached or closed. 安全带、扣子、门窗等已扣紧（keep fastened 保持系牢, tightly fastened 扣得很紧）。fasten 的过去分词作形容词；与 undone / loose 相对。
[例] ① Please keep your seatbelt fastened until the plane has stopped. 飞机停稳前请系好安全带。 ② The gate was fastened with a heavy padlock. 大门用一把大挂锁锁牢了。
[族] fasten 系牢, unfastened 未系牢的
[搭] keep fastened 保持系牢, fasten seatbelt 系紧安全带, fastened with 用……扣牢
[联] undone 松开的, loose 松的, tied 系住的, secured 固定的, locked 锁住的, attached 附着的, open 开着的, detached 分离的
[记] seatbelt fastened 航空广播极频。fastened vs. tied：fastened 偏「扣件、带扣固定」；tied 偏「绳结系住」

**flaccid**
英/美 /ˈflæsɪd/
adj. 松软无力的；萎靡的
[义] Soft and hanging loose; lacking firmness or energy. 肌肉、植物组织松软（flaccid muscles 松弛的肌肉, flaccid leaves 蔫叶）；比喻论点无力（a flaccid response 软弱的回应）。比 limp 更书面。
[例] ① The stems became flaccid after a day without water. 一天没浇水，茎就蔫软了。 ② Critics called the government's flaccid response inadequate. 批评者称政府反应软弱，远远不够。
[族] flaccidity 松软
[搭] flaccid muscles/leaves 松弛的肌肉/蔫叶, flaccid response 软弱回应, become flaccid 变得松软
[联] limp 无力的, drooping 低垂的, sagging 下坠的, stiff 僵硬的, firm 有力的, wilted 蔫了的, weak 弱的, rigid 僵硬的
[记] flaccid 医学、植物学常见；日常口语多用 limp / wilted。flaccid vs. limp：flaccid 更书面、偏「失去张力」

**sagging**
英/美 /ˈsæɡɪŋ/
adj. 下坠的；下垂的；萎靡的
[义] Sinking or hanging down under weight; declining in strength or value. 物理上下垂（sagging roof 下陷的屋顶, sagging skin 松弛的皮肤）；经济等下滑（sagging demand 疲软的需求）。sag 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① They propped up the sagging shelf before it collapsed. 他们在架子塌下来前用东西撑住。 ② Sagging consumer confidence worried retailers before the holiday season. 假日季前消费者信心低迷，零售商忧心忡忡。
[族] sag 下陷, saggy 下垂的（口语）
[搭] sagging roof/demand 下陷的屋顶/疲软需求, sagging skin 松弛皮肤, sagging spirits 低落情绪
[联] drooping 低垂的, limp 无力的, flaccid 松软的, firm 有力的, uplifted 上扬的, declining 下滑的, perky 精神抖擞的, weak 弱的
[记] sagging demand/economy 新闻常见。sagging vs. drooping：sagging 强调「承重下坠」；drooping 强调「往下耷」

**chained**
英/美 /tʃeɪnd/
adj. 被锁链拴住的；受束缚的
[义] Fastened with a chain; unable to move freely. 物理上被链拴住（chained dog 拴着的狗, chained bicycle 链锁锁着的自行车）；比喻受约束（chained to a desk 困在案头）。chain 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The bicycle was chained to the railing outside the station. 自行车用链锁拴在车站外的栏杆上。 ② She felt chained to a schedule that left no time for rest. 她觉得自己被排程捆死，毫无休息余地。
[族] chain 链条, unchained 未拴住的
[搭] chained to 拴在/困于, chained dog/bicycle 拴着的狗/链锁自行车, remain chained 仍被拴住
[联] shackled 戴镣的, tethered 拴住的, bound 被束缚的, free 自由的, locked 锁住的, trapped 困住的, attached 附着的, loose 松的
[记] chained to a desk 比喻「案头工作脱不开」。chained vs. shackled：chained 更日常；shackled 更绑镣铐、压迫感

**released**
英/美 /rɪˈliːst/
adj. 已释放的；已发布的
[义] Set free from confinement; made available to the public. 人从拘禁中获释（newly released prisoner 刚获释的囚犯）；影片、数据等已发布（newly released album 新发行的专辑）。release 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Newly released prisoners often need help finding housing and work. 刚获释者往往需要帮助解决住房和工作。 ② The newly released figures show unemployment falling slightly. 新发布的数据显示失业率略有下降。
[族] release 释放, unreleased 未发布的
[搭] newly released 新释放的/新发布的, released prisoner/album/data 获释囚犯/新专辑/已发布数据, remain unreleased 仍未发布
[联] freed 获释的, trapped 困住的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, published 已发布的, available 可用的, confined 受困的, leaked 泄露的, secret 秘密的
[记] newly released 可指「刚出狱」或「刚上架」。released vs. freed：released 更正式、常绑法律/官方程序；freed 更宽

**stranded**
英/美 /ˈstrændɪd/
adj. 被困的；滞留的
[义] Left without the means to move or continue; stuck in an inconvenient place. 交通中断而滞留（stranded passengers 滞留旅客, stranded abroad 困在国外）；孤立无援（feel stranded 感到走投无路）。strand 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Hundreds of passengers were stranded at the airport after flights were cancelled. 航班取消后，数百名旅客滞留在机场。 ② Without a car in the suburbs, she felt stranded on weekends. 郊区没车，她周末觉得哪儿也去不了。
[族] strand 使滞留
[搭] stranded passengers/travellers 滞留旅客, stranded abroad 困在国外, feel stranded 感到被困, leave stranded 使滞留
[联] trapped 困住的, stuck 卡住的, marooned 被困的, rescued 获救的, abandoned 被遗弃的, waiting 等待的, delayed 延误的, mobile 可移动的
[记] stranded passengers 新闻极频。stranded vs. stuck：stranded 强调「因外部原因无法离开某地」；stuck 更口语通用

**wedged**
英/美 /wedʒd/
adj. 楔入的；挤住的
[义] Fixed tightly in a narrow space; stuck by being pressed in. 被楔子或挤压力卡住（wedged door 卡住的门, wedged between 挤在……之间）。wedge 的过去分词作形容词；与 stuck / jammed 近义，wedged 更绑「狭缝挤入」。
[例] ① The drawer was wedged shut by something behind it. 抽屉被后面的东西顶死，拉不开。 ② We were wedged between two trucks in heavy traffic. 堵车时我们被夹在两辆卡车中间。
[族] wedge 楔子
[搭] wedged shut/stuck 卡死, wedged between 挤在……之间, tightly wedged 紧紧楔入
[联] stuck 卡住的, jammed 塞满的, trapped 困住的, loose 松的, squeezed 挤压的, fixed 固定的, narrow 窄的, free 自由的
[记] wedged between 物理与比喻均可用。wedged vs. jammed：wedged 偏「狭缝挤入、楔住」；jammed 偏「塞满、堵死」

**lodged**
英/美 /lɑːdʒd/ 英 /lɒdʒd/
adj. 卡住的；嵌入的；暂住的
[义] Stuck firmly in a place; embedded; staying temporarily. 异物嵌入（lodged bullet 嵌在体内的子弹）；投诉正式提交（lodged complaint 已提交的投诉）；短暂住宿（lodged with friends 暂住朋友家）。lodge 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Surgeons removed a lodged fragment from his knee. 外科医生取出了嵌在他膝里的碎片。 ② A formal complaint was lodged with the housing authority. 已向住房管理部门正式提交投诉。
[族] lodge 嵌入, lodging 住宿
[搭] lodged bullet/fragment 嵌入的子弹/碎片, lodged complaint 已提交的投诉, lodged with 暂住于
[联] stuck 卡住的, embedded 嵌入的, wedged 楔入的, trapped 困住的, loose 松的, fixed 固定的, submitted 已提交的, removed 取出的
[记] lodged complaint 英式正式语体常见。lodged vs. wedged：lodged 可指「嵌入体内」或「提交文件」；wedged 专指「挤卡」

**rooted**
英/美 /ˈruːtɪd/
adj. 扎根的；根深蒂固的
[义] Fixed firmly in the ground or in one's mind; unable or unwilling to move. 植物扎根（firmly rooted trees 根系扎实的树）；观念、习惯根深蒂固（deeply rooted beliefs 根深蒂固的信念）；人愣住不动（rooted to the spot 愣在原地）。root 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Oaks are deeply rooted and can survive strong winds. 橡树根系深，能扛住大风。 ② Discrimination can be rooted in ignorance as much as in malice. 歧视既可能源于恶意，也可能源于无知。
[族] root 根, rootless 无根的
[搭] deeply rooted 根深蒂固的, rooted in 植根于, rooted to the spot 愣在原地, firmly rooted 牢牢扎根
[联] anchored 锚定的, entrenched 根深蒂固的, ingrained 根深蒂固的, fixed 固定的, grounded 着地的, mobile 可移动的, shallow 浅的, tradition 传统
[记] rooted to the spot 指「惊得挪不动步」。rooted in 极频：rooted in tradition/culture

**ingrained**
英/美 /ɪnˈɡreɪnd/
adj. 根深蒂固的；彻入的
[义] Firmly fixed in someone's mind or habits; deeply embedded. 习惯、偏见、态度难以改变（ingrained habit 根深蒂固的习惯, ingrained prejudice 根深蒂固的偏见）。比 rooted 更绑「长期熏染、难以根除」。
[例] ① Punctuality was an ingrained habit after years in the military. 军旅多年，守时成了根深蒂固的习惯。 ② The report examined ingrained biases in hiring practices. 报告审视了招聘中根深蒂固的偏见。
[族] ingrain 使根深蒂固
[搭] ingrained habit/prejudice/bias 根深蒂固的习惯/偏见, deeply ingrained 深深植入的, ingrained in 深植于
[联] rooted 扎根的, entrenched 根深蒂固的, habitual 习惯性的, deep-seated 深层的, superficial 表面的, fixed 固定的, changeable 可变的, prejudice 偏见
[记] ingrained in 表「深植于（文化、组织）」。ingrained vs. rooted：ingrained 更绑习惯与态度；rooted 可指物理扎根

**entrenched**
英/美 /ɪnˈtrentʃt/
adj. 根深蒂固的；牢固确立的
[义] Firmly established and difficult to change, especially opinions, power, or privilege. 态度、利益、权力结构难以撼动（entrenched attitudes 根深蒂固的态度, entrenched interests 既得利益）。本义来自战壕（trench），引申「像壕沟一样牢固」。
[例] ① Entrenched attitudes toward gender roles are slow to shift. 对性别角色的根深蒂固看法转变缓慢。 ② Reformers struggled against entrenched interests in the industry. 改革者与行业既得利益集团艰难博弈。
[源] 源自 trench（战壕）→ 躲进壕沟、固守 → 根深蒂固的。
[族] entrench 使根深蒂固, trench 战壕
[搭] entrenched attitudes/interests 根深蒂固的态度/既得利益, deeply entrenched 深深固化的, remain entrenched 仍然根深蒂固
[联] ingrained 根深蒂固的, rooted 扎根的, established 确立的, rigid 僵化的, flexible 灵活的, reform 改革, privilege 特权, power 权力
[记] entrenched interests 政治经济高频，指「既得利益」。entrenched vs. ingrained：entrenched 更绑权力、制度、利益结构

**freed**
英/美 /friːd/
adj. 获释的；被解放的
[义] Released from captivity, restraint, or obligation. 从拘禁、束缚或义务中解脱（newly freed hostages 刚获释的人质, freed from debt 摆脱债务）。free 的过去分词作形容词；与 released 近义，freed 更宽、更口语。
[例] ① Newly freed prisoners need support finding housing and work. 刚获释者需要帮衬解决住房和工作。 ② She felt freed from the pressure to please everyone. 她觉得自己不必再讨好所有人，松了口气。
[族] free 自由的, freedom 自由, freedman 获释奴隶（史）
[搭] newly freed 刚获释的, freed from 从……中解脱, freed hostage/prisoner 获释人质/囚犯
[联] released 已释放的, trapped 困住的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, liberated 获解放的, bound 被束缚的, confined 受困的, independent 独立的, captive 被俘的
[记] freed from 极频：freed from worry/debt。freed vs. released：freed 更口语、可指抽象解脱；released 更正式

**imprisoned**
英/美 /ɪmˈprɪznd/
adj. 被囚禁的；身陷……的
[义] Kept in prison; confined in a narrow situation. 关在狱中（wrongly imprisoned 被错判入狱）；比喻困在某种处境（imprisoned by routine 被日常困住）。imprison 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Wrongly imprisoned activists were finally exonerated after new evidence emerged. 新证据出现后，被错判入狱的活动人士终于洗冤。 ② She felt imprisoned in a job that offered no growth. 她觉得困在一份没有发展的工作里。
[族] imprison 监禁, imprisonment 监禁, prisoner 囚犯
[搭] wrongly imprisoned 被错判入狱, imprisoned by 被……困住, remain imprisoned 仍被囚禁
[联] trapped 困住的, confined 受困的, freed 获释的, released 已释放的, captive 被俘的, shackled 戴镣的, free 自由的, routine 日常
[记] imprisoned by 比喻「被……困住」。imprisoned vs. trapped：imprisoned 更绑「监禁、失去自由」；trapped 更口语通用

**restricted**
英/美 /rɪˈstrɪktɪd/
adj. 受限制的；限定的
[义] Limited in size, amount, or access; available only to certain people. 范围、数量、进入受限（restricted area 禁区, restricted access 限制访问）；饮食等有限制（restricted diet 限制饮食）。restrict 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Staff may not enter the restricted area without a security pass. 无安全通行证，员工不得进入禁区。 ② The patient was put on a restricted diet after surgery. 术后患者须限制饮食。
[族] restrict 限制, restriction 限制, unrestricted 不受限制的
[搭] restricted area/access 禁区/限制访问, restricted diet 限制饮食, restricted to 限于, highly restricted 高度受限
[联] unrestricted 不受限制的, limited 有限的, confined 受困的, open 开放的, banned 禁止的, allowed 允许的, exclusive 专属的, free 自由的
[记] restricted to 表「仅限于……」。restricted area 机场、军事、工地常见。restricted vs. limited：restricted 强调「规则禁止/限定」；limited 更宽

**unrestricted**
英/美 /ˌʌnrɪˈstrɪktɪd/
adj. 不受限制的；无约束的
[义] Not subject to limits on access, use, or movement. 无进入或使用限制（unrestricted access 无限制访问, unrestricted use 随意使用）。与 restricted 相对。
[例] ① The archive grants unrestricted access to researchers with valid credentials. 档案馆向持有效证件的研究者完全开放。 ② Unrestricted fishing in the bay has depleted local stocks. 海湾无限制捕捞已使本地鱼种减少。
[族] restrict 限制, restricted 受限制的
[搭] unrestricted access/use 无限制访问/使用, unrestricted movement 自由行动, largely unrestricted 大体不受限
[联] restricted 受限制的, unlimited 无限的, open 开放的, free 自由的, limited 有限的, confined 受困的, exclusive 专属的, regulated 受管制的
[记] unrestricted access 技术、档案、政策语境常见。unrestricted vs. unlimited：unrestricted 偏「无规则限制」；unlimited 偏「数量/程度无上限」

**drained**
英/美 /dreɪnd/
adj. 耗尽的；排空的
[义] Empty of liquid; extremely tired or depleted of resources. 液体被排干（drained battery 耗尽的电池, drained pool 放空的池）；精力、资源耗尽（feel drained 感到精疲力竭）。drain 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The phone was completely drained after a day of navigation and video calls. 导航和视频通话用了一天后，手机彻底没电。 ② After the long shift, she felt emotionally drained. 长班结束后，她感到心力交瘁。
[族] drain 排出, drainage 排水, draining 耗尽的
[搭] feel drained 感到耗尽, drained battery/pool 耗尽的电池/放空的池, emotionally drained 心力交瘁, completely drained 完全耗尽
[联] depleted 耗尽的, exhausted 精疲力竭的, empty 空的, full 满的, refreshed 恢复精力的, tired 疲惫的, charged 充电的, saturated 饱和的
[记] feel drained 口语极频。drained vs. exhausted：drained 偏「被抽干、掏空」；exhausted 偏「累到极限」

**unfilled**
英/美 /ʌnˈfɪld/
adj. 未填满的；空缺的
[义] Not filled to capacity; (of a job) not yet occupied. 容器、空间未装满（unfilled seats 空座, unfilled orders 未交付订单）；职位空缺（unfilled vacancy 空缺职位）。与 full / filled 相对。
[例] ① Several unfilled seats remained at the back of the hall. 大厅后面仍有几排空座。 ② The role has been unfilled for six months despite repeated hiring attempts. 尽管多次招聘，该岗位已空缺六个月。
[族] fill 填满, filled 满的, unfilled 未填满的
[搭] unfilled seats/orders/vacancy 空座/未交付订单/空缺, remain unfilled 仍然空缺, partially unfilled 部分未填满
[联] full 满的, vacant 空的, empty 空的, occupied 占用的, filled 满的, available 可用的, open 空缺的, complete 完整的
[记] unfilled vacancy/order 商务常见。unfilled vs. vacant：unfilled 可指「未装满」或「职位未招到」；vacant 更绑「空着、无人占用」

**folded**
英/美 /ˈfoʊldɪd/ 英 /ˈfəʊldɪd/
adj. 折叠的；合拢的
[义] Bent so that one part lies over another; closed by folding. 纸、布、肢体等叠起（a folded letter 折好的信, folded arms 双臂交叉）；合拢（folded wings 收拢的翅膀）。fold 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① She handed him a folded map with the route marked in red. 她递给他一张折好的地图，路线标了红。 ② He listened with folded arms and a sceptical expression. 他双臂交叉，一脸怀疑地听着。
[搭] folded arms/letter/map 交叉双臂/折好的信/地图, neatly folded 折得整齐, folded in half 对折
[联] unfolded 展开的, wrapped 包裹的, flat 平的, creased 有褶的, open 开着的, bent 弯的, crossed 交叉的, spread 展开的
[记] folded arms 表「双臂交叉、防备或怀疑」。folded vs. bent：folded 专指「叠层」；bent 更宽，指「弯折」

**unfolded**
英/美 /ʌnˈfoʊldɪd/ 英 /ʌnˈfəʊldɪd/
adj. 展开的；打开的
[义] Not folded; spread out or opened up. 地图、纸张、翅膀等已展开（an unfolded newspaper 摊开的报纸, unfolded wings 展开的翅膀）。与 folded 相对。
[例] ① The unfolded blueprint covered most of the table. 展开的蓝图占了大半张桌子。 ② Butterflies rest with unfolded wings to warm in the sun. 蝴蝶停落时展开翅膀晒太阳。
[族] unfold 展开, fold 折叠, folded 折叠的
[搭] unfolded map/newspaper 展开的地图/报纸, unfolded wings 展开的翅膀, lay unfolded 摊开
[联] folded 折叠的, spread 展开的, open 开着的, flat 平的, wrapped 包裹的, creased 有褶的, extended 伸展开的, closed 关闭的
[记] unfolded vs. open：unfolded 专指「从折叠状态展开」；open 更宽

**bolted**
英/美 /ˈboʊltɪd/
adj. 栓上的；闩住的
[义] Fastened with a bolt or latch; (door) secured from the inside. 用螺栓或门闩固定（bolted door 闩上的门, bolted shelf 螺栓固定的架子）。bolt 的过去分词作形容词；与 locked 近义，bolted 更绑「门闩/螺栓」。
[例] ① The back door was bolted from the inside for the night. 后门从里面闩上，过夜。 ② Heavy equipment must be bolted to the floor in the lab. 实验室里重型设备须用螺栓固定在地面上。
[族] bolt 螺栓, unbolt 拔门闩
[搭] bolted door/gate 闩上的门/大门, bolted to the floor 固定在地面上, tightly bolted 栓得很牢
[联] locked 锁住的, fastened 系牢的, secured 固定的, open 开着的, loose 松的, screwed 拧上的, latched 闩上的, fixed 固定的
[记] bolt the door 英式常指「闩门」。bolted vs. locked：bolted 靠门闩/螺栓；locked 靠锁

**glued**
英/美 /ɡluːd/
adj. 粘住的；紧盯着……的
[义] Fixed with glue; (informal) unable to look away from something. 用胶黏牢（glued joints 胶合处）；口语「眼睛离不开」（glued to the screen 盯着屏幕）。glue 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The broken vase was carefully glued back together. 碎花瓶被小心地粘了回去。 ② Kids were glued to the screen throughout the entire movie. 孩子们整部电影都盯着屏幕。
[族] glue 胶水, unglued 未粘住的
[搭] glued to the screen 盯着屏幕, glued together 粘在一起, glued joints 胶合处
[联] stuck 卡住的, attached 附着的, fixed 固定的, loose 松的, nailed 钉住的, pinned 别住的, absorbed 全神贯注的, detached 分离的
[记] glued to 口语极频：glued to the phone/TV。glued vs. stuck：glued 专指「用胶黏」；stuck 更宽

**pinned**
英/美 /pɪnd/
adj. 别住的；钉住的；被压制的
[义] Held in place with a pin or similar fastener; unable to move (informal). 用别针、图钉固定（pinned badge 别上的徽章）；被压住动弹不得（pinned under debris 被压在废墟下）。pin 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A pinned note on the board listed the week's deadlines. 布告栏上钉了张便条，列着本周截止日。 ② Rescuers found the driver pinned behind the steering wheel. 救援人员在方向盘后发现了被卡住的司机。
[族] pin 别针, unpin 取下别针
[搭] pinned badge/note 别上的徽章/钉住的便条, pinned under 被压在……下, pinned down 被压制
[联] nailed 钉住的, glued 粘住的, fastened 系牢的, stuck 卡住的, trapped 困住的, loose 松的, fixed 固定的, attached 附着的
[记] pinned down 可指物理「被压住」，也可指「被问住、被钉死在某话题」。pinned vs. nailed：pinned 用针/钉；nailed 更绑锤子钉入

**armed**
英/美 /ɑːrmd/ 英 /ɑːmd/
adj. 武装的；配备……的
[义] Carrying weapons; equipped with something useful. 携带武器（armed guard 武装警卫, armed robbery 持械抢劫）；配备工具或信息（armed with facts 手握事实）。arm 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Armed guards patrolled the entrance to the embassy. 武装警卫在大使馆入口巡逻。 ② She went into the meeting armed with detailed notes. 她带着详细笔记去开会。
[族] arm 武装, arms 武器, unarmed 无武装的
[搭] armed guard/robbery 武装警卫/持械抢劫, armed with 配备/手握, heavily armed 全副武装
[联] unarmed 无武装的, weapon 武器, equipped 配备的, dangerous 危险的, protected 受保护的, loaded 上膛的, prepared 有准备的, bare 赤手的
[记] armed with 比喻「手握、配备」：armed with data/questions。armed vs. loaded：armed 指「携带武器」；loaded 可指「上膛、满载」

**unarmed**
英/美 /ʌnˈɑːrmd/ 英 /ʌnˈɑːmd/
adj. 无武装的；赤手空拳的
[义] Not carrying weapons. 未携带武器（unarmed civilian 手无寸铁的平民, unarmed combat 徒手格斗）。与 armed 相对。
[例] ① Witnesses said the suspect was unarmed when arrested. 目击者称嫌疑人被捕时未持武器。 ② Unarmed civilians were caught in the crossfire. 手无寸铁的平民卷入交火。
[族] arm 武装, armed 武装的
[搭] unarmed civilian/combat 无武装平民/徒手格斗, remain unarmed 仍未武装, allegedly unarmed 据称未持械
[联] armed 武装的, weaponless 无武器的, civilian 平民, dangerous 危险的, peaceful 和平的, bare-handed 赤手的, protected 受保护的, vulnerable 脆弱的
[记] unarmed civilian 新闻与人权语境常见。unarmed vs. defenceless：unarmed 专指「无武器」；defenceless 更宽，指「无法自卫」

**unshackled**
英/美 /ʌnˈʃækld/
adj. 未戴镣的；不受束缚的
[义] Not restrained by shackles; free from severe restrictions. 物理上未戴镣铐；比喻不受严重约束（unshackled creativity 不受束缚的创造力）。与 shackled 相对。
[例] ① The reform left markets relatively unshackled from old price controls. 改革使市场相对摆脱了旧价格管制。 ② She wrote her best work when she felt creatively unshackled. 她在创作不受束缚时写出了最好的作品。
[族] shackle 镣铐, shackled 戴镣的
[搭] creatively unshackled 创作上不受束缚, relatively unshackled 相对不受限, leave unshackled 不加束缚
[联] shackled 戴镣的, freed 获释的, restricted 受限制的, bound 被束缚的, free 自由的, liberated 获解放的, chained 锁链拴住的, constrained 受约束的
[记] unshackled 比喻义新闻、评论常见。unshackled vs. unrestricted：unshackled 带「摆脱枷锁」画面；unrestricted 更中性

**unfastened**
英/美 /ʌnˈfæsnd/
adj. 未系牢的；松开的
[义] Not securely closed or tied. 安全带、扣子、门窗等未扣紧（unfastened seatbelt 未系安全带, unfastened coat 未扣好的外套）。与 fastened 相对。
[例] ① The inspector noted an unfastened safety harness on the scaffold. 检查员发现脚手架上有一条安全带没系好。 ② Her coat hung unfastened in the wind as she ran for the bus. 她跑去赶公交时，外套在风里敞着没扣。
[族] fasten 系牢, fastened 系牢的
[搭] unfastened seatbelt/harness 未系安全带/安全绳, unfastened coat 未扣好的外套, leave unfastened 不扣紧
[联] fastened 系牢的, undone 松开的, loose 松的, tied 系住的, secured 固定的, open 开着的, locked 锁住的, tight 紧的
[记] unfastened seatbelt 交通安全提醒常见。unfastened vs. undone：unfastened 偏「扣件未扣」；undone 可指鞋带、扣子松开

**marooned**
英/美 /məˈruːnd/
adj. 被困的；孤立的
[义] Left alone in an isolated place, especially an island; stranded without help. 困在孤岛或偏远处（marooned on an island 困在岛上）；比喻被撇下、孤立无援（marooned at the office 独自困在办公室）。比 stranded 更带「被遗弃、隔绝」感。
[例] ① The sailors were marooned on a tiny island for three weeks. 水手们在一座小岛上困了三个星期。 ② After the last train left, we were marooned in the suburbs with no taxi in sight. 末班车走后，我们困在郊区，看不见出租车。
[族] maroon 使困住
[搭] marooned on an island 困在岛上, feel marooned 感到被抛弃, marooned at 困在
[联] stranded 滞留的, trapped 困住的, isolated 孤立的, abandoned 被遗弃的, rescued 获救的, alone 单独的, deserted 被遗弃的, stuck 卡住的
[记] marooned 本义来自「放逐到荒岛」。marooned vs. stranded：marooned 更绑「被留下、隔绝」；stranded 偏「交通中断滞留」

**besieged**
英/美 /bɪˈsiːdʒd/
adj. 被围困的；应接不暇的
[义] Surrounded by armed forces; overwhelmed by requests or problems. 军事上被围攻（besieged city 被围城市）；比喻被问题、电话、请求淹没（besieged by calls 电话应接不暇）。besiege 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Aid convoys struggled to reach the besieged town. 援助车队难以抵达被围城镇。 ② After the announcement, the helpline was besieged by angry callers. 公告发布后，求助热线被愤怒来电淹没。
[族] besiege 围困, siege 围攻
[搭] besieged city/town 被围城市/城镇, besieged by calls/questions 被电话/问题淹没, feel besieged 感到应接不暇
[联] encircled 被包围的, surrounded 被环绕的, trapped 困住的, overwhelmed 被压垮的, attacked 遭攻击的, safe 安全的, free 自由的, isolated 孤立的
[记] besieged by 比喻「被……围攻/淹没」极频。besieged vs. encircled：besieged 带「持续攻击、压力」；encircled 仅指「围住」

**unconfined**
英/美 /ˌʌnkənˈfaɪnd/
adj. 不受困的；自由的
[义] Not kept in a confined space; free to move or develop. 不在封闭空间内（unconfined space 开放空间）；比喻不受拘束（unconfined joy 尽情释放的喜悦）。与 confined（距离章）相对，本章收「不受限状态」。
[例] ① Gas can expand freely in an unconfined space. 气体在开放空间可自由膨胀。 ② The dancers moved with unconfined energy across the stage. 舞者以毫无拘束的活力在台上移动。
[族] confine 限制, confined 受困的
[搭] unconfined space/joy 开放空间/尽情释放的喜悦, remain unconfined 仍不受困, largely unconfined 大体不受限
[联] confined 受困的, free 自由的, unrestricted 不受限制的, open 开放的, trapped 困住的, bound 被束缚的, limited 有限的, spacious 宽敞的
[记] unconfined 地质语境指「未封闭的地层/空间」。unconfined vs. unrestricted：unconfined 偏「物理上不封闭」；unrestricted 偏「规则不限制」

**missing**
英/美 /ˈmɪsɪŋ/
adj. 缺失的；失踪的
[义] Not present; unable to be found. 某物不在该在的位置（missing page 缺页, missing data 缺失数据）；人下落不明（missing person 失踪人员）。miss 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① One missing page made the contract impossible to verify. 缺了一页，合同无法核实。 ② Rescue teams searched the hills for missing hikers. 救援队在山区搜寻失踪徒步者。
[族] miss 错过, missingness 缺失
[搭] missing person/page/data 失踪人员/缺页/缺失数据, go missing 失踪/丢失, reported missing 报失踪
[联] absent 缺席的, present 在场的, lost 丢失的, found 找到的, incomplete 不完整的, available 可用的, gone 不见的, intact 完好的
[记] go missing 英式「丢失、失踪」极频。missing vs. absent：missing 强调「找不到、不知在哪」；absent 强调「不在场」

**tied**
英/美 /taɪd/
adj. 系住的；捆绑的；平局的
[义] Fastened with string or rope; equal in score. 用绳等系牢（tied bundle 捆好的包裹, hands tied 双手被绑）；比分相同（tied game 平局）。tie 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The tied bundle of newspapers sat on the doorstep. 一捆扎好的报纸放在门阶上。 ② The teams remained tied at one goal each after extra time. 加时赛后双方仍一比一平。
[族] tie 系, untied 未系住的, tie 平局（名词）
[搭] tied bundle/hands 捆好的包裹/被绑的双手, tied game/score 平局, tied to 系于/绑于
[联] untied 未系住的, fastened 系牢的, bound 被束缚的, loose 松的, knotted 打结的, tied up 被占用的, even 平的, secured 固定的
[记] tied game/score 体育「平局」。tied to 可指物理系住，也可指「与……绑定」（tied to a contract）

**untied**
英/美 /ʌnˈtaɪd/
adj. 未系住的；松开的
[义] Not fastened with a knot or bow; loose. 鞋带、蝴蝶结、绳索等未系（untied shoelaces 松开的鞋带, come untied 松开了）。与 tied 相对。
[例] ① He tripped on his untied shoelaces running for the train. 他跑去赶火车时被松开的鞋带绊了一下。 ② The boat drifted away when the rope came untied. 绳子松开后，小船漂走了。
[族] tie 系, tied 系住的, untie 解开
[搭] untied shoelaces/bow 松开的鞋带/蝴蝶结, come untied 松开了, leave untied 不系
[联] tied 系住的, loose 松的, undone 松开的, fastened 系牢的, knotted 打结的, secure 牢固的, drifting 漂流的, bound 被束缚的
[记] come untied 固定说法。untied vs. undone：untied 专指「绳结、鞋带」；undone 可指扣子、拉链

**latched**
英/美 /lætʃt/
adj. 闩上的；锁扣扣住的
[义] Fastened with a latch. 门、窗、箱子等用门闩或搭扣扣住（latched gate 闩上的大门, latched suitcase 扣好的行李箱）。latch 的过去分词作形容词；与 bolted / locked 近义，latched 更绑「搭扣、门闩」。
[例] ① Make sure the gate stays latched so the dog cannot get out. 请确保大门闩好，别让狗跑出去。 ② She travelled with a latched leather case at her side. 她随身带着一只扣好的皮箱。
[族] latch 门闩, unlatched 未闩上的
[搭] latched gate/door/case 闩上的大门/门/箱子, keep latched 保持闩上, tightly latched 扣得很牢
[联] bolted 栓上的, locked 锁住的, fastened 系牢的, unlatched 未闩上的, open 开着的, shut 关着的, secured 固定的, loose 松的
[记] latched vs. bolted：latched 多用搭扣、门闩；bolted 多用螺栓或粗门闩。keep the door latched 口语常见

**unlatched**
英/美 /ʌnˈlætʃt/
adj. 未闩上的；扣开的
[义] Not fastened with a latch. 门、窗、容器等搭扣未扣（an unlatched window 未闩的窗, leave unlatched 不闩上）。与 latched 相对。
[例] ① An unlatched window rattled in the wind all night. 一扇没闩的窗整夜在风中哐啷响。 ② The toddler pushed through an unlatched gate into the street. 幼儿推开未闩的大门走到了街上。
[族] latch 门闩, latched 闩上的
[搭] unlatched window/gate 未闩的窗/大门, leave unlatched 不闩上, remain unlatched 仍未闩上
[联] latched 闩上的, open 开着的, unlocked 未锁的, shut 关着的, loose 松的, secured 固定的, ajar 半开的, fastened 系牢的
[记] leave unlatched 安全提醒常见。unlatched vs. unlocked：unlatched 专指「搭扣/门闩未扣」；unlocked 指「锁未上」

**screwed**
英/美 /skruːd/
adj. 拧上的；用螺丝固定的
[义] Fastened with a screw or screws; (slang) in trouble. 用螺丝拧紧（screwed lid 拧紧的盖子, screwed together 螺丝固定）；俚语「完蛋了、搞砸了」（I'm screwed 我完了）。screw 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The panel must remain screwed to the frame during transport. 运输期间面板须用螺丝固定在框架上。 ② Without backup, we were screwed when the server crashed. 服务器一崩，我们又没备份，彻底完了。
[族] screw 螺丝, unscrewed 未拧上的
[搭] screwed together/lid 螺丝固定/拧紧的盖子, screwed to 拧在……上, screwed on tight 拧得很紧
[联] bolted 栓上的, nailed 钉住的, glued 粘住的, unscrewed 未拧上的, fixed 固定的, loose 松的, tight 紧的, attached 附着的
[记] screwed on/off 指「拧上/拧开」。俚语 be screwed 指「处境不妙」，正式写作慎用。screwed vs. bolted：screwed 用螺丝；bolted 用螺栓/门闩

**nailed**
英/美 /neɪld/
adj. 钉住的； nailed down 确定的
[义] Fixed with nails; (informal) settled or certain. 用钉子钉牢（nailed boards 钉好的木板）；口语「钉死、确定」（nailed down the details 敲定细节）。nail 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The shelf was nailed directly to the wall studs. 架子直接钉在墙龙骨上。 ② Once the price is nailed down, we can sign the contract. 价格一敲定，我们就能签合同。
[族] nail 钉子
[搭] nailed to the wall 钉在墙上, nailed down 敲定的, nailed shut 钉死的
[联] screwed 拧上的, glued 粘住的, pinned 别住的, fixed 固定的, loose 松的, hammered 锤入的, attached 附着的, settled 确定的
[记] nailed down 商务「敲定」。nailed shut 指「用钉子封死」。nailed vs. screwed：nailed 用钉；screwed 用螺丝

**hooked**
英/美 /hʊkt/
adj. 钩住的； hooked on 上瘾的
[义] Caught or fastened with a hook; (informal) addicted or very keen on something. 用钩子挂住（hooked rug 钩针地毯, hooked on a nail 挂在钉上）；口语「上瘾、着迷」（hooked on coffee 咖啡上瘾）。hook 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The coat was hooked over the back of the chair. 外套钩在椅背上。 ② Many users become hooked on short-form video apps. 许多用户对短视频应用上了瘾。
[族] hook 钩, unhooked 未钩住的
[搭] hooked on 对……上瘾/着迷, hooked over 钩在……上, hooked rug 钩针地毯, get hooked 上瘾
[联] unhooked 未钩住的, attached 附着的, hung 挂着的, addicted 上瘾的, caught 被钩住的, loose 松的, pinned 别住的, keen 热衷的
[记] hooked on 极频：hooked on drugs/games。hooked vs. hung：hooked 强调「钩住」；hung 更宽，指「悬挂」

**unhooked**
英/美 /ʌnˈhʊkt/
adj. 未钩住的；脱钩的
[义] Not attached by a hook; disconnected. 未挂钩、已脱钩（unhooked trailer 脱钩的拖车, unhooked cable 未接好的线缆）。与 hooked 相对。
[例] ① The trailer came unhooked on the steep hill. 拖车在陡坡上脱钩了。 ② Leave the cable unhooked until the technician arrives. 技术员到达前别接线缆。
[族] hook 钩, hooked 钩住的
[搭] come unhooked 脱钩, unhooked trailer/cable 脱钩拖车/未接线缆, leave unhooked 不挂钩
[联] hooked 钩住的, detached 分离的, connected 连接的, loose 松的, attached 附着的, linked 相连的, disconnected 断开的, secured 固定的
[记] come unhooked 指「脱钩、松开」。unhooked vs. disconnected：unhooked 专指「钩扣分离」；disconnected 更宽

**rolled**
英/美 /roʊld/ 英 /rəʊld/
adj. 卷起的；轧制的；滚动的
[义] Turned over on itself to form a cylinder; processed by rolling; moving on wheels. 纸、地毯等卷起（rolled carpet 卷起的地毯, rolled-up sleeves 卷起的袖子）；金属等轧制（rolled steel 轧钢）；窗户可滚动开合（rolled-down window 摇下的窗）。roll 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① He stored the rolled map in a cardboard tube. 他把卷好的地图放进纸筒里。 ② Rolled steel is used for beams in modern construction. 现代建筑用轧钢做梁。
[族] roll 卷, unrolled 展开的
[搭] rolled carpet/map 卷起的地毯/地图, rolled-up sleeves 卷起的袖子, rolled steel 轧钢, rolled-down window 摇下的窗
[联] unrolled 展开的, folded 折叠的, wrapped 包裹的, flat 平的, cylindrical 圆柱形的, spread 展开的, coiled 盘绕的, pressed 压制的
[记] rolled-up 常作复合形容词。rolled vs. folded：rolled 卷成筒；folded 叠层

**unrolled**
英/美 /ʌnˈroʊld/ 英 /ʌnˈrəʊld/
adj. 展开的；铺开的
[义] Not rolled up; spread out flat. 地图、地毯、胶卷等已展开（an unrolled scroll 展开的卷轴, unrolled film 已冲洗的胶卷）。与 rolled 相对。
[例] ① The unrolled blueprint covered the entire workbench. 展开的蓝图占满了整张工作台。 ② She watched the unrolled film dry on the line. 她看着冲好的胶卷挂在绳上晾干。
[族] roll 卷, rolled 卷起的, unroll 展开
[搭] unrolled map/scroll 展开的地图/卷轴, lay unrolled 摊开, fully unrolled 完全展开
[联] rolled 卷起的, spread 展开的, flat 平的, folded 折叠的, wrapped 包裹的, extended 伸展开的, coiled 盘绕的, closed 关闭的
[记] unrolled vs. unfolded：unrolled 专指「从卷状展开」；unfolded 指「从折叠展开」

**clipped**
英/美 /klɪpt/
adj. 夹住的；剪短的；简略的
[义] Fastened with a clip; cut short; abrupt in speech. 用夹子固定（clipped papers 夹在一起的纸）；头发、指甲剪短（clipped hedge 修剪过的树篱）；说话短促（clipped tones 简短语气）。clip 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A clipped stack of receipts sat on the accountant's desk. 会计桌上夹着一叠收据。 ② He answered in clipped sentences, clearly impatient. 他用短句回答，显然不耐烦。
[族] clip 夹子, unclipped 未夹住的
[搭] clipped papers/hedge 夹住的纸/修剪的树篱, clipped tones 简短语气, clipped wings 被剪翅（比喻受限制）
[联] pinned 别住的, trimmed 修剪过的, short 短的, unclipped 未夹的, fastened 系牢的, abrupt 突兀的, attached 附着的, loose 松的
[记] clipped tones 指「短促、冷淡的语气」。clipped wings 比喻「受束缚、无法施展」。clipped vs. trimmed：clipped 可指「夹子夹」或「剪短」

**zipped**
英/美 /zɪpt/
adj. 拉链拉上的； zipped up 系紧的
[义] Closed with a zip or zipper. 用拉链合上（zipped pocket 有拉链的口袋, zipped up 拉链拉好）。zip 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Keep valuables in a zipped inner pocket while travelling. 旅行时把贵重物品放在内层拉链袋里。 ② She left the tent fully zipped against the mosquitoes. 她把帐篷拉链全拉上以防蚊子。
[族] zip 拉链, unzip 拉开拉链, unzipped 拉链未拉的
[搭] zipped pocket/jacket 拉链袋/拉链外套, zipped up 拉链拉好, keep zipped 保持拉上
[联] unzipped 拉链未拉的, buttoned 扣好的, fastened 系牢的, open 开着的, closed 关闭的, sealed 密封的, loose 松的, tight 紧的
[记] zipped up 口语「把拉链拉好」。zipped vs. buttoned：zipped 用拉链；buttoned 用纽扣

**buttoned**
英/美 /ˈbʌtnd/
adj. 扣好的； buttoned-up 保守拘谨的
[义] Fastened with buttons. 纽扣扣好（buttoned shirt 扣好的衬衫, buttoned coat 扣严的大衣）；比喻「保守、拘谨」（a buttoned-up culture 拘谨的文化）。button 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① He arrived in a neatly buttoned shirt despite the heat. 尽管天热，他仍穿着扣得整齐的衬衫。 ② The firm has a buttoned-up approach to public relations. 这家公司在公关上作风保守拘谨。
[族] button 纽扣, unbuttoned 未扣的
[搭] buttoned shirt/coat 扣好的衬衫/大衣, buttoned up 扣严/拘谨的, top buttoned 最上一颗扣好
[联] unbuttoned 未扣的, zipped 拉链拉上的, fastened 系牢的, open 开着的, neat 整洁的, formal 正式的, loose 松的, conservative 保守的
[记] buttoned-up 英式可指「衣着整齐、性格拘谨」。buttoned vs. fastened：buttoned 专指纽扣；fastened 更宽

**unbuttoned**
英/美 /ʌnˈbʌtnd/
adj. 未扣的； relaxed 放松的
[义] With buttons undone; informal and relaxed in manner. 扣子未扣（unbuttoned collar 敞开的领子）；比喻「放松、不拘束」（unbuttoned conversation 畅快的交谈）。与 buttoned 相对。
[例] ① He loosened his tie and left his collar unbuttoned after work. 下班后他松开领带，领口也没扣。 ② The interview became more unbuttoned once the cameras stopped. 镜头一停，采访气氛就松下来了。
[族] button 纽扣, buttoned 扣好的
[搭] unbuttoned collar/shirt 敞开的领子/未扣的衬衫, unbuttoned conversation 畅快的交谈, leave unbuttoned 不扣
[联] buttoned 扣好的, open 开着的, relaxed 放松的, informal 非正式的, undone 松开的, loose 松的, stiff 拘谨的, casual 随意的
[记] unbuttoned 比喻义「不拘束、畅所欲言」在评论、访谈语境常见。unbuttoned vs. undone：unbuttoned 专指纽扣；undone 更宽

**mobile**
英/美 /ˈmoʊbl/ 英 /ˈməʊbaɪl/
adj. 可移动的；行动方便的
[义] Able to move or be moved; able to walk and function physically. 可移动、非固定（mobile equipment 移动设备, mobile library 流动图书馆）；身体行动方便（remain mobile 仍能动弹）。名词 mobile 指「手机」见科技章，本章收形容词「可移动状态」。
[例] ① The clinic uses a mobile unit to reach rural villages. 诊所用流动医疗车深入乡村。 ② After physiotherapy, she stayed mobile enough to climb stairs. 理疗后她仍能上下楼梯。
[族] mobility 可动性, mobilize 动员, immobile 不能动的
[搭] mobile phone/library/unit 手机/流动图书馆/移动单元, remain mobile 保持行动方便, highly mobile 高度流动的
[联] immovable 不可移动的, portable 便携的, movable 可移动的, fixed 固定的, stationary 静止的, active 活跃的, flexible 灵活的, static 静态的
[记] mobile 作名词指「手机」极频，形容词义「可移动」勿混。mobile vs. portable：mobile 强调「能移动位置」；portable 强调「便于携带」

**portable**
英/美 /ˈpɔːrtəbl/ 英 /ˈpɔːtəbl/
adj. 便携的；可搬动的
[义] Easy to carry or move; designed to be used in different places. 设计便于携带（portable speaker 便携音箱, portable heater 可移动取暖器）；可随人转移使用。与 fixed / immovable 相对。
[例] ① A portable generator kept the lights on during the outage. 停电期间，一台便携发电机维持照明。 ② She prefers a portable laptop for working in cafés. 她喜欢用便携笔记本在咖啡馆工作。
[族] port 携带, portability 可移植性
[搭] portable device/speaker/generator 便携设备/音箱/发电机, fully portable 完全便携, portable version 便携版
[联] mobile 可移动的, movable 可移动的, fixed 固定的, compact 紧凑的, lightweight 轻量的, handheld 手持的, bulky 笨重的, immovable 不可移动的
[记] portable 技术语境亦指「可移植（软件）」。portable vs. mobile：portable 偏「轻便、随身带」；mobile 偏「能换地方、能走动」

**movable**
英/美 /ˈmuːvəbl/
adj. 可移动的；动产的
[义] Capable of being moved; (law) personal property as opposed to real estate. 可以挪动位置（movable furniture 可移动家具, movable partition 活动隔断）；法律上指「动产」。与 immovable / fixed 相对。
[例] ① Movable partitions let the hall switch between layouts. 活动隔断让大厅能在多种布局间切换。 ② The will listed both movable assets and property. 遗嘱列明了动产与不动产。
[族] move 移动, movement 移动, immovable 不可移动的
[搭] movable furniture/partition 可移动家具/隔断, movable assets 动产, easily movable 易于移动
[联] immovable 不可移动的, mobile 可移动的, portable 便携的, fixed 固定的, stationary 静止的, flexible 灵活的, rigid 僵硬的, asset 资产
[记] movable assets 法律「动产」。movable vs. mobile：movable 强调「可被挪动」；mobile 可含「自身能移动、行动方便」

**immovable**
英/美 /ɪˈmuːvəbl/
adj. 不可移动的；固定的
[义] Unable to be moved; fixed in place; (law) real property. 物理上无法挪动（immovable fixture 固定装置）；法律上指「不动产」。与 movable / mobile 相对。
[例] ① The statue is an immovable fixture in the town square. 这座雕像是城镇广场上的固定陈设。 ② Immovable property transfers require different legal steps. 不动产转让须走不同的法律程序。
[族] move 移动, movable 可移动的, immovably 不可移动地
[搭] immovable fixture/object 固定装置/物体, immovable property 不动产, remain immovable 仍然固定不动
[联] movable 可移动的, fixed 固定的, stationary 静止的, mobile 可移动的, rooted 扎根的, anchored 锚定的, flexible 灵活的, asset 资产
[记] immovable property 法律「不动产」。immovable vs. fixed：immovable 强调「搬不动」；fixed 可指「安装固定」或「数据不变」

**unfixed**
英/美 /ʌnˈfɪkst/
adj. 未固定的；未确定的
[义] Not attached firmly; not settled or decided. 物理上未固定（unfixed samples 未固定样本）；日期、价格等未定（unfixed date 未定日期）。与 fixed（距离章）相对，本章收「未固定状态」。
[例] ① Unfixed tissue must be handled carefully in the lab. 未固定组织在实验室须谨慎处理。 ② The meeting remains unfixed until both sides confirm availability. 在双方确认时间前，会议日期仍未定。
[族] fix 固定, fixed 固定的
[搭] unfixed date/price 未定日期/价格, unfixed samples 未固定样本, remain unfixed 仍未固定
[联] fixed 固定的, unset 未设定的, loose 松的, anchored 锚定的, settled 确定的, stable 稳定的, tentative 暂定的, secured 固定的
[记] unfixed 科学语境指「未用固定液处理」。unfixed vs. unset：unfixed 偏「未物理固定/未敲定」；unset 可指「未凝固、心里不踏实」

**knotted**
英/美 /ˈnɑːtɪd/ 英 /ˈnɒtɪd/
adj. 打结的；缠结的；紧张的
[义] Tied with a knot; tangled; tense with emotion. 绳、线等打结（knotted rope 打结的绳）；肌肉、胃等「揪紧」（knotted muscles 紧张肌肉, knotted stomach 胃打结）；比喻关系复杂（knotted family ties 复杂的家庭关系）。knot 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Climbers checked every knotted rope before the ascent. 登山者出发前检查每根打结的绳索。 ② She felt a knotted stomach before walking on stage. 上台前她紧张得胃都揪紧了。
[族] knot 结, unknot 解开
[搭] knotted rope/muscles 打结的绳/紧张肌肉, knotted stomach 胃打结, knotted ties 复杂关系
[联] tangled 缠结的, tied 系住的, tense 紧张的, loose 松的, smooth 顺的, twisted 扭曲的, bound 被束缚的, relaxed 放松的
[记] knotted stomach 指「紧张得胃揪紧」。knotted vs. tangled：knotted 强调「有结」；tangled 强调「缠成一团」

**tangled**
英/美 /ˈtæŋɡld/
adj. 缠结的；混乱的
[义] Twisted together in a confused mass; complicated and difficult. 线、发、树枝等缠在一起（tangled hair 缠结的头发, tangled wires 乱线）；抽象上复杂难解（tangled legal dispute 错综复杂的纠纷）。tangle 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① It took an hour to untangle the tangled Christmas lights. 花了一小时才解开缠成一团的圣诞灯串。 ② The company is caught in a tangled ownership structure. 该公司陷入错综复杂的股权结构。
[族] tangle 缠结, untangle 解开
[搭] tangled hair/wires 缠结的头发/乱线, tangled dispute/affair 错综纠纷/纠葛, get tangled 缠在一起
[联] knotted 打结的, twisted 扭曲的, messy 乱的, smooth 顺的, simple 简单的, complicated 复杂的, neat 整齐的, bound 被束缚的
[记] tangled web 比喻「错综复杂的局面」。tangled vs. knotted：tangled 是一团乱；knotted 有明确绳结

**unscrewed**
英/美 /ʌnˈskruːd/
adj. 未拧上的；拧开的
[义] Not fastened with a screw; with the screw removed. 盖子、部件等未用螺丝拧紧或已拧开（unscrewed cap 拧开的瓶盖, leave unscrewed 不拧紧）。与 screwed 相对。
[例] ① Fuel leaked from an unscrewed cap on the generator. 发电机一个未拧紧的盖子导致燃油泄漏。 ② Keep the panel unscrewed until all wiring is checked. 所有线路检查完前，先别把面板螺丝拧紧。
[族] screw 螺丝, screwed 拧上的, unscrew 拧开
[搭] unscrewed cap/lid 拧开的瓶盖/盖子, leave unscrewed 不拧紧, remain unscrewed 仍未拧上
[联] screwed 拧上的, loose 松的, tight 紧的, open 开着的, sealed 密封的, fixed 固定的, removed 取下的, attached 附着的
[记] leave unscrewed 维修、实验语境常见。unscrewed vs. loose：unscrewed 专指「螺丝未拧」；loose 更宽

**unzipped**
英/美 /ʌnˈzɪpt/
adj. 拉链未拉的；解压的（略）
[义] With the zip not closed. 拉链没拉上（unzipped bag 拉链未拉的包, leave unzipped 不拉拉链）。与 zipped 相对；计算机 unzip 属动词，本条收物理「拉链未闭合」状态。
[例] ① She found her wallet missing from an unzipped backpack. 她发现拉链没拉好的背包里钱包不见了。 ② He walked in with an unzipped jacket, still breathless from the run. 他拉链敞着夹克走进来，跑步后还在喘气。
[族] zip 拉链, zipped 拉链拉上的, unzip 拉开拉链
[搭] unzipped bag/jacket 拉链未拉的包/夹克, leave unzipped 不拉拉链, half unzipped 拉链半开
[联] zipped 拉链拉上的, open 开着的, buttoned 扣好的, closed 关闭的, loose 松的, fastened 系牢的, exposed 暴露的, secure 安全的
[记] half unzipped 可指「拉链半开」。unzipped vs. unbuttoned：unzipped 用拉链；unbuttoned 用纽扣

**unclipped**
英/美 /ʌnˈklɪpt/
adj. 未夹住的；未剪短的
[义] Not held with a clip; not cut short. 纸张、线缆等未用夹子夹住（unclipped papers 散页）；头发、指甲未剪（unclipped nails 未剪的指甲）。与 clipped 相对。
[例] ① Unclipped papers blew off the desk when the window opened. 开窗后，没夹住的纸被吹下桌子。 ② The dog's unclipped nails scratched the floorboards. 狗没剪的指甲刮过木地板。
[族] clip 夹子, clipped 夹住的, unclip 取下夹子
[搭] unclipped papers/nails 散页/未剪指甲, leave unclipped 不夹/不剪, remain unclipped 仍未夹住
[联] clipped 夹住的, loose 松的, pinned 别住的, trimmed 修剪过的, fastened 系牢的, scattered 散落的, neat 整齐的, long 长的
[记] unclipped 可指「未夹」或「未剪短」。unclipped vs. untied：unclipped 用夹子；untied 用绳结

**coiled**
英/美 /kɔɪld/
adj. 盘绕的；卷曲的
[义] Wound into a series of loops or a spiral. 绳、蛇、弹簧等盘绕（coiled rope 盘绕的绳, coiled spring 螺旋弹簧）；身体蜷缩（coiled posture 蜷缩姿势）。coil 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The coiled hose lay ready beside the fire station. 盘好的水带放在消防站旁待用。 ② The snake remained coiled under the warm rock. 蛇在温热的岩石下盘着不动。
[族] coil 盘绕, uncoil 展开
[搭] coiled rope/spring 盘绕的绳/弹簧, coiled posture 蜷缩姿势, tightly coiled 紧紧盘绕
[联] rolled 卷起的, twisted 扭曲的, unwound 展开的, spiral 螺旋的, curled 卷曲的, straight 直的, looped 成圈的, tense 绷紧的
[记] coiled spring 机械高频。coiled vs. rolled：coiled 盘成圈/螺旋；rolled 卷成筒

**twisted**
英/美 /ˈtwɪstɪd/
adj. 扭曲的；变形的；乖僻的
[义] Bent or turned out of shape; (informal) morally strange or cruel. 物理上扭弯（twisted metal 扭弯的金属, twisted ankle 扭伤的踝）；比喻心理「乖僻、变态」（twisted sense of humour 黑色幽默）。twist 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Twisted rails showed how violent the derailment had been. 扭曲的铁轨说明脱轨有多猛烈。 ② The film explores a twisted family secret. 影片挖掘了一个乖僻的家庭秘密。
[族] twist 扭曲, untwist 弄直
[搭] twisted metal/ankle 扭弯的金属/扭伤的踝, twisted logic/humour 歪理/黑色幽默, badly twisted 严重扭曲
[联] bent 弯的, straight 直的, tangled 缠结的, warped 翘曲的, normal 正常的, deformed 变形的, crooked 弯曲的, injured 受伤的
[记] twisted ankle 极频。twisted 比喻义「乖僻、变态」口语与媒体常见，正式写作须依语境。twisted vs. bent：twisted 强调「拧转变形」

**straightened**
英/美 /ˈstreɪtnd/
adj. 弄直的；挺直的
[义] Made straight; upright in posture. 物体被弄直（straightened wire 弄直的金属线）；人挺直身子（straightened back 挺直的背）。straighten 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The straightened nail would still not hold the board firmly. 弄直后的钉子仍挂不牢木板。 ② She sat with a straightened back despite hours at the desk. 尽管伏案数小时，她仍挺直着背。
[族] straighten 弄直, straight 直的
[搭] straightened back/wire 挺直的背/弄直的线, sit straightened 坐直, fully straightened 完全弄直
[联] bent 弯的, twisted 扭曲的, upright 竖直的, slouched 驼背的, neat 整齐的, crooked 弯曲的, aligned 对齐的, stiff 僵硬的
[记] straightened 多作「刚被弄直」的结果。straightened vs. straight：straight 本身可作形容词「直的」；straightened 强调「经整理后变直」

**crumpled**
英/美 /ˈkrʌmpld/
adj. 皱的；压瘪的
[义] Crushed into folds or wrinkles; damaged by crushing. 纸、布料、金属等皱巴巴（crumpled letter 揉皱的信, crumpled suit 皱巴巴的西装）；车辆等撞瘪（crumpled hood 撞瘪的引擎盖）。crumple 的过去分词作形容词。
