[例] ① He pulled a crumpled receipt from his pocket to prove the purchase. 他从兜里掏出一张皱收据证明购买。 ② The crumpled front end showed the force of the collision. 撞瘪的前端显示碰撞力度很大。
[族] crumple 弄皱
[搭] crumpled paper/letter/suit 皱纸/揉皱的信/皱西装, crumpled hood 撞瘪的引擎盖, look crumpled 显得皱巴巴
[联] creased 有褶的, wrinkled 起皱的, smooth 平整的, flat 平的, neat 整洁的, damaged 损坏的, folded 折叠的, crushed 压碎的
[记] crumpled vs. creased：crumpled 强调「揉成一团、皱巴巴」；creased 偏「有折痕」

**welded**
英/美 /ˈweldɪd/
adj. 焊接的；牢不可分的
[义] Joined by welding; (figurative) firmly united. 金属等经焊接固定（welded joint 焊缝, welded frame 焊接框架）；比喻「牢不可分」（welded together 紧密结合）。weld 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Inspect every welded seam before the tank goes into service. 储罐投入使用前要检查每条焊缝。 ② The crisis welded the team together in unexpected ways. 危机以意外方式把团队拧成一股绳。
[族] weld 焊接, welding 焊接
[搭] welded joint/frame/seam 焊缝/焊接框架/焊缝, welded together 焊接在一起/紧密结合
[联] glued 粘住的, screwed 拧上的, bolted 栓上的, loose 松的, fixed 固定的, joined 接合的, separate 分离的, bonded 结合的
[记] welded together 比喻「紧密团结」。welded vs. glued：welded 用焊；glued 用胶

**stapled**
英/美 /ˈsteɪpld/
adj. 订书机订好的；订书钉缝合的
[义] Fastened with a staple or staples; (medical) closed with surgical staples. 纸等用订书钉订住（stapled documents 订好的文件）；伤口用钉合（stapled incision 钉合切口）。staple 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A stapled packet of forms awaited signature on her desk. 她桌上等着签字的是一沓订好的表格。 ② The surgeon closed the stapled wound with a dressing. 医生给钉合的伤口上了敷料。
[族] staple 订书钉, stapler 订书机
[搭] stapled documents/packet 订好的文件/一沓, stapled incision 钉合切口, stapled together 订在一起
[联] pinned 别住的, clipped 夹住的, glued 粘住的, loose 散页的, bound 装订的, sewn 缝制的, attached 附着的, medical 医学的
[记] stapled 医学语境指「皮肤钉合」。stapled vs. pinned：stapled 用订书钉；pinned 用别针

**handcuffed**
英/美 /ˈhændkʌft/
adj. 戴手铐的
[义] With the hands secured by handcuffs. 双手被手铐铐住（handcuffed suspect 戴铐嫌疑人, handcuffed to a chair 铐在椅子上）。handcuff 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The handcuffed suspect was led into the courtroom. 戴铐嫌疑人被带进法庭。 ② Protesters were handcuffed to the railings for hours. 抗议者被铐在栏杆上数小时。
[族] handcuff 手铐
[搭] handcuffed suspect/prisoner 戴铐嫌疑人/囚犯, handcuffed to 铐在……上, remain handcuffed 仍戴手铐
[联] shackled 戴镣的, chained 锁链拴住的, bound 被束缚的, freed 获释的, arrested 被捕的, restrained 受约束的, loose 自由的, detained 被拘留的
[记] handcuffed to 可指物理铐在物体上。handcuffed vs. shackled：handcuffed 专指手铐；shackled 指脚镣或更广义束缚

**unchained**
英/美 /ʌnˈtʃeɪnd/
adj. 未拴链的；获释的
[义] Not held by a chain; freed from restraint. 物理上未用链拴（unchained dog 未拴的狗）；比喻摆脱束缚（unchained creativity 不受束缚的创造力）。与 chained 相对。
[例] ① An unchained dog ran into the road, causing drivers to brake. 一条没拴的狗冲上马路，司机纷纷刹车。 ② The reform left markets relatively unchained from old rules. 改革使市场相对摆脱旧规束缚。
[族] chain 链条, chained 拴链的
[搭] unchained dog 未拴的狗, unchained creativity 不受束缚的创造力, remain unchained 仍未拴/仍自由
[联] chained 拴链的, freed 获释的, loose 松的, bound 被束缚的, restrained 受约束的, liberated 获解放的, tethered 拴住的, restricted 受限制的
[记] unchained 比喻「摆脱束缚」与 unshackled 近义。unchained vs. unshackled：unchained 更绑链条；unshackled 更绑镣铐

**unbound**
英/美 /ʌnˈbaʊnd/
adj. 未装订的；未绑住的；无限的
[义] Not tied or fastened; (of a book) not bound with a cover; (formal) unlimited. 未捆绑（unbound hands 未绑的手）；书籍未装订（unbound pages 散页）；正式语体「无约束」（unbound enthusiasm 尽情释放的热情）。与 bound（数字章有 bound 其他义）相对，本章收「未束缚」状态。
[例] ① Unbound pages from the manuscript were stored in archival boxes. 手稿散页存放在档案盒里。 ② The crowd responded with unbound enthusiasm. 人群以无比热烈的反应回应。
[族] bind 捆绑, bound 被束缚的
[搭] unbound pages/hands 散页/未绑的手, unbound enthusiasm 尽情释放的热情, remain unbound 仍未装订/未束缚
[联] bound 被束缚的, loose 松的, freed 获释的, chained 拴链的, printed 印刷的, published 出版的, limited 有限的, tethered 拴住的
[记] unbound 出版语境指「未装订成册」。unbound enthusiasm 正式语体「无拘无束的热情」

**detained**
英/美 /dɪˈteɪnd/
adj. 被拘留的；被耽搁的
[义] Kept in official custody; delayed and unable to leave. 被官方扣留（detained suspect 被拘嫌疑人, detained at the border 在边境被扣）；因事耽搁（detained at work 被工作绊住）。detain 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Several journalists were detained overnight for questioning. 几名记者被通宵拘留问话。 ② Sorry I'm late—I was detained in a meeting. 抱歉来晚了——我被会议绊住了。
[族] detain 拘留, detention 拘留
[搭] detained suspect/passenger 被拘嫌疑人/被扣旅客, detained at 在……被扣/被耽搁, briefly detained 短暂拘留
[联] arrested 被捕的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, freed 获释的, released 已释放的, delayed 延误的, held 被扣的, questioned 被问话的, waiting 等待的
[记] detained at work/meeting 礼貌说法「被事耽搁」。detained vs. arrested：detained 可指「短暂扣留」；arrested 更绑「正式逮捕」

**arrested**
英/美 /əˈrestɪd/
adj. 被捕的；停滞的
[义] Taken into police custody; (formal) stopped in development. 被警方逮捕（arrested suspect 被捕嫌疑人）；正式语体「停滞的」（arrested development 发育停滞）。arrest 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The arrested suspect was read his rights at the station. 被捕嫌疑人在警局听取了权利告知。 ② The project showed arrested development after funding was cut. 资金削减后，项目进展陷入停滞。
[族] arrest 逮捕, arresting 醒目的
[搭] arrested suspect 被捕嫌疑人, arrested development 发育停滞/发展受阻, placed under arrested 被逮捕
[联] detained 被拘留的, imprisoned 被囚禁的, freed 获释的, released 已释放的, charged 被起诉的, guilty 有罪的, innocent 无辜的, stalled 停滞的
[记] arrested development 心理学固定术语「发育停滞」。arrested vs. detained：arrested 通常指正式逮捕；detained 范围更宽

**unknotted**
英/美 /ʌnˈnɑːtɪd/ 英 /ʌnˈnɒtɪd/
adj. 未打结的；解开的
[义] With no knots; having had knots removed. 绳、线等未打结或已解开（unknotted rope 无结的绳, come unknotted 结松开了）。与 knotted 相对。
[例] ① Climbers prefer unknotted rope ends that are cleanly melted. 登山者偏好末端熔合处理、无结的绳头。 ② The necklace came unknotted in her bag. 项链在包里结自己松开了。
[族] knot 结, knotted 打结的, unknot 解开
[搭] unknotted rope 无结的绳, come unknotted 结松开, remain unknotted 仍未打结
[联] knotted 打结的, tied 系住的, loose 松的, tangled 缠结的, smooth 顺的, bound 被束缚的, free 自由的, secure 牢固的
[记] come unknotted 指「结自己松开」。unknotted vs. untied：unknotted 强调「结已解」；untied 可指鞋带等未系

**untangled**
英/美 /ʌnˈtæŋɡld/
adj. 未缠结的；理顺的
[义] Not twisted together; freed from a tangle. 线、发等未缠在一起或已理顺（untangled hair 梳顺的头发, untangled wires 理好的线）。与 tangled 相对。
[例] ① She brushed her hair until it lay smooth and untangled. 她把头发梳到顺滑、不打结。 ② Untangled cables made the workspace easier to manage. 理好的线缆让工位更好打理。
[族] tangle 缠结, tangled 缠结的, untangle 解开
[搭] untangled hair/wires 梳顺的头发/理好的线, keep untangled 保持不缠结, fully untangled 完全理顺
[联] tangled 缠结的, smooth 顺的, neat 整齐的, knotted 打结的, messy 乱的, combed 梳过的, organized 整理好的, loose 松的
[记] untangled vs. unknotted：untangled 强调「不再缠成一团」；unknotted 强调「结已解」

**unwound**
英/美 /ʌnˈwaʊnd/
adj. 展开的；放松的
[义] Not wound up; relaxed after tension. 磁带、线等未绕紧或已展开（unwound spool 展开的线轴）；人「放松下来」（feel unwound 感到松弛）。unwind 的过去分词作形容词；与 wound（身体章「伤」）同形异义。
[例] ① The unwound cable stretched across the workshop floor. 展开的电缆横在车间地面上。 ② After a long walk, she finally felt unwound enough to sleep. 长途散步后，她终于放松下来能睡了。
[族] unwind 展开, wound 缠绕
[搭] unwound cable/spool 展开的电缆/线轴, feel unwound 感到放松, fully unwound 完全展开
[联] wound 缠绕的, coiled 盘绕的, rolled 卷起的, relaxed 放松的, tense 紧张的, loose 松的, tight 紧的, stretched 拉伸的
[记] feel unwound 指「紧张过后松弛」。unwound vs. unrolled：unwound 强调「从绕紧状态松开」；unrolled 强调「从卷筒展开」

**riveted**
英/美 /ˈrɪvɪtɪd/
adj. 铆接的；目光紧锁的
[义] Joined with rivets; (figurative) completely focused. 金属等用铆钉固定（riveted joint 铆接）；比喻目光、注意力「钉住」（riveted to the screen 紧盯着屏幕）。rivet 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The riveted plates held even under heavy vibration. 铆接钢板在剧烈振动下仍不松。 ② The audience sat riveted through the final scene. 观众紧盯着看完最后一幕。
[族] rivet 铆钉, riveting 引人入胜的
[搭] riveted joint/plates 铆接/铆接钢板, riveted to 紧盯着/铆在, sit riveted 坐着一动不动地看
[联] welded 焊接的, bolted 栓上的, fixed 固定的, focused 专注的, glued 粘住的, attached 附着的, loose 松的, captivated 被吸引的
[记] riveted to the screen 极频，指「目光离不开屏幕」。riveted vs. glued：riveted 可指铆接或目光；glued 多指胶或比喻「粘住」

**soldered**
英/美 /ˈsɑːdərd/ 英 /ˈsɒdəd/
adj. 焊接的（软焊）
[义] Joined with solder, especially in electronics. 电子元件等经软焊连接（soldered connection 焊点, poorly soldered joint 虚焊）。solder 的过去分词作形容词；与 welded（硬焊）相对。
[例] ① A poorly soldered joint caused intermittent signal loss. 虚焊导致信号时断时续。 ② Each soldered connection was tested before assembly closed. 组装合拢前每个焊点都经测试。
[族] solder 焊料, soldering 软焊
[搭] soldered connection/joint 焊点/焊点, poorly soldered 虚焊, soldered board 焊好的板
[联] welded 焊接的, wired 接线的, connected 连接的, loose 松的, fixed 固定的, electronic 电子的, broken 断的, faulty 有故障的
[记] soldered vs. welded：soldered 多指电子软焊；welded 多指金属硬焊。 poorly soldered 指「虚焊」

**unglued**
英/美 /ʌnˈɡluːd/
adj. 未粘住的；散架的（口语）
[义] Not fixed with glue; (informal) emotionally upset or falling apart. 未用胶粘牢（unglued label 粘不牢的标签）；口语「情绪崩溃、散架」（come unglued 崩溃）。与 glued 相对。
[例] ① An unglued corner of the poster flapped in the breeze. 海报一角没粘牢，在风中啪啪响。 ② She nearly came unglued when she saw the exam results. 看到考试结果，她差点崩溃。
[族] glue 胶水, glued 粘住的
[搭] come unglued 崩溃/散架, unglued label/corner 粘不牢的标签/角, remain unglued 仍未粘住
[联] glued 粘住的, loose 松的, detached 分离的, upset 情绪激动的, falling apart 散架的, fixed 固定的, stressed 有压力的, calm 冷静的
[记] come unglued 美式口语「情绪失控、快撑不住」。unglued vs. loose：unglued 专指「胶没粘住或人快崩溃」

**charged**
英/美 /tʃɑːrdʒd/ 英 /tʃɑːdʒd/
adj. 充电的；带电的；情绪激昂的
[义] Containing electrical energy; filled with strong emotion or tension. 电池、设备已充电（fully charged 充满电）；气氛、话语带强烈情绪（charged atmosphere 剑拔弩张的气氛, emotionally charged 情绪激烈的）。charge 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Make sure the device is fully charged before the trip. 出行前请确保设备充满电。 ② The debate took place in a highly charged atmosphere. 辩论在高度紧张的气氛中进行。
[族] charge 充电, discharge 放电, rechargeable 可充电的
[搭] fully charged 充满电, charged atmosphere 紧张气氛, emotionally charged 情绪激烈的, charged particle 带电粒子
[联] discharged 放电的, powered 通电的, empty 空的, tense 紧张的, electric 带电的, loaded 满载的, calm 平静的, heated 激烈的, active 活跃的
[记] emotionally charged 极频。charged vs. loaded：charged 可指「充电、带电、情绪 charged」；loaded 多指「装载、上膛、带偏见」

**discharged**
英/美 /dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒd/ 英 /dɪsˈtʃɑːdʒd/
adj. 已放电的；已出院/退伍的
[义] With electrical energy removed; officially allowed to leave (hospital, military). 电池等已放空（discharged battery 没电的电池）；人获准离开（discharged patient 出院患者, discharged soldier 退伍士兵）。discharge 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The remote stopped working because of a discharged battery. 遥控器因电池没电不工作了。 ② Discharged patients still need follow-up care at home. 出院患者在家仍需随访护理。
[族] discharge 放电, charged 充电的
[搭] discharged battery 没电的电池, discharged patient/soldier 出院患者/退伍士兵, medically discharged 因健康原因退伍
[联] charged 充电的, empty 空的, released 已释放的, admitted 入院的, powered 通电的, depleted 耗尽的, free 自由的, active 现役的
[记] medically discharged 指「因伤病退伍/退役」。discharged vs. released：discharged 多绑医院、军队、电池；released 更宽

**wired**
英/美 /ˈwaɪərd/
adj. 接线的；联网的；神经紧张的
[义] Connected by wires; linked to a network; (informal) nervous or overstimulated. 设备已接线（hard-wired system 硬接线系统）；口语「神经紧绷、像打了兴奋剂」（feel wired after coffee 喝完咖啡后精神亢奋）。wire 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The lab uses a hard-wired alarm that cannot be disabled remotely. 实验室使用无法远程关闭的硬接线报警器。 ② She felt wired and could not fall asleep until after midnight. 她神经兴奋，直到午夜后才睡着。
[族] wire 电线, wireless 无线的, rewire 重新布线
[搭] hard-wired 硬接线的, feel wired 感到神经兴奋, wired network 有线网络, wired for sound 装好音响线路
[联] wireless 无线的, connected 连接的, nervous 紧张的, powered 通电的, unplugged 未插电的, tense 紧张的, calm 冷静的, linked 相连的, alert 警觉的
[记] feel wired 口语「咖啡因/压力后睡不着」。wired vs. connected：wired 可指物理接线或口语「亢奋」

**switched**
英/美 /swɪtʃt/
adj. 已切换的；开关打开的
[义] Turned on or off with a switch; changed from one state to another. 开关已打开或关闭（switched off 已关闭, switched on 已打开）；系统、模式已切换（switched to manual mode 切到手动模式）。switch 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① All non-essential lights were switched off to save power. 所有非必要照明已关闭以省电。 ② The factory switched to backup power when the grid failed. 电网故障时，工厂切到备用电源。
[族] switch 开关, switch off/on 关/开
[搭] switched off/on 关闭/打开, switched to 切换到, remain switched off 保持关闭
[联] plugged 插好的, powered 通电的, offline 离线的, online 在线的, active 激活的, inactive 未激活的, connected 连接的, manual 手动的
[记] switched off 可指设备关闭，也可口语指「走神、不在状态」。switched vs. plugged：switched 用开关；plugged 用插头

**liberated**
英/美 /ˈlɪbəreɪtɪd/
adj. 获解放的；思想开放的
[义] Set free from control or oppression; free in attitude, especially about social norms. 从压迫、占领中解放（liberated town 解放的城镇）；思想、行为较不受传统束缚（liberated attitudes 开放态度）。liberate 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Liberated villages received aid within days of the ceasefire. 停火后数日内，解放的村庄收到援助。 ② She grew up in a relatively liberated household where careers were discussed openly. 她在相对开明的家庭长大，职业话题可以敞开谈。
[族] liberate 解放, liberation 解放, liberating 解放性的
[搭] liberated town/people 解放的城镇/人民, liberated attitudes 开放态度, feel liberated 感到解放
[联] freed 获释的, released 已释放的, trapped 困住的, oppressed 受压迫的, independent 独立的, conservative 保守的, occupied 被占领的, empowered 被赋能的
[记] feel liberated 指「摆脱束缚后的轻松」。liberated vs. freed：liberated 更绑「从压迫/旧规中解放」；freed 更宽

**rescued**
英/美 /ˈreskjuːd/
adj. 获救的；被救出的
[义] Saved from danger or harm. 从危险中救出（rescued hikers 获救徒步者, rescued data 抢救出的数据）。rescue 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Rescued miners were taken straight to hospital for checks. 获救矿工被直接送往医院检查。 ② Rescued files were restored from the backup drive. 抢救出的文件从备份盘恢复了。
[族] rescue 救援, rescuer 救援者
[搭] rescued hikers/miners 获救徒步者/矿工, rescued data 抢救出的数据, rescued from 从……中救出
[联] saved 获救的, trapped 困住的, stranded 滞留的, freed 获释的, lost 丢失的, safe 安全的, recovered 恢复的, endangered 濒危的
[记] rescued from 固定搭配。rescued vs. saved：rescued 强调「经救援行动」；saved 更宽

**unbolted**
英/美 /ʌnˈboʊltɪd/
adj. 未栓上的；螺栓未固定的
[义] Not secured with a bolt. 门闩未闩、螺栓未拧紧（unbolted door 未栓的门, unbolted frame 未用螺栓固定的框架）。与 bolted 相对。
[例] ① Wind blew the unbolted gate open during the storm. 暴风雨中，未栓的大门被吹开。 ② Never operate machinery with an unbolted guard in place. 防护罩未螺栓固定时切勿运行机器。
[族] bolt 螺栓, bolted 栓上的, unbolt 拔门闩
[搭] unbolted door/gate 未栓的门/大门, unbolted frame 未螺栓固定的框架, leave unbolted 不栓上
[联] bolted 栓上的, unlocked 未锁的, open 开着的, loose 松的, secured 固定的, latched 闩上的, fastened 系牢的, shut 关着的
[记] unbolted vs. unlocked：unbolted 靠门闩/螺栓；unlocked 靠锁

**unnailed**
英/美 /ʌnˈneɪld/
adj. 未钉牢的；未用钉子固定的
[义] Not fixed with nails. 木板、框架等未钉牢（unnailed board 未钉的板, leave unnailed 暂不钉）。与 nailed 相对，略罕作形容词。
[例] ① An unnailed board shifted underfoot on the temporary walkway. 临时走道上一块未钉牢的板子在脚下移动。 ② They left the trim unnailed until the walls were fully dry. 墙面完全干透前，他们暂不钉装饰条。
[族] nail 钉子, nailed 钉住的
[搭] unnailed board/trim 未钉的板/装饰条, leave unnailed 暂不钉, remain unnailed 仍未钉牢
[联] nailed 钉住的, loose 松的, screwed 拧上的, glued 粘住的, fixed 固定的, temporary 临时的, secured 固定的, unstable 不稳定的
[记] unnailed 多用于施工、DIY 语境。unnailed vs. loose：unnailed 专指「钉子未打」

**unpinned**
英/美 /ʌnˈpɪnd/
adj. 未别住的；未钉住的
[义] Not held with a pin. 徽章、文件、布料等未用别针固定（unpinned badge 未别的徽章, unpinned note 未别住的便条）。与 pinned 相对。
[例] ① An unpinned note fluttered off the board in the draft. 通风把未别住的便条从板上吹落。 ② She entered with an unpinned medal still in her pocket. 她进来时，一枚未别的奖章还在兜里。
[族] pin 别针, pinned 别住的, unpin 取下别针
[搭] unpinned note/badge 未别住的便条/徽章, leave unpinned 不别住, remain unpinned 仍未固定
[联] pinned 别住的, loose 松的, clipped 夹住的, fastened 系牢的, attached 附着的, scattered 散落的, secured 固定的, fallen 掉落的
[记] unpinned vs. unclipped：unpinned 用别针；unclipped 用夹子

**snapped**
英/美 /snæpt/
adj. 突然断开的；啪地合上的；仓促完成的
[义] Broken suddenly; closed with a snap; done quickly and without care. 物体突然断掉（snapped cable 断缆）；扣件啪地扣上（snapped shut 啪地关上）；仓促完成（snapped photo 随手拍）。snap 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A snapped cable brought the lift to an emergency stop. 断缆使电梯紧急停止。 ② The lid snapped shut when she pressed it down. 她按下后，盖子啪地合上。
[族] snap 啪地折断, snap 快照
[搭] snapped shut 啪地关上, snapped cable/rope 断缆/断绳, snapped photo 随手拍, snapped off 折断
[联] broken 断的, intact 完好的, closed 关闭的, loose 松的, quick 快速的, torn 撕裂的, fastened 系牢的, sudden 突然的
[记] snapped shut 指「啪地扣上」。snapped photo 指「随手拍、非精心构图」。snapped vs. broken：snapped 强调「突然断、脆断」

**clicked**
英/美 /klɪkt/
adj. 咔嗒合上的；一点就通的（口语）
[义] Fastened with a click; (informal) suddenly understood or worked well. 咔嗒一声扣上（clicked into place 咔嗒就位）；口语「突然明白、对上频道」（everything clicked 一切豁然开朗）。click 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The cover clicked into place with a satisfying sound. 盖子咔嗒一声到位，听着很踏实。 ② After weeks of practice, everything finally clicked for the team. 练了几周后，全队终于开窍了。
[族] click 咔嗒声, clickable 可点击的
[搭] clicked into place 咔嗒就位, everything clicked 一切豁然开朗, clicked shut 咔嗒关上
[联] snapped 啪地合上的, locked 锁住的, fastened 系牢的, loose 松的, clear 清楚的, understood 明白的, fitted 契合的, broken 断的
[记] everything clicked 口语「突然想通、配合默契」。clicked into place 物理或比喻「完美契合」

**turned**
英/美 /tɜːrnd/ 英 /tɜːnd/
adj. 翻转的；变质的；转向的
[义] Rotated or flipped; spoiled (food); directed in a particular way. 物体被转动（turned key 转动的钥匙, turned page 翻过的页）；食物变质（turned milk 变质的奶）；方向改变（turned ankle 扭伤的踝）。turn 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The smell told her the milk had turned overnight. 一闻就知道奶过夜变质了。 ② He sat with a turned ankle elevated on a cushion. 他扭伤的脚踝垫高放在靠垫上。
[搭] turned milk 变质的奶, turned ankle 扭伤的踝, turned page 翻过的页, badly turned 严重扭伤
[联] twisted 扭曲的, rotated 旋转的, spoiled 变质的, fresh 新鲜的, sprained 扭伤的, straight 直的, flipped 翻转的, broken 断的
[记] turned milk/food 指「变质」。turned ankle 极频。turned vs. twisted：turned ankle 常指扭伤；twisted 强调「拧转变形」

**rotated**
英/美 /ˈroʊteɪtɪd/ 英 /rəʊˈteɪtɪd/
adj. 旋转的；轮换的
[义] Turned around an axis; changed in position by rotation. 物理上旋转一定角度（rotated image 旋转后的图像, rotated crops 轮作作物）；岗位、班次轮换（rotated shifts 轮班）。rotate 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The rotated image was easier to read on the screen. 旋转后的图像在屏幕上更易读。 ② Staff work rotated shifts to cover nights and weekends. 员工轮班以覆盖夜间和周末。
[族] rotate 旋转, rotation 旋转
[搭] rotated image/crops 旋转后的图像/轮作作物, rotated shifts 轮班, rotated 90 degrees 旋转90度
[联] turned 翻转的, spun 旋转的, fixed 固定的, aligned 对齐的, straight 直的, inverted 倒置的, scheduled 排班的, static 静态的
[记] rotated shifts 人事/运营高频。rotated vs. turned：rotated 强调「绕轴旋转」；turned 更宽

**spun**
英/美 /spʌn/
adj. 旋转的；纺成的
[义] Turned round rapidly; made by spinning fibres. 快速旋转（spun around 转圈）；纺织上「纺成的」（spun sugar 糖丝, finely spun thread 细纺线）。spin 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The dancers finished with a spun pirouette that drew applause. 舞者以高速旋转结束，赢得掌声。 ② Spun glass ornaments hung from the market stall. 玻璃丝饰品挂在集市摊位上。
[族] spin 旋转, spinning 旋转的
[搭] spun sugar/glass 糖丝/玻璃丝, spun around 旋转, finely spun 细纺的
[联] rotated 旋转的, coiled 盘绕的, woven 编织的, twisted 扭曲的, still 静止的, fast 快速的, delicate 精细的, static 静态的
[记] spun sugar 指「棉花糖式糖丝」。spun 作形容词不如 rotate 系列常见，多出现在复合词

**exhausted**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzɔːstɪd/
adj. 精疲力竭的；耗尽的
[义] Extremely tired; completely used up. 人极度疲惫（feel exhausted 感到精疲力竭）；资源、供应耗尽（exhausted supplies 耗尽的补给）。exhaust 的过去分词作形容词；与 drained 近义，exhausted 更绑「累到极限」。
[例] ① The medical team was exhausted after thirty-six hours on duty. 医疗组值班三十六小时后精疲力竭。 ② Exhausted supplies forced the clinic to turn patients away. 补给耗尽，诊所不得不拒收患者。
[族] exhaust 耗尽, exhaustion 衰竭, exhausting 令人疲惫的
[搭] feel exhausted 感到精疲力竭, physically exhausted 体力耗尽, exhausted supplies 耗尽的补给, completely exhausted 完全耗尽
[联] drained 耗尽的, tired 疲惫的, depleted 耗尽的, refreshed 恢复精力的, weary 疲倦的, empty 空的, full 满的, energetic 精力充沛的
[记] feel exhausted 极频。exhausted vs. drained：exhausted 偏「人累到极点」；drained 偏「被抽干、掏空」

**unbounded**
英/美 /ʌnˈbaʊndɪd/
adj. 无限的；不受约束的
[义] Without limits; not restricted. 范围、热情等无边界（unbounded enthusiasm 无比热情, unbounded confidence 无比自信）。与 restricted / bounded 相对，正式语体常见。
[例] ① She tackled the project with unbounded enthusiasm. 她以无比热情投入项目。 ② The theory assumes unbounded economic growth, which many critics reject. 该理论假设经济无限增长，许多批评者并不认同。
[族] bound 界限, bounded 有界的, unbound 未装订的
[搭] unbounded enthusiasm/confidence 无比热情/自信, unbounded growth 无限增长, seemingly unbounded 看似无限的
[联] unlimited 无限的, restricted 受限制的, boundless 无限的, finite 有限的, confined 受困的, free 自由的, modest 适度的, constrained 受约束的
[记] unbounded enthusiasm 正式语体常见。unbounded vs. unlimited：近义；unbounded 更书面、可绑抽象品质

**seated**
英/美 /ˈsiːtɪd/
adj. 坐着的；就座的
[义] In a sitting position; (formal) firmly established. 人坐着（seated audience 就座观众, remain seated 请就座）；正式语体「根深蒂固」（deeply seated beliefs 根深蒂固的信念）。seat 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Please remain seated until the seatbelt sign is off. 安全带指示灯熄灭前请保持就座。 ② Deeply seated distrust made negotiation difficult. 根深蒂固的不信任使谈判艰难。
[搭] remain seated 保持就座, seated audience/position 就座观众/坐姿, deeply seated 根深蒂固的
[联] standing 站着的, sitting 坐着的, lying 躺着的, upright 竖直的, rooted 扎根的, mobile 可移动的, audience 观众, posture 姿势
[记] remain seated 广播极频。deeply seated 指「根深蒂固」，与 rooted/ingrained 近义。seated vs. sitting：seated 更正式、常指「就座状态」

**standing**
英/美 /ˈstændɪŋ/
adj. 站立的；长期的；停滞的
[义] In an upright position on the feet; continuing to exist; not flowing. 站着（standing ovation 起立鼓掌, standing room 站位）；长期存在（standing order 长期订单, standing joke 老笑话）；液体 stagnant（standing water 死水）。stand 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① There was only standing room left on the crowded train. 拥挤的列车上只剩站位。 ② Standing water in the yard attracted mosquitoes. 院里的死水招来了蚊子。
[族] stand 站立, standstill 停滞
[搭] standing ovation 起立鼓掌, standing room 站位, standing order 长期订单, standing water 死水
[联] seated 坐着的, lying 躺着的, upright 竖直的, stagnant 停滞的, mobile 可移动的, permanent 长期的, flowing 流动的, audience 观众
[记] standing ovation 极频。standing water 指「不流动的水」。standing vs. seated：standing 站着；seated 坐着

**lying**
英/美 /ˈlaɪɪŋ/
adj. 躺着的；平放的；位于的
[义] In a flat or resting position; placed horizontally; situated (formal). 人躺着（lying patient 卧床患者, lying on the sofa 躺在沙发上）；物体平放（lying flat 平放）；正式语体表位置（a valley lying between two hills 夹在两山之间的山谷）。lie 的现在分词作形容词；与 lying「说谎的」同形，靠语境区分。
[例] ① The injured player remained lying on the pitch for several minutes. 受伤球员在球场上躺了几分钟。 ② Keep the bottles lying flat during transport. 运输时瓶子请平放。
[族] lie 躺, lay 放置
[搭] lying flat/on one's back 平放/仰躺, lying patient 卧床患者, lying between 位于……之间, remain lying 保持躺姿
[联] seated 坐着的, standing 站着的, reclining 斜躺的, upright 竖直的, horizontal 水平的, prone 俯卧的, awake 醒着的, asleep 睡着的, mobile 可移动的
[记] lying on one's back/side 极频。lying flat 运输、摄影常用。lying vs. laying：lying 来自 lie（躺）；laying 来自 lay（放置）

**kneeling**
英/美 /ˈniːlɪŋ/
adj. 跪着的；跪姿的
[义] In a position with one or both knees on the ground. 单膝或双膝着地（kneeling position 跪姿, kneeling figure 跪像）。kneel 的现在分词作形容词；宗教、仪式、求婚等语境常见。
[例] ① A kneeling figure prayed quietly at the altar. 一个跪姿的人静静在祭坛前祈祷。 ② Gardeners often work in a kneeling position for hours. 园丁常跪姿作业数小时。
[族] kneel 跪下, knee 膝盖
[搭] kneeling position 跪姿, kneeling figure 跪像, remain kneeling 保持跪姿, kneeling down 跪着
[联] standing 站着的, seated 坐着的, crouching 蹲着的, upright 竖直的, bowing 鞠躬的, praying 祈祷的, squatting 蹲坐的, mobile 可移动的
[记] kneeling position 健身、园艺、宗教高频。kneeling vs. crouching：kneeling 膝着地；crouching 整体蹲低、膝未必着地

**crouching**
英/美 /ˈkraʊtʃɪŋ/
adj. 蹲伏的；蜷缩的
[义] With the body lowered and knees bent, often ready to move or hide. 身体蹲低、膝弯曲（crouching tiger 卧虎, crouching position 蹲伏姿势）；可喻「潜伏、蓄势」（crouching threat 潜伏威胁）。crouch 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① A crouching figure moved silently through the shadows. 一个蹲伏的人影悄然穿过阴影。 ② The athlete waited in a crouching start position. 运动员以蹲踞式起跑姿势等候。
[族] crouch 蹲伏
[搭] crouching position 蹲伏姿势, crouching start 蹲踞式起跑, crouching figure 蹲伏的人影, remain crouching 保持蹲伏
[联] kneeling 跪着的, squatting 蹲坐的, hunched 弓背的, hidden 隐藏的, poised 蓄势的, standing 站着的, low 低的, alert 警觉的
[记] crouching start 田径术语。Crouching Tiger 电影名「卧虎藏龙」。crouching vs. squatting：crouching 更绑「伏低、蓄势」；squatting 偏健身蹲姿

**reclining**
英/美 /rɪˈklaɪnɪŋ/
adj. 斜躺的；可后仰的
[义] Lying back in a relaxed position; designed to lean backwards. 人斜靠、半躺（reclining figure 斜躺人像, reclining on a couch 斜靠在沙发上）；座椅可后仰（reclining seat 可躺座椅）。recline 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① Passengers in the reclining seats were asked to return them upright for landing. 请可躺座椅上的乘客在降落前调直。 ② A reclining statue filled one corner of the gallery. 一座斜躺雕像占满画廊一角。
[族] recline 斜靠, recliner 躺椅
[搭] reclining seat/chair 可躺座椅/躺椅, reclining figure 斜躺人像, reclining position 斜躺姿势, fully reclining 完全后仰
[联] lying 躺着的, seated 坐着的, upright 竖直的, relaxed 放松的, lounging 懒散的, stiff 僵硬的, mobile 可移动的, comfortable 舒适的
[记] reclining seat 航空、高铁高频。reclining vs. lying：reclining 多「斜靠、半躺」；lying 可完全平躺

**squatting**
英/美 /ˈskwɑːtɪŋ/ 英 /ˈskwɒtɪŋ/
adj. 蹲坐的；擅自占用的
[义] In a position with knees bent and body lowered; (legal) occupying property without permission. 健身或休息蹲姿（squatting position 深蹲姿势）；法律上「擅自占用」（squatting tenants 擅自占房者）。squat 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① She held the squatting position for thirty seconds before standing. 她保持蹲姿三十秒后站起。 ② Squatting residents were given notice to leave the empty building. 擅自占住空楼的人收到搬离通知。
[族] squat 蹲坐, squatter 擅自占用者
[搭] squatting position 蹲姿, deep squatting 深蹲, squatting tenants 擅自占房者, remain squatting 保持蹲姿
[联] crouching 蹲伏的, kneeling 跪着的, standing 站着的, low 低的, illegal 非法的, occupied 被占用的, vacant 空着的, balanced 平衡的
[记] squatting position 健身极频。squatting 法律义指「擅自占房」。squatting vs. crouching：squatting 强调「蹲坐、占房」

**hunched**
英/美 /hʌntʃt/
adj. 弓背的；蜷缩的
[义] With shoulders raised and back bent forward. 肩耸、背弯（hunched shoulders 耸肩, hunched over a desk 伏案弓背）。hunch 的过去分词作形容词；常带疲惫或专注色彩。
[例] ① He sat hunched over his laptop until midnight. 他弓背对着笔记本电脑坐到午夜。 ② Hunched shoulders can signal stress or cold. 耸肩可能表示紧张或寒冷。
[族] hunch 弓背, hunchback 驼背（旧称）
[搭] hunched shoulders 耸肩, hunched over 伏在……上弓背, remain hunched 保持弓背, slightly hunched 微微弓背
[联] slumped 瘫坐的, stooped 弯腰的, upright 竖直的, tense 紧张的, relaxed 放松的, bent 弯曲的, cramped 拥挤的
[记] hunched over 极频，指伏案、看手机。hunched vs. slumped：hunched 肩背前弓；slumped 整体瘫软下垂

**slumped**
英/美 /slʌmpt/
adj. 瘫坐的；陡降的
[义] Sitting or standing with the body hanging loosely, often from tiredness; (of prices) fallen sharply. 人瘫坐、垮坐（slumped in a chair 瘫在椅里, slumped shoulders 耷拉的双肩）；价格等骤降（slumped sales 销量大跌）。slump 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① She was slumped against the wall, too tired to speak. 她靠墙瘫坐着，累得说不出话。 ② Slumped demand forced the factory to cut shifts. 需求骤降迫使工厂减产轮班。
[族] slump 骤降, slumping 下滑的
[搭] slumped in a chair 瘫在椅里, slumped shoulders 耷拉双肩, slumped sales/demand 销量/需求大跌, remain slumped 仍瘫坐着
[联] hunched 弓背的, exhausted 精疲力竭的, upright 竖直的, alert 警觉的, drooping 下垂的, sagging 下坠的, rising 上升的, energetic 精力充沛的
[记] slumped in a chair 口语极频。slumped vs. hunched：slumped 强调整体垮掉；hunched 强调肩背前弓

**sprawled**
英/美 /sprɔːld/
adj. 四肢摊开躺/坐的；蔓延的
[义] With arms and legs spread out in an uncontrolled way; spread over a large area. 人四肢摊开（sprawled on the grass 摊在草地上, sprawled across the sofa 摊在沙发上）；城市、建筑蔓延（sprawled suburbs 蔓延的郊区）。sprawl 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The children were sprawled on the rug, reading comics. 孩子们摊在地毯上看漫画。 ② Sprawled housing estates ringed the old town. 蔓延的住宅带环绕老城。
[族] sprawl 蔓延, sprawling 蔓延的
[搭] sprawled on/across 摊在……上, sprawled suburbs 蔓延郊区, sprawled out 四肢摊开, urban sprawled 城市蔓延
[联] lying 躺着的, seated 坐着的, compact 紧凑的, cramped 拥挤的, relaxed 放松的, messy 凌乱的, dense 密集的, scattered 分散的
[记] sprawled out 口语「摊成大字」。sprawled suburbs 城市规划高频。sprawled vs. lying：sprawled 强调「四肢摊开、占大面积」

**dangling**
英/美 /ˈdæŋɡlɪŋ/
adj. 悬垂的；晃荡的
[义] Hanging loosely and swinging slightly. 末端悬空、可晃（dangling wires 垂下的电线, dangling legs 悬空的腿, dangling modifier 悬垂修饰语）。dangle 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① Dangling cables blocked the corridor after the ceiling panel fell. 吊顶板掉落后，垂下的电缆挡了走廊。 ② She sat on the high stool with dangling legs. 她坐在高凳上，双腿悬空晃荡。
[族] dangle 悬垂, dangler 悬挂物
[搭] dangling wires/modifier 垂线/悬垂修饰语, dangling legs 悬空的腿, dangling from 从……垂下, remain dangling 仍悬垂
[联] hanging 悬挂的, suspended 悬吊的, loose 松的, taut 拉紧的, fixed 固定的, swinging 摆动的, attached 附着的, severed 断开的
[记] dangling modifier 语法术语「悬垂修饰语」。dangling legs 儿童坐高凳极频。dangling vs. hanging：dangling 强调「晃荡、未固定末端」

**hanging**
英/美 /ˈhæŋɪŋ/
adj. 悬挂的；悬而未决的
[义] Attached at the top so the lower part is free; (formal) not yet decided. 上端固定、下端悬空（hanging basket 吊篮, hanging gardens 空中花园）；正式语体「悬而未决」（a hanging question 悬而未决的问题）。hang 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① Hanging plants softened the bare balcony railing. 悬挂的绿植柔化了光秃的阳台栏杆。 ② The case remains hanging while the court reviews new evidence. 法院审查新证据期间，案件仍悬而未决。
[族] hang 悬挂, hanger 衣架
[搭] hanging basket/gardens 吊篮/空中花园, hanging question 悬而未决的问题, hanging wire 悬挂的电线, remain hanging 仍悬而未决
[联] dangling 悬垂的, suspended 悬吊的, mounted 安装的, fixed 固定的, pending 待定的, settled 已定的, attached 附着的, loose 松的
[记] hanging gardens 指「空中/悬园」。hanging question 正式语体。hanging vs. pending：hanging 可指物理悬挂或「悬而未决」；pending 专指「待处理」

**leaning**
英/美 /ˈliːnɪŋ/
adj. 倾斜的；倚靠的
[义] Resting against something for support; at an angle from vertical. 倚靠支撑（leaning against the wall 靠墙, leaning on a cane 拄拐）；整体倾斜（leaning tower 斜塔, leaning forward 前倾）。lean 的现在分词作形容词；与 leaning「倾向、爱好」名词同形。
[例] ① A bicycle was leaning against the fence outside the café. 咖啡馆外一辆自行车靠在栅栏上。 ② She sat leaning forward, eager to catch every word. 她前倾着坐，生怕漏听一个字。
[族] lean 倚靠, lean 倾斜
[搭] leaning against/on 靠在/倚在, leaning tower 斜塔, leaning forward 前倾, remain leaning 保持倚靠
[联] tilted 倾斜的, upright 竖直的, slanted 倾斜的, balanced 平衡的, supported 有支撑的, free-standing 独立站立的, inclined 倾向/倾斜的, straight 直的
[记] leaning against the wall 极频。leaning forward 表「前倾、专注」。leaning vs. tilted：leaning 常含「倚靠」；tilted 强调「角度偏斜」

**tilted**
英/美 /ˈtɪltɪd/
adj. 倾斜的；翘起的
[义] At an angle; not level or upright. 物体一端抬高或整体歪斜（tilted head 歪头, tilted screen 倾斜的屏幕, tilted floor 不平的地面）。tilt 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① She gave him a tilted smile and walked away. 她歪头一笑，转身走了。 ② The table wobbled because one leg was tilted. 桌子晃，因为一条腿歪了。
[族] tilt 倾斜, tilting 倾斜的
[搭] tilted head/screen 歪头/斜屏, tilted at an angle 成角度倾斜, slightly tilted 微微倾斜, remain tilted 保持倾斜
[联] leaning 倚靠的, inclined 倾斜的, upright 竖直的, level 水平的, crooked 歪的, straight 直的, balanced 平衡的, slanted 倾斜的
[记] tilted head 拍照、表情常用。tilted screen 驾驶、办公场景。tilted vs. inclined：tilted 偏「物理翘/歪」；inclined 还可表「倾向、斜坡」

**inclined**
英/美 /ɪnˈklaɪnd/
adj. 倾斜的；倾向于……的
[义] At a slope; having a tendency or willingness. 物理倾斜（inclined plane 斜面, inclined surface 斜面）；抽象「倾向于」（inclined to agree 倾向于同意, morally inclined 道德取向）。incline 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The ramp provides an inclined path for wheelchair users. 坡道为轮椅使用者提供斜面通道。 ② I am inclined to think we should wait for more data. 我倾向于认为应等更多数据。
[族] incline 倾斜, inclination 倾向
[搭] inclined plane/surface 斜面, inclined to 倾向于, steeply inclined 陡斜的, feel inclined 觉得倾向
[联] tilted 倾斜的, leaning 倚靠的, willing 愿意的, reluctant 不情愿的, flat 平的, steep 陡的, prone 易于……的, horizontal 水平的
[记] inclined to do 极频。inclined plane 物理术语。inclined vs. prone：inclined to 表「倾向、意愿」；prone to 表「易于遭受」

**outstretched**
英/美 /ˌaʊtˈstretʃt/
adj. 伸展开的；张开的
[义] Extended to full length. 手臂、双手、翅膀等张开伸展（outstretched arms 张开的双臂, outstretched hand 伸出的手, outstretched wings 展开的翅膀）。outstretch 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① She ran toward him with outstretched arms. 她张开双臂向他跑去。 ② An outstretched hand asked for help from the crowd. 一只伸出的手向人群求助。
[族] outstretch 伸展, stretch 伸展
[搭] outstretched arms/hand 张开的双臂/伸出的手, outstretched wings 展开的翅膀, with outstretched …… 张开……
[联] extended 延伸的, reaching 伸出的, folded 折叠的, cramped 拥挤的, welcoming 欢迎的, open 敞开的, closed 闭合的, sprawled 摊开的
[记] with outstretched arms 欢迎、拥抱极频。outstretched vs. extended：近义；outstretched 更绑「向人/外张开」

**crowded**
英/美 /ˈkraʊdɪd/
adj. 拥挤的；挤满的
[义] Full of people or things; packed closely together. 空间、时间、内容挤满（crowded room 拥挤的房间, crowded schedule 排满的日程, crowded market 拥挤的市场）。crowd 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The crowded train left little space to move. 拥挤的列车几乎无处挪步。 ② She kept a crowded calendar during the conference week. 会议周她的日程排得满满当当。
[族] crowd 人群, overcrowded 过度拥挤的
[搭] crowded room/street 拥挤的房间/街道, crowded schedule 排满的日程, crowded with 挤满, feel crowded 感到拥挤
[联] packed 塞满的, sparse 稀疏的, empty 空的, busy 繁忙的, vacant 空着的, spacious 宽敞的, dense 密集的, scattered 分散的
[记] crowded with 后可接人/物。crowded schedule 职场极频。crowded vs. packed：近义；packed 更强调「塞满、无空隙」

**localized**
英/美 /ˈloʊkəlaɪzd/ 英 /ˈləʊkəlaɪz/
adj. 局部的；局限的
[义] Limited to a particular area or part. 问题、疼痛、冲突等局限在一处（localized pain 局部疼痛, localized outbreak 局部爆发, localized service 本地化服务）。localize 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① The doctor found localized swelling behind the knee. 医生发现膝后有局部肿胀。 ② A localized power failure affected only two streets. 局部停电只影响两条街。
[族] localize 使局部化, local 局部的, localization 本地化
[搭] localized pain/outbreak 局部疼痛/爆发, localized service 本地化服务, remain localized 仍局限在局部, highly localized 高度局限
[联] widespread 广泛的, scattered 分散的, concentrated 集中的, regional 区域的, global 全球的, limited 有限的, diffuse 弥漫的, specific 特定的
[记] localized pain 医疗极频。localized vs. widespread：一对反义。localized outbreak 疫情、冲突报道常用

**widespread**
英/美 /ˈwaɪdspred/
adj. 广泛的；普遍的
[义] Existing or happening over a large area or among many people. 现象、问题、信念分布广（widespread support 广泛支持, widespread damage 大面积破坏, widespread belief 普遍看法）。复合形容词，无动词过去分词来源。
[例] ① There is widespread concern about rising housing costs. 人们对房价上涨普遍担忧。 ② Widespread flooding cut off several villages. 大面积洪水使数个村庄与外界隔绝。
[族] wide 宽的, spread 扩散
[搭] widespread support/concern 广泛支持/担忧, widespread damage 大面积破坏, widespread use 广泛使用, become widespread 变得普遍
[联] localized 局部的, common 常见的, rare 罕见的, extensive 广泛的, limited 有限的, universal 普遍的, scattered 分散的, concentrated 集中的
[记] widespread concern/use 新闻极频。widespread vs. common：widespread 强调「地理或人群范围广」；common 强调「常见、不罕见」

**concentrated**
英/美 /ˈkɑːnsntreɪtɪd/ 英 /ˈkɒnsntreɪtɪd/
adj. 集中的；浓缩的
[义] Gathered in one place; stronger because water or other substance has been removed. 人或物集中（concentrated effort 全力以赴, concentrated population 人口集中）；液体浓缩（concentrated juice 浓缩果汁）。concentrate 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Concentrated effort in the final week saved the project. 最后一周的全力冲刺救了项目。 ② Add water to the concentrated solution before use. 使用前请往浓缩液里加水。
[族] concentrate 集中, concentration 浓度
[搭] concentrated effort/attack 全力冲刺/集中攻击, concentrated juice/solution 浓缩果汁/溶液, highly concentrated 高度集中/浓缩, remain concentrated 仍集中
[联] dispersed 分散的, scattered 分散的, diluted 稀释的, focused 专注的, diffuse 弥漫的, sparse 稀疏的, intense 强烈的, widespread 广泛的
[记] concentrated effort 职场极频。concentrated juice 食品标签常见。concentrated vs. focused：concentrated 可指「物理集中或浓缩」；focused 偏「注意力集中」

**dispersed**
英/美 /dɪˈspɜːrst/ 英 /dɪˈspɜːst/
adj. 分散的；散布的
[义] Spread over a wide area; no longer gathered together. 人群、资源、颗粒等散开（dispersed crowd 散开的人群, dispersed settlement 分散聚落, dispersed particles 分散颗粒）。disperse 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Dispersed teams met online because offices were closed. 办公室关闭后，分散的团队在线开会。 ② Light passed through dispersed droplets in the mist. 光穿过雾中分散的水滴。
[族] disperse 分散, dispersion 分散
[搭] dispersed crowd/teams 散开的人群/分散团队, dispersed settlement 分散聚落, widely dispersed 广泛散布, remain dispersed 仍分散
[联] concentrated 集中的, scattered 分散的, gathered 聚集的, clustered 成簇的, localized 局部的, widespread 广泛的, compact 紧凑的, unified 统一的
[记] dispersed teams 远程办公高频。dispersed vs. scattered：近义；dispersed 更正式、可指「有意散开或地理分散」

**stooped**
英/美 /stuːpt/
adj. 弯腰的；驼背的
[义] With the head and shoulders bent forward, often from age or habit. 因年龄、习惯或看近处而弯腰（stooped figure 驼背老人, stooped over a book 俯身看书, stooped shoulders 佝偻的肩）。stoop 的过去分词作形容词；与 hunched 近义，stooped 更绑「整体前倾、驼背感」。
[例] ① A stooped man shuffled slowly along the pavement. 一位驼背老人沿人行道缓步前行。 ② She remained stooped over the microscope for hours. 她俯在显微镜前看了好几个小时。
[族] stoop 弯腰, stooping 弯腰的
[搭] stooped figure/shoulders 驼背的人/佝偻的肩, stooped over 俯在……上, slightly stooped 微微弯腰, remain stooped 保持弯腰
[联] hunched 弓背的, upright 竖直的, bent 弯曲的, slumped 瘫坐的, elderly 年老的, straight 直的, cramped 拥挤的, attentive 专注的
[记] stooped shoulders 描写老人极频。stooped vs. hunched：stooped 偏「整体弯、驼背」；hunched 偏「耸肩弓背」

**cross-legged**
英/美 /ˌkrɔːs ˈleɡd/ 英 /ˌkrɒs ˈleɡd/
adj. 盘腿坐的
[义] Sitting with one leg crossed over the other. 双腿交叉盘坐（sit cross-legged 盘腿坐, cross-legged on the floor 在地板上盘腿）。复合形容词，无单独动词过去分词。
[例] ① Children sat cross-legged on the rug for story time. 孩子们盘腿坐在地毯上听故事。 ② He waited cross-legged in the shade of a tree. 他在树荫下盘腿等候。
[搭] sit cross-legged 盘腿坐, cross-legged on the floor 在地板上盘腿, cross-legged position 盘腿姿势
[联] seated 坐着的, kneeling 跪着的, squatting 蹲坐的, upright 竖直的, relaxed 放松的, cramped 拥挤的, formal 正式的, sprawled 摊开的
[记] sit cross-legged 瑜伽、课堂、地毯场景极频。cross-legged vs. squatting：cross-legged 腿交叉；squatting 膝弯蹲低

**lounging**
英/美 /ˈlaʊndʒɪŋ/
adj. 懒散的；斜躺的
[义] Sitting or lying in a relaxed, lazy way. 懒散地靠、躺（lounging on a sofa 瘫在沙发上, lounging by the pool 在池边闲躺）。lounge 的现在分词作形容词。
[例] ① He spent the afternoon lounging in a deck chair. 他整个下午都懒洋洋地躺在躺椅里。 ② Lounging students blocked the corridor between classes. 几个懒散的学生堵在课间走廊里。
[族] lounge 闲逛, lounger 躺椅
[搭] lounging on/in 懒散地躺在……上, lounging by the pool 在池边闲躺, lounging around 四处闲逛, remain lounging 仍懒散地躺着
[联] reclining 斜躺的, sprawled 摊开的, seated 坐着的, upright 竖直的, relaxed 放松的, busy 繁忙的, active 活跃的, stiff 僵硬的
[记] lounging around 口语「到处晃、无所事事」。lounging vs. reclining：lounging 带「懒散」；reclining 可较中性

**prostrate**
英/美 /ˈprɑːstreɪt/ 英 /prɒsˈtreɪt/
adj. 俯卧的；拜倒的；衰竭的
[义] Lying flat with the face down; completely exhausted or overwhelmed. 脸朝下平趴（prostrate figure 伏地的人）；宗教、礼仪「拜倒」（fall prostrate 伏地叩拜）；喻「彻底垮掉」（prostrate with grief 悲痛欲绝）。与 prone 近义，prostrate 更正式、可带仪式或极端衰竭色彩。
[例] ① Worshippers fell prostrate before the altar. 信众在祭坛前伏地叩拜。 ② She was prostrate with fever and could not leave bed. 她烧得虚脱，起不了床。
[族] prostration 衰竭, prostate 前列腺（拼写勿混）
[搭] fall prostrate 伏地拜倒, prostrate with grief/fever 因悲痛/发烧而垮掉, prostrate figure 伏地的人
[联] prone 俯卧的, supine 仰卧的, lying 躺着的, exhausted 精疲力竭的, upright 竖直的, kneeling 跪着的, collapsed 倒下的, reverent 恭敬的
[记] fall prostrate 宗教、正式语体。prostrate with 后接 grief/fever/exhaustion。prostrate vs. prone：prone 在方位章；prostrate 更绑「拜倒、虚脱」

**buried**
英/美 /ˈberid/
adj. 被埋的；被覆盖的；埋头于……的
[义] Placed under earth or another covering; hidden beneath something; deeply involved in. 被土、雪、 debris 掩埋（buried treasure 埋藏的财宝, buried under snow 被雪埋）；喻「被淹没」（buried in work 埋头工作, buried in emails 被邮件淹没）。bury 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Rescue teams searched for people buried under the rubble. 救援队搜寻被埋在废墟下的人。 ② She was buried in paperwork until midnight. 她埋头处理文书直到午夜。
[族] bury 埋葬, burial 葬礼
[搭] buried treasure 埋藏的财宝, buried under/in 被……埋/淹没, buried alive 活埋, remain buried 仍被掩埋
[联] hidden 隐藏的, exposed 暴露的, submerged 淹没的, trapped 困住的, free 自由的, overwhelmed 应接不暇的, excavated 挖掘出的, covered 被覆盖的
[记] buried in work/emails 职场极频。buried under 后可接 snow/rubble/files。buried vs. hidden：buried 强调「被盖住、难见」

**floating**
英/美 /ˈfloʊtɪŋ/
adj. 漂浮的；浮动的；不固定的
[义] Resting on or moving through liquid or air without fixed support; not fixed in one place. 物理漂浮（floating debris 漂浮碎片, floating ice 浮冰）；不固定（floating population 流动人口, floating exchange rate 浮动汇率）。float 的现在分词作形容词；方位章 **afloat** 偏「在水面不沉」，本章收 broader 状态义。
[例] ① Floating plastic gathered near the river mouth. 漂浮塑料聚集在河口附近。 ② The team relies on a floating schedule that changes weekly. 团队靠每周变动的弹性日程运转。
[族] float 漂浮, afloat 不沉的
[搭] floating debris/ice 漂浮碎片/浮冰, floating population/rate 流动人口/浮动汇率, floating platform 浮动平台, remain floating 仍漂浮
[联] afloat 不沉的, submerged 淹没的, stationary 静止的, mobile 可移动的, fixed 固定的, drifting 漂流的, anchored 锚定的, sunken 沉没的
[记] floating population/rate 新闻、经济高频。floating vs. afloat：afloat 多指「不沉」；floating 可指「漂动、不固定」

**overturned**
英/美 /ˌoʊvərˈtɜːrnd/ 英 /ˌəʊvəˈtɜːnd/
adj. 翻倒的；倾覆的
[义] Turned upside down or onto its side. 车辆、船只、桌子等倾覆（overturned car 翻覆的车, overturned table 打翻的桌子）；喻「被推翻的」（overturned decision 被推翻的裁定）。overturn 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① An overturned truck blocked both lanes of the highway. 一辆翻覆的卡车堵住了公路两条车道。 ② The court restored an overturned verdict on appeal. 上诉法院恢复了先前被推翻的裁决。
[族] overturn 推翻, turnover 营业额
[搭] overturned car/truck 翻覆的车/卡车, overturned table 打翻的桌子, overturned verdict 被推翻的裁决, remain overturned 仍倾覆
[联] toppled 倒塌的, upended 倒立的, upright 竖直的, stable 稳定的, crashed 撞毁的, intact 完好的, broken 破损的, horizontal 水平的
[记] overturned car/truck 新闻极频。overturned verdict 法律报道常用。overturned vs. toppled：overturned 可指侧翻或打翻；toppled 偏「倒下、坍塌」

**toppled**
英/美 /ˈtɑːpl/
adj. 倒塌的；被推翻的
[义] Fallen over; (of a government or leader) removed from power. 物体倒下（toppled statue 倒下的雕像, toppled wall 倒塌的墙）；政权被推翻（toppled regime 被推翻的政权）。topple 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A toppled tree crushed the garden fence. 一棵倒下的树压垮了花园栅栏。 ② Photos showed toppled shelves after the earthquake. 照片显示地震后货架倒塌。
[族] topple 倒塌
[搭] toppled statue/tree 倒下的雕像/树, toppled regime 被推翻的政权, toppled shelves 倒塌的货架, remain toppled 仍倒伏
[联] overturned 翻倒的, fallen 倒下的, upright 竖直的, stable 稳定的, collapsed 坍塌的, intact 完好的, standing 站立的, damaged 损坏的
[记] toppled regime 政治新闻常用。toppled vs. collapsed：toppled 强调「失去平衡倒下」；collapsed 可指「向内塌、崩溃」

**upended**
英/美 /ʌpˈendɪd/
adj. 倒立的；颠覆的
[义] Turned so the top is at the bottom; (figurative) completely disrupted. 物体倒过来（upended box 倒扣的箱子, upended bucket 翻扣的水桶）；喻「生活、计划被彻底打乱」（upended routines 被打乱的日常）。upend 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Tools spilled from an upended toolbox on the floor. 倒扣的工具箱把工具洒了一地。 ② Travel restrictions upended holiday plans for millions. 旅行限制打乱了数百万人的假期计划。
[族] upend 倒转
[搭] upended box/bucket 倒扣的箱/桶, upended routines/plans 被打乱的日常/计划, remain upended 仍倒扣
[联] overturned 翻倒的, inverted 倒置的, upright 竖直的, stable 稳定的, disrupted 被打乱的, normal 正常的, spilled 洒出的, balanced 平衡的
[记] upended routines/plans 疫情、政策报道极频。upended vs. inverted：upended 口语感更强；inverted 更技术、正式

**sunken**
英/美 /ˈsʌŋkən/
adj. 沉没的；凹陷的
[义] That has gone below the surface of water; curved inward below the normal level. 船只等沉没（sunken ship 沉船）；眼窝、脸颊凹陷（sunken eyes 凹陷的双眼, sunken cheeks 凹陷的脸颊）。sink 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Divers mapped a sunken wreck off the coast. 潜水员测绘了近海一处沉船。 ② Illness left him with sunken cheeks and little energy. 一场病后他脸颊凹陷、精力不济。
[族] sink 下沉, sunk 沉没的
[搭] sunken ship/wreck 沉船, sunken eyes/cheeks 凹陷的双眼/脸颊, sunken garden 下沉式花园, remain sunken 仍沉没/凹陷
[联] submerged 淹没的, floating 漂浮的, afloat 不沉的, hollow 空心的, swollen 肿胀的, intact 完好的, raised 凸起的, drowned 溺水的
[记] sunken eyes/cheeks 描写病容、疲惫极频。sunken garden 建筑术语「下沉式花园」。sunken vs. submerged：sunken 可指「已沉、凹陷」；submerged 强调「在水下被淹」

**beached**
英/美 /biːtʃt/
adj. 搁浅的；冲上岸的
[义] Stranded on a beach or shore; (informal) resting without working. 鲸、船等搁浅（beached whale 搁浅的鲸, beached boat 搁浅的船）；口语「闲着」（beached executive 被架空的高管）。beach 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Volunteers tried to refloat a beached dolphin at dawn. 志愿者黎明时分试图让搁浅的海豚重回水里。 ② A beached fishing boat lay tilted on the sand. 一艘搁浅的渔船歪在沙滩上。
[族] beach 海滩, beaching 搁浅
[搭] beached whale/boat 搁浅的鲸/船, beached executive 被架空的高管, remain beached 仍搁浅
[联] stranded 滞留的, marooned 被困的, afloat 不沉的, floating 漂浮的, ashore 在岸上的, adrift 漂流的, anchored 锚定的, rescued 获救的
[记] beached whale 环保新闻极频。beached executive 商业口语「被架空、无实权」。beached vs. stranded：beached 专指「在滩/岸」

**unavailable**
英/美 /ˌʌnəˈveɪləbl/
adj. 不可用的；没空的；买不到的
[义] Not able to be used, obtained, or reached; not free to talk or meet. 资源、商品不可得（unavailable seats 无票座位, temporarily unavailable 暂时不可用）；人没空（The director is unavailable 主管没空）。与 available 相对。
[例] ① The product is currently unavailable in this region. 该产品目前在本地区缺货。 ② She was unavailable for comment when reporters called. 记者致电时她不便发表评论。
[族] available 可用的, availability 可用性, unavailability 不可用
[搭] temporarily unavailable 暂时不可用, unavailable for comment 不便评论, currently unavailable 目前无货, make unavailable 使不可用
[联] available 可用的, busy 繁忙的, accessible 可及的, out of stock 缺货的, occupied 被占用的, free 有空的, restricted 受限制的, engaged 占线的
[记] unavailable for comment 新闻极频。temporarily unavailable 系统、商品页面常见。unavailable vs. busy：unavailable 更正式、可指物；busy 偏人忙

**unprepared**
英/美 /ˌʌnprɪˈperd/
adj. 未准备的；无准备的
[义] Not ready for what is needed or expected. 人、机构、材料未备好（unprepared students 没准备的学生, caught unprepared 措手不及, unprepared speech 即席发言）。与 prepared 相对。
[例] ① Many households were unprepared for the sudden blackout. 许多家庭对突然停电毫无准备。 ② Unprepared remarks sometimes reveal more than scripted answers. 即席发言有时比照稿回答更露真情。
[族] prepare 准备, prepared 有准备的, preparation 准备
[搭] caught unprepared 措手不及, unprepared for 对……无准备, unprepared speech/remarks 即席发言, remain unprepared 仍未准备
[联] prepared 有准备的, ready 就绪的, surprised 意外的, equipped 装备齐全的, alert 警觉的, rehearsed 排练过的, vulnerable 脆弱的, confident 自信的
[记] caught unprepared 极频。unprepared for 后接 disaster/exam/news。unprepared vs. unready：近义；unprepared 更绑「没做功课、没预案」

**enclosed**
英/美 /ɪnˈkloʊzd/ 英 /ɪnˈkləʊzd/
adj. 封闭的；随信附上的
[义] Surrounded on all sides; sent in an envelope or attachment. 空间被围起（enclosed garden 封闭式花园, enclosed space 密闭空间）；信件「随函附上」（please find enclosed 随信附上）。enclose 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Workers must wear masks in enclosed spaces with poor ventilation. 通风差的密闭空间内工人必须戴口罩。 ② Please find enclosed a copy of the signed contract. 随函附上已签合同一份。
[族] enclose 围住, enclosure 围场
[搭] enclosed space/garden 密闭空间/封闭式花园, please find enclosed 随信附上, enclosed area 封闭区域, fully enclosed 全封闭
[联] sealed 密封的, open 敞开的, exposed 暴露的, ventilated 通风的, attached 附上的, surrounding 周围的, cramped 拥挤的, spacious 宽敞的
[记] please find enclosed 商务邮件极频。enclosed space 疫情、安全规范常用。enclosed vs. sealed：enclosed 指「围起来」；sealed 指「封死、密封」

**overcrowded**
英/美 /ˌoʊvərˈkraʊdɪd/ 英 /ˌəʊvəˈkraʊdɪd/
adj. 过度拥挤的
[义] With too many people or things for the space available. 比 crowded 程度更深（overcrowded prison 人满为患的监狱, overcrowded classroom 超员教室）。over- + crowded。
[例] ① Overcrowded buses made the commute unsafe during rush hour. 高峰时段过度拥挤的公交让通勤不安全。 ② Overcrowded hospitals struggled to isolate new patients. 过度拥挤的医院难以隔离新患者。
[族] crowd 人群, crowded 拥挤的
[搭] overcrowded prison/bus/hospital 过度拥挤的监狱/公交/医院, overcrowded classroom 超员教室, become overcrowded 变得过度拥挤
[联] crowded 拥挤的, packed 塞满的, spacious 宽敞的, sparse 稀疏的, unsafe 不安全的, vacant 空着的, dense 密集的, comfortable 舒适的
[记] overcrowded prison/hospital 社会新闻极频。overcrowded vs. crowded：overcrowded 强调「超出承受限度」

**overloaded**
英/美 /ˌoʊvərˈloʊdɪd/ 英 /ˌəʊvəˈləʊdɪd/
adj. 超载的；负荷过重的
[义] Carrying too much weight or too many tasks. 车辆、电路、系统超载（overloaded truck 超载货车, overloaded circuit 过载电路）；人任务过重（overloaded staff 超负荷的员工）。overload 的过去分词作形容词；与 loaded 相对。
[例] ① An overloaded van lost a wheel on the steep hill. 一辆超载的面包车在陡坡上掉了一个轮子。 ② Overloaded servers caused the website to crash. 服务器过载导致网站崩溃。
[族] overload 超载, loaded 装载的
[搭] overloaded truck/circuit 超载货车/过载电路, overloaded staff/system 超负荷员工/系统, become overloaded 变得过载
[联] loaded 装载的, unloaded 未装载的, exhausted 精疲力竭的, busy 繁忙的, balanced 平衡的, strained 吃紧的, efficient 高效的, broken 损坏的
[记] overloaded circuit/server IT 极频。overloaded staff 职场常用。overloaded vs. busy：overloaded 强调「超出承载上限」

**underused**
英/美 /ˌʌndərˈjuːzd/
adj. 未充分利用的；使用不足的
[义] Not used as much as it could or should be. 资源、设施、人才闲置（underused capacity 闲置产能, underused skills 未发挥的技能, underused room 利用率低的房间）。under- + used。
[例] ① The report found underused lab space on the top floor. 报告发现顶层实验室空间利用不足。 ② Her language skills were underused in the previous role. 她先前的岗位未能充分发挥其语言能力。
[族] use 使用, overused 过度使用的
[搭] underused capacity/skills 闲置产能/未发挥技能, underused asset/room 利用率低的资产/房间, remain underused 仍利用不足
[联] idle 闲置的, overused 过度使用的, busy 繁忙的, wasted 浪费的, efficient 高效的, vacant 空着的, productive 高产的, neglected 被忽视的
[记] underused capacity 经济、运营报告常用。underused vs. idle：underused 指「能用但没用足」；idle 指「闲着、空转」

**waterlogged**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtərlɔːɡd/ 英 /ˈwɔːtəlɒɡd/
adj. 浸水的；水涝的
[义] Full of or damaged by water; (of soil) saturated with water. 物体吸饱水（waterlogged shoes 浸水的鞋, waterlogged phone 进水的手机）；田地水涝（waterlogged fields 涝田）。waterlog 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① Waterlogged fields delayed planting for two weeks. 涝田使播种推迟了两周。 ② She threw away the waterlogged books from the flooded basement. 她把地下室淹过后浸水的书扔掉了。
[族] waterlog 浸水
[搭] waterlogged fields/shoes 涝田/浸水的鞋, waterlogged soil 积水土壤, become waterlogged 变得浸水
[联] soaked 湿透的, flooded 被淹的, dry 干燥的, saturated 饱和的, submerged 淹没的, damp 潮湿的, damaged 损坏的, drained 排干的
[记] waterlogged fields 农业、气象报道极频。waterlogged vs. soaked：waterlogged 偏「泡透、涝、难排」；soaked 偏「湿透」
